Motivation: 工具增强的 LLM 智能体在 API、数据库、多步工作流中扮演自主控制器，失败往往发生在"执行过程"中（schema 漂移、延迟、部分失败），并沿动作链级联，而非像传统对抗/分布鲁棒性那样表现为输入上的静态扰动。实践上智能体常能自我纠错，但缺乏形式化理论来预测"何时恢复成功、何时失败、错误如何随长时程累积"。已有自纠错工作（PALADIN、ToolReflect、CRITIC、Reflexion 等）主要是描述性的，只刻画"是否恢复"，没有量化"可恢复多少"的原理；同时 Regret 界（RDP 等）与可观测的执行统计之间缺少可操作的桥接，理论遗憾与可观测行为之间无直接换算关系。作者因此要建立一条类比 scaling law 的"定律"：具有明确有效域、可被伪证的一阶规律，而非普适不变量。

Method: **形式化。** 在执行级设定下（状态 s_t、动作 a_t、随机执行噪声 δ_t ~ F(s_t,a_t)、折扣 γ、转移 T(s,a,δ)），假设：有界单步成本 c_t∈[0,c_max]；损失 ℓ 对 δ Lipschitz 连续；扰动过程时间不变（平稳）；有限折扣时程 γ∈(0,1)（实验取 0.9）。定义 π* = argmin_π L(π,F)，ERR(π)=E_F[L(π,F)−L(π*,F)]。观测代理量：Recovery Rate RR、Cost-Sensitive Recovery CSR=RR−λC_r（按纠正步数计费，仅作解读）、Efficiency Score ES=RR/(1+λC)（C 为按全局最大轨迹成本归一化的 token 成本，λ=0.5）。

**理论推导。** 附录 A 用性能差分引理（Lemma A.1，避免逐步对齐分解）把 ERR 写成 π 自身轨迹上的优势期望；Lemma A.2 做一阶线性化 E[A^{π*}] = αE[c_t(1−ρ_t)] + O(Var(ρ_t))；由有界成本得 ERR ≤ (αc_max/(1−γ))(1−ρ̄)，把常数吸收进归一化后得 ERR ≈ (1/(1−γ))(1−ES) + O(λc_max)（Prop A.4 给出上界形式）。Theorem 3.2 证明在"可接受代理类"（单调、边界归一、补与 ERR 仿射等价）下 ES 是唯一解；Corollary 3.3 说明 RR−λC 这类加性代理只能保序、无法标定量级；Lemma A.5/A.6 给出二阶曲率项 βVar(C)，并推出"规模越大→方差越小→对齐越紧"（Cor A.7）。

**实例化。** 提出 FORTIFY 作为轻量恢复机制的"探针"（非本文方法贡献）：在合成失败轨迹上训练监督恢复先验（3 epoch），推理时用余弦相似度检索 K 个失败—恢复样例做条件化（不改权重），配合迭代重发循环直至成功或预算耗尽。恢复策略族（监督纠错、自反思、批判式修订、无恢复基线）作为探针 ERR–ES 流形的"仪器"。

**实验协议。** 4 个基准：PaladinEval（受控扰动）、ToolReflectEval（诊断推理失败）、API–Bank（真实 API schema 漂移）、WikidataLive（跨域语义查询）；另在 3 个线上生产 API（金融/旅游/天气）做定性诊断（附录 F，仅 50–100 条 trace）。骨干：Qwen 2.5 14B、Gemma 3 12B、Llama 3 8B、Thinking V1 32B；每组 200 次 MC rollout、3 个随机种子、95% bootstrap CI，γ=0.9、λ=0.5，预测值一律用 β=0 的一阶式（未做任何拟合/校准）。单步损失 ℓ = I[fail_t] + λ·c_t/c_max（未解决的工具错误/schema 非法/语义错误且当步未纠正）。π* 用"评估族中平均折扣损失最低的策略" π̂* 近似，ERR_obs = L̂(π) − L̂(π̂*)。贴合度指标 Δ_pred = |ERR_obs − (1−ES)/(1−γ)|，Δ_norm = Δ_pred / 预测值。

**消融与边界。** 附录 G 固定策略/ES/γ、只抬高执行成本方差 σ²，划分线性（≤1e-2）、曲率（1e-2~1e-1）、崩溃（≥1e-1）三区；附录 H 用加性 S_add=RR−λC 与调和平均 S_hm 替换 ES；附录 I 在 λ∈[0.05,0.60]、γ∈[0.80,0.975] 的 8×8 网格做敏感性。

Result: **效率—遗憾坍缩（Table 1）。** 架构与策略差异极大的系统落在同一条一维流形上，观测遗憾随 ES 单调下降：Vanilla 14B（RR 38.2%、ES 0.312、ERR 7.17）→ ToolBench（0.504、5.02）→ ToolReflect（0.577、4.31）→ CRITIC（0.661、3.45）→ FORTIFY（0.814、1.87），该对齐非训练所得。

**机制不变性（Table 2）。** 去掉检索（ΔERR +0.89）与去掉恢复（+1.07）只是沿同一条曲线平移，不产生新regime——检索主要压执行方差，恢复加权主要改成本归一化效率。

**规模只抑制方差（Table 3）。** 8B→32B 的 ES 由 0.755 升到 0.823、预测 ERR 由 2.45 降到 1.77，几何形状不变。

**早期步主导（Table 4）。** Vanilla 在 S3 后崩塌（12.7→3.9→0.0），FORTIFY 到 S6 仍保持 59.5；早期效率增益以几何方式抑制下游级联，对应 Cor 3.5。

**定律贴合度（Tables 5–6）。** 跨模型 Δ_norm ∈ [0.005, 0.042]，均值约 0.019；跨环境（四个基准）ES≈0.813–0.816，Δ_norm≈0.016–0.017。附录 H 显示替换代理后误差膨胀：加性代理 Δ_norm 均值 0.152（误差近似常数 0.14–0.17，即"仿射于错误变量"），调和平均 0.187（低效率策略误差随效率下降而放大），三者均保序但只有 ES 能标定量级。

**边界与失效（§7、附录 G/F）。** ① 非平稳语义漂移：API 中途改 schema 使 ERR 升高而 ES 不变，系统性低估；② λ→0 或重试无界（最多 15 次、几乎零罚）时成本方差超出 Lipschitz 假设，ES 失去意义；③ 非遍历交互（缓存/记忆/自适应补丁）使 ERR 定义在固定轨迹分布上失效，长期累积发散。附录 G 证实偏差仅由方差膨胀引起并按理论预期的三区演化；附录 F 线上 API 上预测值系统性略低估，与 §7 判据一致。

**作者自陈局限。** π* 为经验前沿（相对同一参考测量的"紧致"可能部分来自共享参考，而非定律本身，尚未做更换参考策略的检验）；线性化只在聚合层面被验证，比例常数 α 被吸收进归一化、未独立估计；模型覆盖仅 8B–32B 开源权重，未验证闭源前沿模型及同一崩溃阈值。

Conclusion: 本文把"可恢复性"从经验现象提升为一阶可量化属性：ERR–ES 定律将不可观测的最优策略遗憾与可从少量离线 rollout 估计的 ES 联系起来，为预测失效级联、分配纠正预算、构造课程与安全约束提供可操作的信号；作者强调这是类 scaling law 意义上的"有效域内规律"而非普适恒等式。其结构性贡献在于把鲁棒性从静态不变性重构为交互动力学的几何（效率—遗憾流形），并明确划出其失效边界（平稳扰动、有界纠正成本、遍历动力学），指明向语义漂移、无界重试、非遍历状态演化的推广是自然的后续理论工作。需注意：摘要称"五个基准"而正文为四个（另有 3 个线上 API 仅作定性诊断），且 π* 的经验近似与一阶线性化的验证强度构成论文最主要的证据薄弱点。

Abstract: Language model agents often appear capable of self-recovery after failing tool call executions, yet this behavior lacks a formal explanation. We present a predictive theory that resolves this gap by showing that recoverability follows a measurable law. To elaborate, we formalize recoverability through Expected Recovery Regret (ERR), which quantifies the deviation of a recovery policy from the optimal one under stochastic execution noise, and derive a first-order relationship between ERR and an empirical observable quantity, the Efficiency Score (ES). This yields a falsifiable first-order quantitative law of recovery dynamics in tool-using agents. We empirically validate the law across five tool-use benchmarks spanning controlled perturbations, diagnostic reasoning, and real-world APIs. Across model scales, perturbation regimes, and recovery horizons, predicted regret under the ERR-ES law closely matched observed post-failure regret measured from Monte Carlo rollouts, within delta less than or equal to 0.05. Our results reveal that recoverability is not an artifact of model scale or architecture, but a governed property of interaction dynamics, providing a theoretical foundation for execution-level robustness in language agents.

</details>


### [45] [Relative Wasserstein Angle and the Problem of the $W_2$-Nearest Gaussian Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22355)
*Binshuai Wang,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在最优传输（OT）几何框架下，提出"相对 Wasserstein 角（RW₂ angle）"与"正交投影距离（projection distance）"两个新几何量，用于定量刻画经验分布偏离高斯族的程度。核心做法是：利用平移不变二次 Wasserstein 空间 (P₂(ℝᵈ)/~_T, RW₂) 的锥结构——所有 Dirac 分布坍缩为锥顶 [δ₀]，正比例缩放同一"形状"的分布构成一条测地射线；用余弦定理定义两条射线间的夹角 ∠([[μ]],[[ν]])=arccos((a²+b²−c²)/2ab)，并证明两条射线间的"填充锥"在至少一条射线由绝对连续分布生成时具有零截曲率、可等距嵌入二维欧氏平面，从而角度、正交投影与类内积 ⟨[μ],[ν]⟩=‖[μ‖‖[ν‖cosθ 均被严格定义。据此把"μ 距离高斯族有多远"重述为 μ 到高斯凸锥 C(N)={N(0,Σ)} 的最小正交投影问题，即 W₂-最近高斯问题。一维情形利用单调 OT 映射/分位数表示给出闭式解：投影长度 l=Σxᵢ(φ(z_{i−1})−φ(zᵢ))，θ=arccos(l/σ_μ)，p=σ_μ sinθ，并推广到 uniform、Laplace、logistic 等位置-尺度族；该统计量等价于 del Barrio et al. (1999) 的 Rₙ=p²/σ_μ²=sin²θ。高维情形将问题化为单位迹协方差优化 min_{U,Λ}RW₂²([μ],[N(0,UΛUᵀ)])，提出基于离散 OT 耦合梯度的粒子/流形优化算法（Algorithm 2），配合 PCA 降维。关键理论结论：矩匹配高斯 N(μ̄,Σ_μ) 一般不是 W₂-最近高斯（角>0 时投影长度 r cosθ<r），甚至其特征值与特征向量都可能不同（二维反例 ω★≈0.7983π vs ω_MM≈0.8340π，谱 {0.9411,0.0589} vs {0.7778,0.2222}）。实验显示 RW₂ 角比投影距离/Wasserstein 距离更稳健（CV 更低），W₂-最近高斯给出的角度始终小于矩匹配基线，可作为分布异质性的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集的分布结构难以可视化，通常借助与"参照形状"（如球、矩形）的几何比较来刻画数据质量、异质性与覆盖度。高斯族是最常用的单源参照，但传统统计量（偏度、峰度等高阶矩）只能刻画局部差异，缺乏统一几何框架；信息论方法（负熵、KL/Rényi 散度）也绑定于特定泛函。作者因此追问：从最优传输的角度，给定经验分布 μ，它距离高斯族有多远、等价地 μ 在多大程度上可视为高斯？该问题此前理论刻画有限。同时，尽管 Wasserstein 距离已用于比较分布与高斯（Bobkov & Ledoux; Gelbrich; van Oostrum 的 Bures–Wasserstein 角是本文角在高斯族上的特例），矩匹配高斯仍被默认当作最优高斯近似，而本文要证明这一默认是错的。

Method: 【预备】定义 RW_p([μ],[ν])=min_t W_p(μ+t,ν)；二次情形有分解 W₂²(μ,ν)=‖μ̄−ν̄‖²+RW₂²([μ],[ν])。所有 Dirac 分布坍缩为同一等价类 [δ₀]，作为锥顶；正比例膨胀等价 ~_S 把空间分割成互不相交的射线 [[μ]]={(s·Id)_#[μ]: s>0}，每条射线对应一个"形状"。【定义】(Def 3.1) RW₂ 角 ∠([[μ]],[[ν]])=arccos((a²+b²−c²)/2ab)，其中 a=RW₂([μ],[δ₀])、b=RW₂([ν],[δ₀])、c=RW₂([μ],[ν])，对每条射线上的正缩放不变；(Def 3.3) 投影距离 p([μ],[[ν]])=min_{[\eta]∈[[ν]]∪{[δ₀]}}RW₂([μ],[η])=‖[μ]‖_{RW₂} sin∠，并定义 ⟨[μ],[ν]⟩=‖[μ]‖‖[ν]‖cos∠。【理论】(Thm 3.2) 若两条非顶射线条中至少一条由绝对连续分布生成，则其填充锥（两射线上点间所有测地线的并）具有零截曲率，且等距嵌入二维欧氏锥区域。证明路线：Brenier 定理给出唯一最优映射 T=∇φ；McCann 位移插值给出测地线 ρ_t=(F_t)_#μ, F_t=(1−t)s₀x+t s₁T(x)；填充锥内任意点可唯一写成 ζ=(a·Id+b·T)_#μ，构成全局仿射坐标 (a,b)≥0；距离公式 W₂²(ρ_{a₁b₁},ρ_{a₂b₂})=∫‖(a₁−a₂)x+(b₁−b₂)T(x)‖²dμ=(a₁−a₂)²A+(b₁−b₂)²C+2(a₁−a₂)(b₁−b₂)B，度量系数为常二次型 ⇒ 常数系数二维黎曼流形 ⇒ 零高斯曲率。附录 G 给出三射线反例（π/3 旋转的三条线支撑高斯），说明两两平坦不可延拓为统一欧氏嵌入，故非内积空间。【一维闭式】用分位数表示 W₂²(μ,ν)=∫₀¹(F_μ⁻¹−F_ν⁻¹)²dt，代入 z=Φ(z) 换元积分得 W₂²(μ,N(0,σ²))=σ_μ²−2σl+σ²，故 θ=arccos(l/σ_μ)、p=σ_μ sinθ；复杂度 O(n log n) 时间、O(n) 内存。一般位置-尺度族的通用公式为 l_ν=(1/s_ν)∫₀¹q_μ(u)q_ν(u)du，并据此给出 uniform（l=√12 Σxᵢ(2i−n−1)/(2n²)）、logistic（l=(√3/π)Σxᵢ[H_Lg(uᵢ)−H_Lg(u_{i−1})]）、Laplace（分段反导数 H_Lpc）闭式解。【高维算法】把问题化为 min_{Σ∈Sym*_d} min_{s>0} RW₂²([μ],[N(0,s²Σ)])；因射线只依赖协方差形状，约束 tr(Σ)=1，参数化为 U∈O(d)、Λ⪰0、TrΛ=1。Algorithm 2 迭代：以离散 OT（ot.emd / Sinkhorn）求经验样本与高斯样本间的耦合，由耦合算样本梯度 g_j=Σ_i γ_ij(y_j−x_i) 做梯度下降，中心化后估计协方差、特征分解并把特征值归一化为单位和作为回缩（retraction），再重采样高斯粒子并重算角度，直至收敛；每轮复杂度 O(nm(d+K)+md²+d³)（Sinkhorn K 次迭代）。低维可用半离散 OT（Laguerre 图）更精确，高维（如 d=512）不可行；实践中配合 PCA 降维与归一化协方差以提升稳定性。【特例】附录 F：若分布在 PCA 基下坐标可分离（⊗_k μ_k），最近高斯有闭式 ν*=N(μ̄, U diag(l_k²)Uᵀ)，θ*=arccos√(Σl_k²/Σσ_k²)，p*=√(Σ(σ_k²−l_k²))。附录 E：‖[μ]‖=‖[μ_MM]‖=√Tr(Σ_μ)，非高斯时角>0 ⇒ 投影长度 r cosθ<r ⇒ 矩匹配高斯不是 W₂-最近高斯。

Result: 1) 几何框架：RW₂ 空间被刻画为以 [δ₀] 为锥顶、以正缩放射线为生成元的锥空间；两射线间填充锥在正则条件下平坦（Thm 3.2），故相对 Wasserstein 角、正交投影距离与类内积被严格定义并具有欧氏几何解释；附录 G 说明三射线情形下不存在统一欧氏等距嵌入。2) 理论否证：矩匹配高斯一般不是 W₂-最近高斯（若 μ 非高斯，θ>0，投影长度严格小于 r）；二维三原子反例中最近高斯与矩匹配高斯既不同特征向量（ω★≈0.7983π vs ω_MM≈0.8340π）也不同特征值（{0.9411,0.0589} vs {0.7778,0.2222}），能量景观图显示矩匹配方向不是局部极小点。一维 RW₂ 角与经典 Wasserstein 正态性检验统计量 Rₙ=sin²θ 单调对应，提供了新解释与高维推广。3) 一维实验：对双高斯混合（分离度 m_c∈{1,4}，N=2²~2¹⁰），θ 与 p 随 m_c 减小而减小（混合越接近高斯，非高斯性越弱），采样估计随 N 增大收敛到闭式值；角度的变异系数 CV 始终低于投影距离，说明角度比投影距离/Wasserstein 距离更稳健（原因是角度只衡量方向差异而非点差异）。4) 高维实验：Algorithm 2 在 MNIST 各数字类上角度先快速下降后稳定到类相关水平（收敛性与稳定性得到验证）。在 MNIST、EMNIST、CIFAR-10、CINIC-10（像素空间 / Inception-v3 特征+PCA 至 d=32，n=m=512，50 次迭代）上，W₂-最近高斯给出的角度一致小于矩匹配基线（如 MNIST 全量 0.9287 vs 0.9757；CIFAR-10 全量 0.9228 vs 0.9673），但耗时更高（≈2.9–3.2 s vs ≈0.03 s）。单类中角度最小者最接近单一高斯源（MNIST 数字 1：0.6675；CIFAR-10 truck：0.7987）；随混合类别增多角度单调上升（如 MNIST {0}→{0,1}→{0..3}→{0..7}：0.7694→0.7971→0.8738→0.9203），表明该角可作为分布异质性指标；跨数据集可分辨类级差异（MNIST 数字 4：0.8294 vs EMNIST 0.8523；CIFAR-10 horse 0.8159 vs CINIC-10 horse 0.8527）。摘要另报告最近高斯在 FID 评估上优于矩匹配。

Conclusion: 本文在最优传输几何下给出了偏离高斯族的一个统一、可解释的刻画：以平移不变二次 Wasserstein 空间的锥结构为基础，定义相对 Wasserstein 角与正交投影距离，并借助"两射线间填充锥平坦"的定理使这些欧氏式概念在概率分布空间中严格成立；"高斯近似"由此被重述为到高斯凸锥的正交投影问题，即 W₂-最近高斯问题。主要理论贡献是证明矩匹配高斯并非 W₂-最近高斯（且其协方差特征谱/主方向都可能不同），从而否定了常见直觉；方法上给出一维闭式解（并推广至 uniform/Laplace/logistic 等位置-尺度族，O(n log n)）与高维基于经验 OT 与协方差形状优化的随机流形算法（配合 PCA）。实验（合成混合与 MNIST/EMNIST/CIFAR-10/CINIC-10）表明：两个量均能刻画非高斯性，角度对采样波动更稳健；W₂-最近高斯是比矩匹配更好的高斯近似；角度随类别混合增多而增大，可作为分布异质性的指示量。作者同时给出伦理声明，明确该角度度量的是相对参照族（高斯族）在特定表示与协议下的偏离，不可单独用作公平性、社会代表性或数据质量度量，也不建议单独用于数据集选择或审计决策。附录 G 指出两两平坦性不保证三射线的统一欧氏几何，未来方向包括数据集刻画、分布漂移分析以及流匹配与扩散模型中的应用。

Abstract: We study the problem of quantifying how far an empirical distribution deviates from Gaussianity under the framework of optimal transport. By exploiting the cone geometry of the relative translation invariant quadratic Wasserstein space, we introduce two novel geometric quantities, the relative Wasserstein angle and the orthogonal projection distance, which provide meaningful measures of non-Gaussianity. We prove that the filling cone generated by any two rays in this space is flat, ensuring that angles, projections, and inner products are rigorously well-defined. This geometric viewpoint recasts Gaussian approximation as a projection problem onto the Gaussian cone and reveals that the commonly used moment-matching Gaussian can \emph{not} be the \(W_2\)-nearest Gaussian for a given empirical distribution. In one dimension, we derive closed-form expressions for the proposed quantities and extend them to several classical distribution families, including uniform, Laplace, and logistic distributions; while in high dimensions, we develop an efficient stochastic manifold optimization algorithm based on a semi-discrete dual formulation. Experiments on synthetic data and real-world feature distributions demonstrate that the relative Wasserstein angle is more robust than the Wasserstein distance and that the proposed nearest Gaussian provides a better approximation than moment matching in the evaluation of Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) scores.

</details>


### [46] [PoSafeNet: Safe Learning with Poset-Structured Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22356)
*Kiwan Wong,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PoSafeNet（MIT CSAIL，Kiwan Wong / Wei Xiao / Daniela Rus）提出“poset-structured safety（偏序结构化安全）”这一归纳偏置：把多个安全约束建模为偏序集（部分序），用可微的“安全层”按与偏序一致的线性扩张（linear extension）做序列闭式投影，再由多头网络学习在不同合法安全执行之间选择或混合。相比基于可微QP（ABNet/DFBNet/BarrierNet）的安全层，PoSafeNet 无需求解QP，推理与训练开销小且近线性扩展；在2D多障碍导航、两连杆受限机械臂、VISTA视觉自动驾驶三个任务上，安全性、可行性、跟踪精度与可扩展性均明显优于基线，并给出偏序一致性安全的形式化保证（硬选择无条件成立，凸混合需额外几何条件）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全学习（CBF+dQP/dMPC，如 BarrierNet、ABNet、DFBNet）通常隐含“多约束对称同时满足”或“固定全序（层次/字典序）”两个假设：前者把冲突优先级交给优化器隐式解决，易出现不可行、过度保守、数值发散；后者人为引入不存在的全局优先级。而真实机器人安全需求是异质的，只存在部分优先关系——有些约束可比（高优先级可覆盖低优先级），有些本质不可比（如左/右车道边界、多个障碍物回避），任何全序都是任意人为设定。核心问题即：如何在只有偏序、而非单一全局优先序的情况下，可扩展、上下文自适应地组合多个安全关键策略。论文认为瓶颈不在于缺乏安全证书（CBF已有），而在于缺少支配“约束如何组合”的结构性归纳偏置。

Method: 问题设定：控制仿射系统 x_dot=f(x)+g(x)u，CBF约束 b_j(x)>=0，约束集合 S 上给定设计者先验的偏序 (S, ⪯)；定义“poset-respecting safety”（定义3.1）：低优先级约束 i 仅在强制执行严格更高优先级 j (i≺j) 时才允许被违反，且“先后”指约束强制执行的排序而非物理时间。方法：(1) CBF 在控制仿射系统上诱导仿射半空间 A_j(x)u>=c_j(x)（A_j=L_g b_j, c_j=-L_f b_j-α(b_j)），单约束安全性通过闭式欧氏投影 Π_j(u)=u+ReLU(c_j-A_j u)/||A_j||²·A_j^T 实现，ReLU 保证仅在违反时做最小正交修正（KKT推导见附录A）；CBF 增益 κ_j 作为可学习参数与策略联合优化，以学习更不保守的约束紧度。(2) 序列投影 u^(k)=Π_{j_k}(u^(k-1))，按偏序的线性扩张顺序执行；由于线性扩张必先低位后高位，低位约束被覆盖只可能源于更高优先级约束（推论4.1）。(3) 多头神经结构：H 个头对应 H 个固定线性扩张，各自产生名义控制并做序列投影；训练时用凸混合（权重α）或 Gumbel-softmax 硬选择，推理时取单头或固定混合，得到确定性安全控制；梯度可穿过投影算子反传（算法1）。线性扩张数量可指数增长，实践中取少量“语义规范”扩张（碰撞回避/关节限位先于任务目标）并采样补充，H 固定且实验显示收益递减。(4) 理论保证：定理4.2/B.2给出“不可比约束不互相覆盖”的非干涉条件(NI)，其充分几何条件为 C1（构型空间中排斥方向不相对，∇_q b_i^T ∇_q b_j>=0）与 C2（控制通道各向同性 g_q g_q^T=γI，单积分器精确成立、充分驱动/反馈线性化近似成立），推出控制空间半空间法向兼容 A_i^T A_j>=0（引理B.1）；硬选择无条件安全，凸混合仅在几何条件成立时形式安全，否则只能视为经验性启发。

Result: 计算效率（图3，3个同时激活CBF约束）：PoSafeNet 推理/训练开销与普通MLP同量级、随头数近线性扩展，显著快于 ABNet 的 dQP（qpth）层。2D独轮车三障碍（反链，偏序无序）：ABNet(slack=0) QP可行率仅35%、BarrierNet 44%、DFBNet 78%；PoSafeNet 100%可行且全程安全裕度非负（Mix min 1.63、Hard min 0.00），Hard 终点距离10.95最优、单次rollout 0.276s vs ABNet 36.37s。机械臂（关节限位ϕ 优先于末端避障）：基线普遍出现关节违规/不稳定，ABNet(slack=0) 数值发散（MSE 1e116、ϕ 0.838/NaN、不可行）；PoSafeNet 两变体 ϕ 违规为0、MSE 1.04e-2（Hard）/9.08e-3（Mix）、rollout 0.095–0.173s。VISTA视觉驾驶（碰撞避让优先于车道保持）：E2E 通过率仅15%；BarrierNet（含车道约束）通过率0%、碰撞100%；DFBNet 通过100%但车道/安全裕度为负；PoSafeNet 两变体通过率100%、零碰撞、最小安全裕度2.73(Hard)/1.23(Mix)，Mix 车道越界时间0.1992优于Hard 0.3670。消融（附录D）：错误排序（车道约束高于避障）使通过率降至41%、碰撞59%、最小安全裕度-24.42，证明优先级方向错误即使各约束单独可行也会根本性破坏安全；固定正确排序虽100%无碰撞但车道严重越界，说明需要学习/混合多个合法扩张。附录E 四层偏序（中心线⪯速度⪯车道⪯障碍）展示在不牺牲高层安全的前提下注入低优先级偏好。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：把安全组合作为策略类的结构属性（偏序归纳偏置）而非优化目标中的显式约束，可将“约束满足问题”转化为“结构化学习问题”，在保持可行性、保留优先级语义的同时获得计算效率与适应性优势。局限：(1) 需要人工给出有意义的偏序，可能依赖领域知识，且难以自动从数据中推断；(2) 约束需先验设计，不适用于非结构化环境；(3) 限于控制仿射系统与屏障证书，不覆盖随机动力学与概率安全规格；(4) 混合（软）方式的安全保证依赖 C1/C2 几何条件，视觉驾驶任务可能违反该条件，故其安全结论应视为经验性；未来方向为自动发现/学习偏序、大/动态偏序的可扩展近似、随机与多智能体扩展、概率安全保证与感知不确定性整合。

Abstract: Safe learning is essential for deploying learningbased controllers in safety-critical robotic systems, yet existing approaches often enforce multiple safety constraints uniformly or via fixed priority orders, leading to infeasibility and brittle behavior. In practice, safety requirements are heterogeneous and admit only partial priority relations, where some constraints are comparable while others are inherently incomparable. We formalize this setting as poset-structured safety, modeling safety constraints as a partially ordered set and treating safety composition as a structural property of the policy class. Building on this formulation, we propose PoSafeNet, a differentiable neural safety layer that enforces safety via sequential closed-form projection under poset-consistent constraint orderings, enabling adaptive selection or mixing of valid safety executions while preserving priority semantics by construction. Experiments on multi-obstacle navigation, constrained robot manipulation, and vision-based autonomous driving demonstrate improved feasibility, robustness, and scalability over unstructured and differentiable quadratic program-based safety layers.

</details>


### [47] [Small Talk, Big Impact: The Energy Cost of Thanking AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22357)
*Julien Delavande,Regis Pierrard,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以用户对话末尾一句"thank you"作为可复现的微观交互单元，在单张 NVIDIA H100 上对开源指令模型（以 LLaMA 3.1–8B-Instruct FP32 为核心）进行 GPU/CPU/RAM 分量能耗测量：单次礼貌交互平均耗能约 0.245 Wh（GPU 0.202 Wh、CPU 0.024 Wh、RAM 0.019 Wh），相当于 5W LED 灯泡点亮近 3 分钟；能耗随 prompt/输出长度近似线性增长，decode 阶段占主导长尾，且随模型规模（0.5B→14B）陡增，说明礼貌用语在规模化部署中会累积成可观的算力与能源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 聊天助手普及，用户对模型表现出"ELIZA 效应"式的拟人化礼貌（如结尾的"thank you"）。然而每一句礼貌话术对模型而言都是一次完整推理，会触发数十亿参数的计算与不可忽略的能耗。此前研究多聚焦训练能耗、FLOPs 估算或宏观推理环境影响，很少有人系统量化这类"看似无害的微交互"的真实能源代价；同时缺少将行为学视角（礼貌、输出冗长）与系统能耗视角（prefill/decode 分相、延迟模型）结合的细粒度实证工作。作者希望以礼貌提示作为可控、可复现的代理单元，量化输入长度、输出长度、模型规模对能耗的影响，为可持续 AI 部署提供可操作建议，并讨论"效率 vs. 安全/对齐（礼貌提升模型表现、抑制有害回复、增强用户信任）"的权衡。

Method: 数据集：从 ultrachat_200k 抽取 10,000 条以用户"thank you"结尾的多轮对话，改写为 Instruct 模型的指令跟随模板，prompt 包含完整历史以模拟真实多轮场景。测量协议：每个 prompt 先做 5 次 warmup，再做 10 次 prefill-only（限制输出 1 token）与 10 次完整生成（最多 256 token）测量，用二者均值差分离 prefill 与 decode 能耗；记录输入/输出长度、延迟与各硬件能耗。硬件：单台专用推理服务器，1× NVIDIA H100 SXM 80GB + 8 核 AMD EPYC 7R13，无其他任务共占；GPU 用 NVML、CPU 用 pyRAPL (Intel RAPL)、RAM 用 CodeCarbon 模型估计。模型：核心为 LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct（float32，Transformers，batch size 1）；规模对比用同架构同 tokenizer 的 Qwen 2.5 家族（0.5B/1.5B/3B/7B/14B）与 Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3。理论：构建闭式延迟模型，将每个 GPU kernel 分为计算受限 t_o=max(F_o/F_max, D_o/B_max) 与访存受限，引入经验效率因子 μ_comp=0.675、μ_mem=0.443（由 H100 上 FP32 LLaMA 3.1–8B profiling 校准），忽略 kernel launch 开销并保守假设无重叠；据此拟合 prefill 延迟 t≈αs+βs²+γ（α≈3.18e-4 s/token，β≈1.17e-8 s/token²，γ≈1.68e-2 s）与 decode 延迟 t≈ηg+θsg+φg²+ρ，再结合各阶段平均功耗（prefill 684 W、decode 293 W）将延迟映射为能耗。

Result: 1) 单次"thank you"交互平均总能耗 0.245 Wh（GPU 0.202±0.096 Wh、CPU 0.024±0.014 Wh、RAM 0.019±0.010 Wh），GPU 比 CPU/RAM 高近一个数量级且方差最大，GPU 能耗分布右偏并有长尾。2) 分相分析：prefill 计算密集、并行度高，单位输出 token 成本最高但只发生一次；decode 为访存受限的自回归阶段，逐步累积构成长尾，对长输出总能耗贡献更大。3) 能量-长度关系：prefill 能耗随输入长度线性增长 E≈6.05e-5·s+5.00e-3 Wh（与计算受限一致）；decode 在 g≪10⁵ 时呈双线性 E≈2.13e-3·g+2.87e-7·s·g−4.71e-3 Wh，输出长度为主导、prompt 长度次要（体现逐步重访上下文的注意力开销），理论上的 g² 项在实际范围内不可观测；核级 tiling/block 对齐造成离散跳变但不改变总体线性趋势。4) 模型规模影响：更大模型平均输出更长、decode 能耗更高，二维插值等高线显示模型规模轴是主导因素，输出长度为次要因素；理论上源于层数 N、隐藏维度 h（prefill 复杂度 O(sNh²)、decode 为 O(gNh²)）与输出长度 g 的共同增长。5) 总体结论：礼貌交互在规模化场景会累积成显著算力与能源开销，prefill 与 decode 需区分对待（前者受并行收益，后者对串行开销敏感）。

Conclusion: 礼貌并非免费：一句"thank you"就会触发一次完整推理，规模化后形成可观能源足迹，因此应对 prompt/输出长度、模型规模与推理阶段做精细化优化（短输出、小模型、批处理与高效运行时），并把能耗纳入 LLM 应用的常规评估指标。作者同时提醒存在效率与安全/对齐的权衡——礼貌提示有助提升模型对齐度、抑制有害回复与建立信任，故不应盲目去除。局限包括仅短礼貌提示、batch size=1、仅开源模型与单 H100 + PyTorch 后端、延迟模型忽略 kernel launch 与缓存复用、未换算碳排放与货币成本；未来工作将扩展到多轮长对话、批量推理、其他硬件（A100/L4/AMD/TPU）与 vLLM/TGI 等优化运行时，并引入 kernel 级 profiling 以及能耗→碳排/成本/准确率权衡的映射。

Abstract: Being polite is free - or is it? In this paper, we quantify the energy cost of seemingly innocuous messages such as ``thank you'' when interacting with large language models, often used by users to convey politeness. Using real-world conversation traces and fine-grained energy measurements, we quantify how input length, output length and model size affect energy use. While politeness is our motivating example, it also serves as a controlled and reproducible proxy for measuring the energy footprint of a typical LLM interaction. Our findings provide actionable insights for building more sustainable and efficient LLM applications, especially in increasingly widespread real-world contexts like chat. As user adoption grows and billions of prompts are processed daily, understanding and mitigating this cost becomes crucial - not just for efficiency, but for sustainable AI deployment.

</details>


### [48] [The Unseen Threat: Residual Knowledge in Machine Unlearning under Perturbed Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22359)
*Hsiang Hsu,Pradeep Niroula,Zichang He,Ivan Brugere,Freddy Lecue,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文（JPMorgan Chase Global Technology Applied Research）揭示了机器遗忘（machine unlearning）中一个此前未被关注的隐私漏洞：现有遗忘方法通常通过"遗忘模型与重训练模型在统计上不可区分"（(ε,δ)-不可区分性或 Rényi 遗忘）来认证，但这类保证无法自然推广到被对抗性扰动的输入上。作者将其形式化为"残余知识（residual knowledge）"——遗忘样本的微小扰动变体仍能被遗忘模型正确识别，而重训练模型却无法识别，意味着遗忘样本的局部邻域中仍留有信息痕迹。作者证明在高维空间中这种扰动下的不一致（disagreement）是不可避免的（基于等周不等式），并提出微调策略 RURK：在遗忘样本的 ℓp 邻域内构造"脆弱扰动集"，惩罚模型对其重新识别，从而抑制残余知识。实验覆盖小 CIFAR-5、完整 CIFAR-10、ImageNet-100 及 11 个基线方法，RURK 在与 Re-train 的平均性能差距（Avg. Gap）上最小（2.60 / 2.65），并能有效压低残余知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘被广泛用作避免全量重训练、响应 GDPR"被遗忘权"的实用方案，但重训练成本高昂。现有遗忘算法的认证框架（Guo et al. 2020 的 (ε,δ)-不可区分性、Chourasia & Shah 2023 与 Chien et al. 2024 的 (α,ε)-Rényi 遗忘、基于读出函数的 KL 散度认证）只保证遗忘模型与重训练模型在原始遗忘/保留样本上的统计相似性。作者指出：这些保证不覆盖输入的"邻域"，即对遗忘样本施加不可感知的对抗扰动后，两个模型可能给出不同预测；尤其当"遗忘模型仍正确分类、重训练模型却分错"时，说明遗忘样本的信息仍残留在模型的决策边界中，构成新的隐私风险。此前工作（Marchant et al. 2022、Pawelczyk et al. 2025、Zhao et al. 2024、Xuan & Li 2025）多关注扰动/投毒对遗忘模型本身的影响，未考察"遗忘模型 vs 重训练模型"的可区分性。作者因此提出三个问题：(1) 在统计不可区分的前提下能否构造区分两者的对抗输入？(2) 该不一致在多维空间中是否不可避免？(3) 如何度量并缓解这种残余知识？

Method: 理论部分：定义 (ε,δ)-不可区分性（Definition 1）与读出函数认证框架；将对抗样本生成形式化为读出函数 g_x。Proposition 1 证明：若遗忘模型与重训练模型 (ε,δ)-不可区分，则以概率 2δ/(1-e^{-ε})，对抗样本的可区分度会被"放大一倍"（似然比被 e^{±2ε} 放宽）。Proposition 2 结合等周不等式（Milman & Schechtman 1986 的半球 τ-扩张面积下界，Lemma A.2）与 Lemma A.3（不一致概率下界 > 1-O(e^{-2ε})）证明：设一致区域表面积占比 ≤ 1/2，则以概率 (2δ/(1-e^{-ε}))·{1-O[(π/8)^{1/2}exp(-2ε-(d-1)τ²/2)]}，样本点本身或其 τ-邻域内必存在两模型预测不一致的点；ε=δ=0 时概率为 0，ε→∞ 时上界趋于 2δ，p=2 与 p=∞ 均成立（单位球面假设可推广至单位立方体，Ledoux 2001）。度量部分：定义 adversarial disagreement k_τ(x')=1(m(x')≠a(x'))，并提出更可操作且有明确隐私含义的"残余知识" r_τ((x,y)) = Pr[m(x')=y]/Pr[a(x')=y]，x'~i.i.d. B_p(x,τ)，可通过 Monte Carlo（c=100 次采样，两模型共用同一扰动）估计；Lemma A.4 给出 r_τ 对 E[k_τ] 的上下界（r_τ→1 时不一致趋于 0），说明残余知识是区分度的可操作代理。方法部分：提出 RURK（Robust Unlearning against Residual Knowledge），定义脆弱扰动集 V((x,y),τ)={x'∈B_p(x,τ) | m(x')=y}（通过 min_{z∈B_p} ℓ(w,(z,y)) 求解，可用 FGSM/PGD 等实现），并构造微调目标 L_RURK = (1/|S_r|)Σ_retain ℓ − λ·(1/|S_f|)Σ_forget κ，其中 κ 为脆弱扰动集上损失的平均；Term(i) 保留效用，Term(ii) 惩罚残余知识。工程上取高效近似 V = B_p(x,τ)（整个扰动球）、τ=0.03、v=1、SGD、支持原地更新，计算复杂度与 GD/NGD/NegGrad+ 同级；τ→0 时退化为既有方法的目标；PNGD 下仍满足 (α,ε)-Rényi 遗忘，但 τ 增大会提升损失平滑性/Lipschitz 常数从而增大 ε，形成"不可区分性—鲁棒性"权衡。实验：在小 CIFAR-5（5 类×200 训练/200 测试，遗忘 class 0 的 50%=100 样本）、完整 CIFAR-10（遗忘 class 0 的 2000 样本）、ImageNet-100（ResNet-50，每类 1300 张）三种场景下，与 Original、Re-train 及 11 个基线（CR、Fisher、NTK、GD、NGD、GA、NegGrad+、EU-k、CF-k、SCRUB、SSD）比较，指标包括 Retain/Unlearn/Test/MIA(SVC 攻击失败率) 精度、Avg. Gap、Re-learn Time，并用高斯噪声/targeted FGSM/targeted PGD 三类扰动估计残余知识曲线；另做 class unlearning、τ/λ/v/攻击类型/架构（VGG-11）消融。

Result: 残余知识普遍存在：小 CIFAR-5 上 NTK 在 τ=0.013 时约 11%、τ=0.031 时升至 48% 的遗忘样本残余知识 >1；CIFAR-10 上 NGD 在 τ≈0.009–0.013 时约 8–8.5%（约 160 个样本），摘要中报告 τ≈0.03 时超过 7% 的遗忘样本仍存在残余知识，说明"遗忘准确率/MIA/Re-learn Time"等传统指标不足以刻画该风险（NTK 的 Avg. Gap 与 RURK 相当但残余知识 >1，源于 NTK 线性化忽略高阶项）。RURK 表现最优：small CIFAR-5 上 Avg. Gap 2.60（优于 NTK 3.23、NegGrad+ 10.55、SSD 6.29、SCRUB 3.97、NGD 6.92、GD 18.89），CIFAR-10 上 Avg. Gap 2.65（优于 NegGrad+ 2.71、NGD 6.27、CF-k 8.16、GD 8.22），且 Re-learn Time 在 CIFAR-10 上 >30（接近 Re-train 的 17.33 语义，最难被重新学习）。残余知识曲线方面，RURK 在 τ<0.01 时保持接近 1（不产生过度遗忘），在更大 τ 时有效压制到 1 以下；GD/CF-k 残余知识接近 Original（未显式接触遗忘样本），GA/SSD 在 τ=0 时已低于 1（属过度遗忘），NGD 因权重噪声优于 GD 但弱于 RURK，NegGrad+ 因缺少扰动项而残留更多。ImageNet-100 上 RURK 仍取得最小 Avg. Gap 并最有效抑制残余知识，说明该风险不限于小规模场景。消融显示 τ=0.03 取得最优 Avg. Gap（τ 过小则无法遗忘，过大则测试精度下降），λ 与 τ 趋势一致，v∈{1..4} 结果稳定；用 targeted FGSM/PGD 构造脆弱集可获得接近 Re-train 的 MIA 精度；VGG-11 上 RURK 同样优于 NGD。class unlearning 场景下 EU-k 因类似迁移学习而 Avg. Gap 最优（0.84），但 RURK 在残余知识抑制上更优，且 CIFAR-10 class unlearning 下 RURK 的 Avg. Gap（1.70）为最佳。

Conclusion: 结论：现有遗忘方法虽抹除了遗忘样本的直接记忆，却普遍未消除其周围的隐式泛化，因而遗忘模型比重训练模型更容易"重新认出"被扰动的遗忘样本；残余知识因此是对传统认证框架的必要补充，且在高维空间中不可消除。RURK 通过在遗忘样本邻域上引入扰动一致性正则，在保持保留集精度的同时有效抑制残余知识，且与梯度类方法开销相当、可原地更新、支持大规模模型。局限：要在整个 ℓp 邻域上控制对抗不一致在计算上不可行（难度等同完美对抗鲁棒）；由于真实遗忘场景中拿不到重训练模型，只能约束残余知识的一侧（r_τ ≤ 1）而无法保证两侧。未来方向：把概率分析扩展到假设类复杂度并与对抗样本可迁移性联系；深入研究"不可区分性—鲁棒性"权衡在遗忘与对抗鲁棒中的关系；RURK 目标类似对抗训练的反向版本，值得检验对抗训练/分布鲁棒优化是否反而妨碍遗忘或放大残余知识；MIA 可借助"重新识别遗忘样本所需的最小扰动范数"这一侧信息提升推断成功率；将残余知识概念推广到生成任务（图像/文本生成、概念遗忘、LLM 的重学习与越狱现象）。更广影响：若用户退出基于生物特征（如掌纹、人脸）的支付系统，残余痕迹仍可能让攻击者构造扰动输入被系统接受，从而对"被遗忘权"的现有解释与信任体系构成挑战。

Abstract: Machine unlearning offers a practical alternative to avoid full model re-training by approximately removing the influence of specific user data. While existing methods certify unlearning via statistical indistinguishability from re-trained models, these guarantees do not naturally extend to model outputs when inputs are adversarially perturbed. In particular, slight perturbations of forget samples may still be correctly recognized by the unlearned model - even when a re-trained model fails to do so - revealing a novel privacy risk: information about the forget samples may persist in their local neighborhood. In this work, we formalize this vulnerability as residual knowledge and show that it is inevitable in high-dimensional settings. To mitigate this risk, we propose a fine-tuning strategy, named RURK, that penalizes the model's ability to re-recognize perturbed forget samples. Experiments on vision benchmarks with deep neural networks demonstrate that residual knowledge is prevalent across existing unlearning methods and that our approach effectively prevents residual knowledge.

</details>


### [49] [Understanding Efficiency: Quantization, Batching, and Serving Strategies in LLM Energy Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22362)
*Julien Delavande,Regis Pierrard,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过实证研究发现，大语言模型推理的能源效率显著受系统级设计决策（如数值精度、批处理策略和请求调度）影响，同一模型在不同配置下能耗可能相差百倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在生产环境中的广泛部署，计算资源和能源需求正从训练转向推理。虽然先前工作关注了每个提示或每个token的推理能耗，但本研究强调系统级设计选择对能耗的巨大影响，以推动更可持续的AI服务部署。

Method: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上进行详细的LLM推理能量和延迟实证研究，分析量化、批处理大小和服务配置（如使用Hugging Face的Text Generation Inference服务器）的影响，特别关注计算受限和内存受限的不同阶段。

Result: 研究发现：低精度格式仅在计算受限阶段带来能耗效益；批处理显著提高能源效率，特别是在解码等内存受限阶段；结构化请求时序（到达整形）能将每个请求的能耗降低高达100倍。

Conclusion: 可持续的LLM部署不仅取决于模型内部优化，还依赖于服务栈的编排。这些发现强调了面向阶段的能源分析和系统级优化对实现绿色AI服务的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in production, contributing towards shifting the burden in terms of computational resources and energy demands from training to inference. While prior work has examined the energy cost of inference per prompt or per token, we highlight how \emph{system-level design choices} - such as numerical precision, batching strategy, and request scheduling - can lead to orders-of-magnitude differences in energy consumption for the same model. We perform a detailed empirical study of LLM inference energy and latency on NVIDIA H100 GPUs, analyzing the impact of quantization, batch size, and serving configuration (e.g., with Hugging Face's Text Generation Inference server). Our results reveal that lower-precision formats only yield energy gains in compute-bound regimes; that batching improves energy efficiency, especially in memory-bound phases like decoding; and that structured request timing (arrival shaping) can reduce per-request energy by up to 100 times. We argue that sustainable LLM deployment depends not only on model internals, but also on the orchestration of the serving stack. Our findings motivate phase-aware energy profiling and system-level optimizations for greener AI services.

</details>


### [50] [FIRE: Multi-fidelity Regression with Distribution-conditioned In-context Learning using Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22371)
*Rosen Ting-Ying Yu,Nicholas Sung,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Multi-fidelity (MF) regression often operates in regimes of extreme data imbalance, where the commonly-used Gaussian-process (GP) surrogates struggle with cubic scaling costs and overfit to sparse high-fidelity observations, limiting efficiency and generalization in real-world applications. We introduce FIRE, a training-free MF framework that couples tabular foundation models (TFMs) to perform zero-shot in-context Bayesian inference via a high-fidelity correction model conditioned on the low-fidelity model's posterior predictive distributions. This cross-fidelity information transfer via distributional summaries captures heteroscedastic errors, enabling robust residual learning without model retraining. Across 31 benchmark problems spanning synthetic and real-world tasks (e.g., DrivAerNet, LCBench), FIRE delivers a stronger performance-time trade-off than seven state-of-the-art GP-based or deep learning MF regression methods, ranking highest in accuracy and uncertainty quantification with runtime advantages. Limitations include context window constraints and dependence on the quality of the pre-trained TFM's.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Multi-fidelity (MF) regression often operates in regimes of extreme data imbalance, where the commonly-used Gaussian-process (GP) surrogates struggle with cubic scaling costs and overfit to sparse high-fidelity observations, limiting efficiency and generalization in real-world applications. We introduce FIRE, a training-free MF framework that couples tabular foundation models (TFMs) to perform zero-shot in-context Bayesian inference via a high-fidelity correction model conditioned on the low-fidelity model's posterior predictive distributions. This cross-fidelity information transfer via distributional summaries captures heteroscedastic errors, enabling robust residual learning without model retraining. Across 31 benchmark problems spanning synthetic and real-world tasks (e.g., DrivAerNet, LCBench), FIRE delivers a stronger performance-time trade-off than seven state-of-the-art GP-based or deep learning MF regression methods, ranking highest in accuracy and uncertainty quantification with runtime advantages. Limitations include context window constraints and dependence on the quality of the pre-trained TFM's.

### [51] [Purely Agentic Black-Box Optimization for Biological Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22382)
*Natalie Maus,Yimeng Zeng,Haydn Thomas Jones,Yining Huang,Gaurav Ng Goel,Alden Rose,Kyurae Kim,Hyun-Su Lee,Marcelo Der Torossian Torres,Fangping Wan,Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez,Mark Yatskar,Osbert Bastani,Jacob R. Gardner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Many key challenges in biological design-such as small-molecule drug discovery, antimicrobial peptide development, and protein engineering-can be framed as black-box optimization over vast, complex structured spaces. Existing methods rely mainly on raw structural data and struggle to exploit the rich scientific literature. While large language models (LLMs) have been added to these pipelines, they have been confined to narrow roles within structure-centered optimizers. We instead cast biological black-box optimization as a fully agentic, language-based reasoning process. We introduce Purely Agentic BLack-box Optimization (PABLO), a hierarchical agentic system that uses scientific LLMs pretrained on chemistry and biology literature to generate and iteratively refine biological candidates. On both the standard GuacaMol molecular design and antimicrobial peptide optimization tasks, PABLO achieves state-of-the-art performance, substantially improving sample efficiency and final objective values over established baselines. Compared to prior optimization methods that incorporate LLMs, PABLO achieves competitive token usage per run despite relying on LLMs throughout the optimization loop. Beyond raw performance, the agentic formulation offers key advantages for realistic design: it naturally incorporates semantic task descriptions, retrieval-augmented domain knowledge, and complex constraints. In follow-up in vitro validation, PABLO-optimized peptides showed strong activity against drug-resistant pathogens, underscoring the practical potential of PABLO for therapeutic discovery.

### [52] [Graph is a Substrate Across Data Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22384)
*Ziming Li,Xiaoming Wu,Zehong Wang,Jiazheng Li,Yijun Tian,Jinhe Bi,Yunpu Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G-Substrate框架通过统一结构模式和角色间训练策略，使图表示能够在异构模态和任务间持久积累，超越任务孤立和简单多任务学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图结构学习通常是在模态和任务孤立的方式下进行的，图表示在同一任务上下文中构建后被丢弃。这导致跨模态和任务的结构规律性需要反复重建，无法在中间图表示层面积累。

Method: 1. 提出G-Substrate图基底框架；2. 统一结构模式确保异构模态和任务间图表示的兼容性；3. 角色间训练策略在学习过程中将同一图结构暴露给多个功能角色。

Result: 在多个领域、模态和任务上的实验表明，G-Substrate优于任务孤立和简单多任务学习方法。

Conclusion: 将图结构视为跨学习上下文持续存在的结构基底，并采用表示中心的视角组织学习，能够有效促进图结构在异构模态和任务间的持久积累。

Abstract: Graphs provide a natural representation of relational structure that arises across diverse domains. Despite this ubiquity, graph structure is typically learned in a modality- and task-isolated manner, where graph representations are constructed within individual task contexts and discarded thereafter. As a result, structural regularities across modalities and tasks are repeatedly reconstructed rather than accumulated at the level of intermediate graph representations. This motivates a representation-learning question: how should graph structure be organized so that it can persist and accumulate across heterogeneous modalities and tasks? We adopt a representation-centric perspective in which graph structure is treated as a structural substrate that persists across learning contexts. To instantiate this perspective, we propose G-Substrate, a graph substrate framework that organizes learning around shared graph structures. G-Substrate comprises two complementary mechanisms: a unified structural schema that ensures compatibility among graph representations across heterogeneous modalities and tasks, and an interleaved role-based training strategy that exposes the same graph structure to multiple functional roles during learning. Experiments across multiple domains, modalities, and tasks show that G-Substrate outperforms task-isolated and naive multi-task learning methods.

</details>


### [53] [Score-based Integrated Gradient for Root Cause Explanations of Outliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22399)
*Phuoc Nguyen,Truyen Tran,Sunil Gupta,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Identifying the root causes of outliers is a fundamental problem in causal inference and anomaly detection. Traditional approaches based on heuristics or counterfactual reasoning often struggle under uncertainty and high-dimensional dependencies. We introduce SIREN, a novel and scalable method that attributes the root causes of outliers by estimating the score functions of the data likelihood. Attribution is computed via integrated gradients that accumulate score contributions along paths from the outlier toward the normal data distribution. Our method satisfies three of the four classic Shapley value axioms - dummy, efficiency, and linearity - as well as an asymmetry axiom derived from the underlying causal structure. Unlike prior work, SIREN operates directly on the score function, enabling tractable and uncertainty-aware root cause attribution in nonlinear, high-dimensional, and heteroscedastic causal models. Extensive experiments on synthetic random graphs and real-world cloud service and supply chain datasets show that SIREN outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both attribution accuracy and computational efficiency.

### [54] [Optimization, Generalization and Differential Privacy Bounds for Gradient Descent on Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22409)
*Puyu Wang,Junyu Zhou,Philipp Liznerski,Marius Kloft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) have recently emerged as a structured alternative to standard MLPs, yet a principled theory for their training dynamics, generalization, and privacy properties remains limited. In this paper, we analyze gradient descent (GD) for training two-layer KANs and derive general bounds that characterize their training dynamics, generalization, and utility under differential privacy (DP). As a concrete instantiation, we specialize our analysis to logistic loss under an NTK-separable assumption, where we show that polylogarithmic network width suffices for GD to achieve an optimization rate of order $1/T$ and a generalization rate of order $1/n$, with $T$ denoting the number of GD iterations and $n$ the sample size. In the private setting, we characterize the noise required for $(ε,δ)$-DP and obtain a utility bound of order $\sqrt{d}/(nε)$ (with $d$ the input dimension), matching the classical lower bound for general convex Lipschitz problems. Our results imply that polylogarithmic width is not only sufficient but also necessary under differential privacy, revealing a qualitative gap between non-private (sufficiency only) and private (necessity also emerges) training regimes. Experiments further illustrate how these theoretical insights can guide practical choices, including network width selection and early stopping.

### [55] [MM-OpenFGL: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22416)
*Xunkai Li,Yuming Ai,Yinlin Zhu,Haodong Lu,Yi Zhang,Guohao Fu,Bowen Fan,Qiangqiang Dai,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MMAGs) provide a unified framework for modeling complex relational data by integrating heterogeneous modalities with graph structures. While centralized learning has shown promising performance, MMAGs in real-world applications are often distributed across isolated platforms and cannot be shared due to privacy concerns or commercial constraints. Federated graph learning (FGL) offers a natural solution for collaborative training under such settings; however, existing studies largely focus on single-modality graphs and do not adequately address the challenges unique to multimodal federated graph learning (MMFGL). To bridge this gap, we present MM-OpenFGL, the first comprehensive benchmark that systematically formalizes the MMFGL paradigm and enables rigorous evaluation. MM-OpenFGL comprises 19 multimodal datasets spanning 7 application domains, 8 simulation strategies capturing modality and topology variations, 6 downstream tasks, and 57 state-of-the-art methods implemented through a modular API. Extensive experiments investigate MMFGL from the perspectives of necessity, effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency, offering valuable insights for future research on MMFGL.

### [56] [MetaLead: A Comprehensive Human-Curated Leaderboard Dataset for Transparent Reporting of Machine Learning Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22420)
*Roelien C. Timmer,Necva Bölücü,Stephen Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Leaderboards are crucial in the machine learning (ML) domain for benchmarking and tracking progress. However, creating leaderboards traditionally demands significant manual effort. In recent years, efforts have been made to automate leaderboard generation, but existing datasets for this purpose are limited by capturing only the best results from each paper and limited metadata. We present MetaLead, a fully human-annotated ML Leaderboard dataset that captures all experimental results for result transparency and contains extra metadata, such as the result experimental type: baseline, proposed method, or variation of proposed method for experiment-type guided comparisons, and explicitly separates train and test dataset for cross-domain assessment. This enriched structure makes MetaLead a powerful resource for more transparent and nuanced evaluations across ML research.

### [57] [CoDCL: Counterfactual Data Augmentation Contrastive Learning for Continuous-Time Dynamic Network Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22427)
*Hantong Feng,Yonggang Wu,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The rapid growth and continuous structural evolution of dynamic networks make effective predictions increasingly challenging. To enable prediction models to adapt to complex temporal environments, they need to be robust to emerging structural changes. We propose a dynamic network learning framework CoDCL, which combines counterfactual data augmentation with contrastive learning to address this deficiency.Furthermore, we devise a comprehensive strategy to generate high-quality counterfactual data, combining a dynamic treatments design with efficient structural neighborhood exploration to quantify the temporal changes in interaction patterns.Crucially, the entire CoDCL is designed as a plug-and-play universal module that can be seamlessly integrated into various existing temporal graph models without requiring architectural modifications.Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that CoDCL significantly gains state-of-the-art baseline models in the field of dynamic networks, confirming the critical role of integrating counterfactual data augmentation into dynamic representation learning.

### [58] [ReNCE: Learning to Reason by Noise Contrastive Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22432)
*Wenzheng Zhang,Karl Stratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO is a standard approach to endowing pretrained LLMs with reasoning capabilities. It estimates the advantage of an outcome from a group of $K$ outcomes, and promotes those with positive advantages inside a trust region. Since GRPO discriminates between good and bad outcomes softly, it benefits from additional refinements such as asymmetric clipping and zero-variance data filtering. While effective, these refinements require significant empirical insight and can be challenging to identify. We instead propose an explicit contrastive learning approach. Instead of estimating advantages, we bifurcate $K$ outcomes into positive and negative sets, then maximize the likelihood of positive outcomes. Our approach can be viewed as an online instantiation of (multi-label) noise contrastive estimation for LLM reasoning. We validate our method by demonstrating competitive performance on a suite of challenging math benchmarks against strong baselines such as DAPO and online DPO.

### [59] [Weak Diffusion Priors Can Still Achieve Strong Inverse-Problem Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22443)
*Jing Jia,Wei Yuan,Sifan Liu,Liyue Shen,Guanyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究扩散模型作为先验在逆问题求解中的鲁棒性，特别是当使用不匹配或低质量先验时的表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通常作为逆问题的先验使用，但标准方法假设模型在训练数据与未知信号高度匹配。实践中常需使用不匹配或低质量的先验。令人惊讶的是，这种弱先验往往表现接近完整先验。研究旨在探索逆求解器对弱扩散先验鲁棒性的条件和原因。

Method: 通过大量实验分析弱先验的表现，发现当测量数据高度信息（如有大量观测像素）时能成功。基于贝叶斯一致性的理论，给出高维测量使后验集中于真实信号附近的条件。

Result: 识别出弱先验成功与失败的机制。理论结果表明，在一定条件下高维测量能使后验集中于真实信号附近，为弱扩散先验的可靠使用提供了原理性依据。

Conclusion: 弱扩散先验在逆问题求解中具有鲁棒性，特别是在测量数据高度信息时。该研究为理解和使用弱先验提供了理论基础和实践指导，扩展了扩散模型在不匹配数据场景下的应用可行性。

Abstract: Can a diffusion model trained on bedrooms recover human faces? Diffusion models are widely used as priors for inverse problems, but standard approaches usually assume a high-fidelity model trained on data that closely match the unknown signal. In practice, one often must use a mismatched or low-fidelity diffusion prior. Surprisingly, these weak priors often perform nearly as well as full-strength, in-domain baselines. We study when and why inverse solvers are robust to weak diffusion priors. Through extensive experiments, we find that weak priors succeed when measurements are highly informative (e.g., many observed pixels), and we identify regimes where they fail. Our theory, based on Bayesian consistency, gives conditions under which high-dimensional measurements make the posterior concentrate near the true signal. These results provide a principled justification on when weak diffusion priors can be used reliably.

</details>


### [60] [Automating Forecasting Question Generation and Resolution for AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22444)
*Nikos I. Bosse,Peter Mühlbacher,Jack Wildman,Lawrence Phillips,Dan Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Forecasting future events is highly valuable in decision-making and is a robust measure of general intelligence. As forecasting is probabilistic, developing and evaluating AI forecasters requires generating large numbers of diverse and difficult questions, and accurately resolving them. Previous efforts to automate this laborious work relied on recurring data sources (e.g., weather, stocks), limiting diversity and utility. In this work, we present a system for generating and resolving high-quality forecasting questions automatically and at scale using LLM-powered web research agents. We use this system to generate 1499 diverse, real-world forecasting questions, and to resolve them several months later. We estimate that our system produces verifiable, unambiguous questions approximately 96% of the time, exceeding the rate of Metaculus, a leading human-curated forecasting platform. We also find that our system resolves questions at approximately 95% accuracy. We verify that forecasting agents powered by more intelligent LLMs perform better on these questions (Brier score of 0.134 for Gemini 3 Pro, 0.149 for GPT-5, and 0.179 for Gemini 2.5 Flash). Finally, we demonstrate how our system can be leveraged to directly improve forecasting, by evaluating a question decomposition strategy on a generated question set, yielding a significant improvement in Brier scores (0.132 vs. 0.141).

### [61] [Beyond Activation Patterns: A Weight-Based Out-of-Context Explanation of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22447)
*Yiting Liu,Zhi-Hong Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful technique for decomposing language model representations into interpretable features. Current interpretation methods infer feature semantics from activation patterns, but overlook that features are trained to reconstruct activations that serve computational roles in the forward pass. We introduce a novel weight-based interpretation framework that measures functional effects through direct weight interactions, requiring no activation data. Through three experiments on Gemma-2 and Llama-3.1 models, we demonstrate that (1) 1/4 of features directly predict output tokens, (2) features actively participate in attention mechanisms with depth-dependent structure, and (3) semantic and non-semantic feature populations exhibit distinct distribution profiles in attention circuits. Our analysis provides the missing out-of-context half of SAE feature interpretability.

### [62] [HeaPA: Difficulty-Aware Heap Sampling and On-Policy Query Augmentation for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22448)
*Weiqi Wang,Xin Liu,Binxuan Huang,Hejie Cui,Rongzhi Zhang,Changlong Yu,Shuowei Jin,Jingfeng Yang,Qingyu Yin,Zhengyang Wang,Zheng Li,Yifan Gao,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Lihong Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HeaPA是一种用于RLVR训练的高效提示抽样与池扩充方法，通过堆式边界抽样跟踪能力边界，并采用基于策略的异步验证来扩展池，从而在保持池规模可控的同时减少计算开销、提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR训练中，提示池通常是静态的或与模型学习进度关联松散，导致均匀抽样无法紧跟能力边界变化，造成在已解决或遥不可及的提示上浪费大量生成开销。现有改进方法或假定固定池（难以支持稳定的在线池增长），或引入额外的教师成本和延迟。

Method: 1. 维护一个有界、演化的提示池；2. 使用堆式边界抽样跟踪模型的能力边界；3. 通过基于策略的查询扩充与轻量级异步验证来扩展池；4. 通过拓扑感知的统计重估计与控制重插入来稳定相关查询。

Result: 在两个训练语料库、两种训练方案和七个基准测试中，HeaPA一致提升了准确性，并以更少的计算量达到目标性能，同时保持运行时间相当。分析表明，收益来自边界聚焦抽样和基于策略的池增长，且随着模型规模增大而更为明显。

Conclusion: HeaPA通过动态维护提示池、高效抽样边界样本和基于策略的异步扩充，显著提高了RLVR训练的效率和性能，尤其适用于大规模模型训练。

Abstract: RLVR is now a standard way to train LLMs on reasoning tasks with verifiable outcomes, but when rollout generation dominates the cost, efficiency depends heavily on which prompts you sample and when. In practice, prompt pools are often static or only loosely tied to the model's learning progress, so uniform sampling can't keep up with the shifting capability frontier and ends up wasting rollouts on prompts that are already solved or still out of reach. Existing approaches improve efficiency through filtering, curricula, adaptive rollout allocation, or teacher guidance, but they typically assume a fixed pool-which makes it hard to support stable on-policy pool growth-or they add extra teacher cost and latency. We introduce HeaPA (Heap Sampling and On-Policy Query Augmentation), which maintains a bounded, evolving pool, tracks the frontier using heap-based boundary sampling, expands the pool via on-policy augmentation with lightweight asynchronous validation, and stabilizes correlated queries through topology-aware re-estimation of pool statistics and controlled reinsertion. Across two training corpora, two training recipes, and seven benchmarks, HeaPA consistently improves accuracy and reaches target performance with fewer computations while keeping wall-clock time comparable. Our analyses suggest these gains come from frontier-focused sampling and on-policy pool growth, with the benefits becoming larger as model scale increases. Our code is available at https://github.com/horizon-rl/HeaPA.

</details>


### [63] [Tuning the Implicit Regularizer of Masked Diffusion Language Models: Enhancing Generalization via Insights from $k$-Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22450)
*Jianhao Huang,Baharan Mirzasoleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散语言模型在k-奇偶校验问题中的泛化特性，理论分解其目标为信号学习机制与噪声正则化机制，通过优化掩码概率分布显著提升模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型作为强大的生成范式，其泛化特性相比自回归模型研究不足。本文旨在探究其在k-奇偶校验问题中避免陷入'顿悟'现象的机制，从而改进大规模掩码扩散语言模型的训练效果

Method: 理论分析掩码扩散目标，将其分解为促进特征学习的信号机制和起到隐式正则化作用的噪声机制；基于此优化掩码概率分布；在nanoGPT上训练k-奇偶校验问题；在大规模模型（50M至8B参数）上进行预训练和微调实验验证

Result: 掩码扩散目标从根本上改变学习景观，实现快速同步泛化而无需经历顿悟期；优化掩码分布显著提升50M参数模型的困惑度；在8B参数模型上获得了8.8%的预训练性能提升和5.8%的微调性能提升

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言模型通过独特的信号-噪声分解机制克服了k-奇偶校验问题中的顿悟现象；通过优化掩码概率分布能够有效提升大规模模型的性能，证实了其可扩展性和有效性

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Language Models have recently emerged as a powerful generative paradigm, yet their generalization properties remain understudied compared to their auto-regressive counterparts. In this work, we investigate these properties within the setting of the $k$-parity problem (computing the XOR sum of $k$ relevant bits), where neural networks typically exhibit grokking -- a prolonged plateau of chance-level performance followed by sudden generalization. We theoretically decompose the Masked Diffusion (MD) objective into a Signal regime which drives feature learning, and a Noise regime which serves as an implicit regularizer. By training nanoGPT using MD objective on the $k$-parity problem, we demonstrate that MD objective fundamentally alters the learning landscape, enabling rapid and simultaneous generalization without experiencing grokking. Furthermore, we leverage our theoretical insights to optimize the distribution of the mask probability in the MD objective. Our method significantly improves perplexity for 50M-parameter models and achieves superior results across both pre-training from scratch and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, we observe performance gains peaking at $8.8\%$ and $5.8\%$, respectively, on 8B-parameter models, confirming the scalability and effectiveness of our framework in large-scale masked diffusion language model regimes.

</details>


### [64] [Temporal Graph Pattern Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22454)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Temporal graph learning is pivotal for deciphering dynamic systems, where the core challenge lies in explicitly modeling the underlying evolving patterns that govern network transformation. However, prevailing methods are predominantly task-centric and rely on restrictive assumptions -- such as short-term dependency modeling, static neighborhood semantics, and retrospective time usage. These constraints hinder the discovery of transferable temporal evolution mechanisms. To address this, we propose the Temporal Graph Pattern Machine (TGPM), a foundation framework that shifts the focus toward directly learning generalized evolving patterns. TGPM conceptualizes each interaction as an interaction patch synthesized via temporally-biased random walks, thereby capturing multi-scale structural semantics and long-range dependencies that extend beyond immediate neighborhoods. These patches are processed by a Transformer-based backbone designed to capture global temporal regularities while adapting to context-specific interaction dynamics. To further empower the model, we introduce a suite of self-supervised pre-training tasks -- specifically masked token modeling and next-time prediction -- to explicitly encode the fundamental laws of network evolution. Extensive experiments show that TGPM consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both transductive and inductive link prediction, demonstrating exceptional cross-domain transferability.

### [65] [Machine Unlearning in Low-Dimensional Feature Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22456)
*Kun Fang,Qinghua Tao,Junxu Liu,Yaxin Xiao,Qingqing Ye,Jian Sun,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Machine Unlearning (MU) aims at removing the influence of specific data from a pretrained model while preserving performance on the remaining data. In this work, a novel perspective for MU is presented upon low-dimensional feature subspaces, which gives rise to the potentials of separating the remaining and forgetting data herein. This separability motivates our LOFT, a method that proceeds unlearning in a LOw-dimensional FeaTure subspace from the pretrained model skithrough principal projections, which are optimized to maximally capture the information of the remaining data and meanwhile diminish that of the forgetting data. In training, LOFT simply optimizes a small-size projection matrix flexibly plugged into the pretrained model, and only requires one-shot feature fetching from the pretrained backbone instead of repetitively accessing the raw data. Hence, LOFT mitigates two critical issues in mainstream MU methods, i.e., the privacy leakage risk from massive data reload and the inefficiency of updates to the entire pretrained model. Extensive experiments validate the significantly lower computational overhead and superior unlearning performance of LOFT across diverse models, datasets, tasks, and applications. Code is anonymously available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/4352/.

### [66] [EvoEGF-Mol: Evolving Exponential Geodesic Flow for Structure-based Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22466)
*Yaowei Jin,Junjie Wang,Cheng Cao,Penglei Wang,Duo An,Qian Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Structure-Based Drug Design (SBDD) aims to discover bioactive ligands. Conventional approaches construct probability paths separately in Euclidean and probabilistic spaces for continuous atomic coordinates and discrete chemical categories, leading to a mismatch with the underlying statistical manifolds. We address this issue from an information-geometric perspective by modeling molecules as composite exponential-family distributions and defining generative flows along exponential geodesics under the Fisher-Rao metric. To avoid the instantaneous trajectory collapse induced by geodesics directly targeting Dirac distributions, we propose Evolving Exponential Geodesic Flow for SBDD (EvoEGF-Mol), which replaces static Dirac targets with dynamically concentrating distributions, ensuring stable training via a progressive-parameter-refinement architecture. Our model approaches a reference-level PoseBusters passing rate (93.4%) on CrossDock, demonstrating remarkable geometric precision and interaction fidelity, while outperforming baselines on real-world MolGenBench tasks by recovering bioactive scaffolds and generating candidates that meet established MedChem filters.

### [67] [Unrewarded Exploration in Large Language Models Reveals Latent Learning from Psychology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22474)
*Jian Xiong,Jingbo Zhou,Zihan Zhou,Yixiong Xiao,Le Zhang,Jingyong Ye,Rui Qian,Yang Zhou,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现大型语言模型(LLMs)中同样存在心理学中的潜伏学习现象:在无奖励探索阶段,LLMs能组织任务相关知识而避免奖励驱动偏差,随后引入奖励时性能进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从认知科学角度看,强化学习对外部奖励的依赖限制了灵活性和泛化能力。心理学经典的潜伏学习现象是否能在LLMs训练中体现和应用尚未被探索。

Method: 通过多模型家族和多样化任务域的实验,采用两阶段探索机制:先进行无奖励探索阶段,随后引入奖励。同时提供了理论分析解释无奖励探索带来性能提升的机制。

Result: LLMs在无奖励探索阶段表现出性能小幅提升,引入奖励后性能进一步增强。采用这种两阶段探索机制后训练的LLMs最终表现优于全程使用奖励强化学习的模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明潜伏学习在LLMs中确实存在,提供了不同于传统强化学习的新训练视角,通过减少对奖励的依赖可能提升模型的灵活性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Latent learning, classically theorized by Tolman, shows that biological agents (e.g., rats) can acquire internal representations of their environment without rewards, enabling rapid adaptation once rewards are introduced. In contrast, from a cognitive science perspective, reward learning remains overly dependent on external feedback, limiting flexibility and generalization. Although recent advances in the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), such as OpenAI-o1 and DeepSeek-R1, mark a significant breakthrough, these models still rely primarily on reward-centric reinforcement learning paradigms. Whether and how the well-established phenomenon of latent learning in psychology can inform or emerge within LLMs' training remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present novel findings from our experiments that LLMs also exhibit the latent learning dynamics. During an initial phase of unrewarded exploration, LLMs display modest performance improvements, as this phase allows LLMs to organize task-relevant knowledge without being constrained by reward-driven biases, and performance is further enhanced once rewards are introduced. LLMs post-trained under this two-stage exploration regime ultimately achieve higher competence than those post-trained with reward-based reinforcement learning throughout. Beyond these empirical observations, we also provide theoretical analyses for our experiments explaining why unrewarded exploration yields performance gains, offering a mechanistic account of these dynamics. Specifically, we conducted extensive experiments across multiple model families and diverse task domains to establish the existence of the latent learning dynamics in LLMs.

</details>


### [68] [Continual Policy Distillation from Distributed Reinforcement Learning Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22475)
*Yuxuan Li,Qijun He,Mingqi Yuan,Wen-Tse Chen,Jeff Schneider,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) aims to develop lifelong learning agents to continuously acquire knowledge across diverse tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. This requires efficiently managing the stability-plasticity dilemma and leveraging prior experience to rapidly generalize to novel tasks. While various enhancement strategies for both aspects have been proposed, achieving scalable performance by directly applying RL to sequential task streams remains challenging. In this paper, we propose a novel teacher-student framework that decouples CRL into two independent processes: training single-task teacher models through distributed RL and continually distilling them into a central generalist model. This design is motivated by the observation that RL excels at solving single tasks, while policy distillation -- a relatively stable supervised learning process -- is well aligned with large foundation models and multi-task learning. Moreover, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture and a replay-based approach are employed to enhance the plasticity and stability of the continual policy distillation process. Extensive experiments on the Meta-World benchmark demonstrate that our framework enables efficient continual RL, recovering over 85% of teacher performance while constraining task-wise forgetting to within 10%.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

This requires efficiently managing the stability-plasticity dilemma and leveraging prior experience to rapidly generalize to novel tasks. ### [69] [Transform-Augmented GRPO Improves Pass@k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22478)
*Khiem Le,Youssef Mroueh,Phuc Nguyen,Chi-Heng Lin,Shangqian Gao,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TA-GRPO方法通过生成语义等效的问题变体并跨组池化奖励，解决了GRPO中的多样性和梯度问题，在数学推理基准上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现基于下一个词预测的大语言模型本质上是模式匹配器，对表面表述变化敏感。GRPO旨在改进推理，但反而通过多样性崩溃和梯度消失两种失效模式加剧了这一问题。

Method: 提出TA-GRPO（转换增强的GRPO），通过复述、变量重命名和格式变化为每个问题生成语义等价的变体，并通过在整个组内池化奖励来计算优势度，确保即使原问题过于简单或困难时也能获得混合奖励。

Result: 在数学推理基准上获得一致的Pass@k提升，其中竞争数学（AMC12, AIME24）上提升高达9.84分，在分布外科学推理（GPQA-Diamond）上提升5.05分。

Conclusion: TA-GRPO通过减少零梯度概率和降低训练-测试分布偏移，在理论上证明了其改进泛化能力，并在多个推理基准上验证了效果。

Abstract: Large language models trained via next-token prediction are fundamentally pattern-matchers: sensitive to superficial phrasing variations even when the underlying problem is identical. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) was designed to improve reasoning, but in fact it worsens this situation through two failure modes: diversity collapse, where training amplifies a single solution strategy while ignoring alternatives of gradient signal, and gradient diminishing, where a large portion of questions yield zero gradients because all rollouts receive identical rewards. We propose TA-GRPO (Transform-Augmented GRPO), which generates semantically equivalent transformed variants of each question (via paraphrasing, variable renaming, and format changes) and computes advantages by pooling rewards across the entire group. This pooled computation ensures mixed rewards even when the original question is too easy or too hard, while training on diverse phrasings promotes multiple solution strategies. We provide theoretical justification showing that TA-GRPO reduces zero-gradient probability and improves generalization via reduced train-test distribution shift. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show consistent Pass@k improvements, with gains up to 9.84 points on competition math (AMC12, AIME24) and 5.05 points on out-of-distribution scientific reasoning (GPQA-Diamond).

</details>


### [70] [Mitigating Cognitive Inertia in Large Reasoning Models via Latent Spike Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22484)
*Seojin Lee,ByeongJeong Kim,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: While Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have achieved remarkable performance by scaling test-time compute, they frequently suffer from Cognitive Inertia, a failure pattern manifesting as either overthinking (inertia of motion) or reasoning rigidity (inertia of direction). Existing detection methods, typically relying on superficial textual heuristics like self-correction tokens, often fail to capture the model's unvoiced internal conflicts. To address this, we propose STARS (Spike-Triggered Adaptive Reasoning Steering), a training-free framework designed to rectify cognitive inertia by monitoring latent dynamics. STARS identifies Cognitive Pivots-critical moments of reasoning transition-by detecting distinct L2 distance spikes in the hidden states. Upon detection, the framework employs geometric trajectory analysis to diagnose the structural nature of the transition and injects state-aware language cues to steer the model in real-time. Our experiments across diverse benchmarks confirm that STARS efficiently curtails redundant loops while improving accuracy through the adaptive correction of erroneous trajectories. STARS offers a robust, unsupervised mechanism to optimize the reasoning process of LRMs without requiring additional fine-tuning.

### [71] [Elastic Spectral State Space Models for Budgeted Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22488)
*Dachuan Song,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Foundation models are typically trained at a fixed computational capacity, while real-world applications require deployment across platforms with different resource constraints. Current approaches usually rely on training families of model variants or model distillation, which requires additional training and supports only a pre-selected set of sizes rather than fine-grained adaptation at runtime. In this paper, we propose Elastic Spectral State Space Models (ES-SSM), which require only one-time training at full capacity, but can be directly truncated into arbitrary scales for budgeted, runtime inference without retraining. Our ES-SSM builds on Hankel spectral filtering over a state space model (SSM), coupled with a lightweight input-adaptive gate trained under randomized spectral budgets. Using a shared masked normalization rule over the ordered spectral channels, we encourage predictive capability to concentrate in low-index components, while higher-index components act primarily as refinement. We test our algorithm across long-sequence benchmarks spanning text, logic, retrieval, vision, and audio. We demonstrate that a single ES-SSM model trained once can be truncated to provide competitive performance compared with modern Transformer and SSM baselines at similar parameter scales. Furthermore, by testing under various runtime budgets, we observe smooth and stable budget-performance curves over a wide range of truncation levels.

### [72] [Gradual Fine-Tuning for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22495)
*Gudrun Thorkelsdottir,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fine-tuning flow matching models is a central challenge in settings with limited data, evolving distributions, or strict efficiency demands, where unconstrained fine-tuning can erode the accuracy and efficiency gains learned during pretraining. Prior work has produced theoretical guarantees and empirical advances for reward-based fine-tuning formulations, but these methods often impose restrictions on permissible drift structure or training techniques. In this work, we propose Gradual Fine-Tuning (GFT), a principled framework for fine-tuning flow-based generative models when samples from the target distribution are available. For stochastic flows, GFT defines a temperature-controlled sequence of intermediate objectives that smoothly interpolate between the pretrained and target drifts, approaching the true target as the temperature approaches zero. We prove convergence results for both marginal and conditional GFT objectives, enabling the use of suitable (e.g., optimal transport) couplings during GFT while preserving correctness. Empirically, GFT improves convergence stability and shortens probability paths, resulting in faster inference, while maintaining generation quality comparable to standard fine-tuning. Our results position GFT as a theoretically grounded and practically effective alternative for scalable adaptation of flow matching models under distribution shift.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [73] [Action-Sufficient Goal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22496)
*Jinu Hyeon,Woobin Park,Hongjoon Ahn,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离线分层目标条件强化学习中的目标表示问题，提出动作充分性概念，揭示了传统基于价值充分性的目标表示的缺陷，并证明通过演员训练自然诱导的动作充分表示优于基于价值学习的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层策略在离线目标条件强化学习中通过高层子目标规划和低层动作执行来解决长视野任务。现有方法通常在学习价值函数时推导目标表示，隐含假设保留价值估计足够的信息即可实现最优控制。然而，本文作者发现即使价值估计精准，此类表示可能会坍缩对于动作学习必须区分的不同目标状态，从而导致控制失败。

Method: 引入信息论框架定义了动作充分性——这是最优动作选择所必需的目标表示条件。理论上证明了价值充分性不蕴含动作充分性，并在离散环境中经验验证动作充分性与控制成功的关联更强。进一步展示标准对数损失训练的低层策略自然诱导动作充分表示。

Result: 实验结果显示，在流行的基准测试中，通过演员训练推导的目标表示一致优于基于价值学习获得的表示。

Conclusion: 在离线分层GCRL中，为低层策略学习设计的目标表示应满足动作充分性而非仅仅价值充分性。通过演员训练自然诱导的动作充分表示相较于传统基于价值学习的表示能更有效地支持最优控制。该研究为分层强化学习的表示学习提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: Hierarchical policies in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) addresses long-horizon tasks by decomposing control into high-level subgoal planning and low-level action execution. A critical design choice in such architectures is the goal representation-the compressed encoding of goals that serves as the interface between these levels. Existing approaches commonly derive goal representations while learning value functions, implicitly assuming that preserving information sufficient for value estimation is adequate for optimal control. We show that this assumption can fail, even when the value estimation is exact, as such representations may collapse goal states that need to be differentiated for action learning. To address this, we introduce an information-theoretic framework that defines action sufficiency, a condition on goal representations necessary for optimal action selection. We prove that value sufficiency does not imply action sufficiency and empirically verify that the latter is more strongly associated with control success in a discrete environment. We further demonstrate that standard log-loss training of low-level policies naturally induces action-sufficient representations. Our experimental results a popular benchmark demonstrate that our actor-derived representations consistently outperform representations learned via value estimation.

</details>


### [74] [Keep Rehearsing and Refining: Lifelong Learning Vehicle Routing under Continually Drifting Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22509)
*Jiyuan Pei,Yi Mei,Jialin Liu,Mengjie Zhang,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Existing neural solvers for vehicle routing problems (VRPs) are typically trained either in a one-off manner on a fixed set of pre-defined tasks or in a lifelong manner on several tasks arriving sequentially, assuming sufficient training on each task. Both settings overlook a common real-world property: problem patterns may drift continually over time, yielding massive tasks sequentially arising while offering only limited training resources per task. In this paper, we study a novel lifelong learning paradigm for neural VRP solvers under continually drifting tasks over learning time steps, where sufficient training for any given task at any time is not available. We propose Dual Replay with Experience Enhancement (DREE), a general framework to improve learning efficiency and mitigate catastrophic forgetting under such drift. Extensive experiments show that, under such continual drift, DREE effectively learns new tasks, preserves prior knowledge, improves generalization to unseen tasks, and can be applied to diverse existing neural solvers.

In this paper, we study a novel lifelong learning paradigm for neural VRP solvers under continually drifting tasks over learning time steps, where sufficient training for any given task at any time is not available. We propose Dual Replay with Experience Enhancement (DREE), a general framework to improve learning efficiency and mitigate catastrophic forgetting under such drift. ### [75] [Shattered Compositionality: Counterintuitive Learning Dynamics of Transformers for Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22510)
*Xingyu Zhao,Darsh Sharma,Rheeya Uppaal,Yiqiao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit unexpected errors or unintended behavior, even at scale. While recent work reveals the discrepancy between LLMs and humans in skill compositions, the learning dynamics of skill compositions and the underlying cause of non-human behavior remain elusive. In this study, we investigate the mechanism of learning dynamics by training transformers on synthetic arithmetic tasks. Through extensive ablations and fine-grained diagnostic metrics, we discover that transformers do not reliably build skill compositions according to human-like sequential rules. Instead, they often acquire skills in reverse order or in parallel, which leads to unexpected mixing errors especially under distribution shifts--a phenomenon we refer to as shattered compositionality. To explain these behaviors, we provide evidence that correlational matching to the training data, rather than causal or procedural composition, shapes learning dynamics. We further show that shattered compositionality persists in modern LLMs and is not mitigated by pure model scaling or scratchpad-based reasoning. Our results reveal a fundamental mismatch between a model's learning behavior and desired skill compositions, with implications for reasoning reliability, out-of-distribution robustness, and alignment.

### [76] [DRL-Enabled Trajectory Planing for UAV-Assisted VLC: Optimal Altitude and Reward Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22512)
*Tian-Tian Lin,Yi Liu,Xiao-Wei Tang,Yunmei Shi,Yi Huang,Zhongxiang Wei,Qingqing Wu,Yuhan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于无人机辅助可见光通信系统的三维轨迹规划框架，旨在通过优化无人机飞行高度和水平轨迹最小化飞行距离，从而提高数据采集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机与可见光通信技术的结合为提供灵活通信和高效照明带来了新的可能。该研究旨在解决无人机协助地面用户数据传输时的轨迹规划问题，通过最小化飞行距离来提高数据收集效率，为复杂环境下的自适应无人机运动策略提供解决方案。

Method: 首先推导了在特定可见光通信信道增益阈值下的闭式最优飞行高度表达式；随后提出了一种融合新型信息素驱动奖励机制与双延迟深度确定性策略梯度算法的水平轨迹优化方法，实现了复杂环境中的自适应无人机运动策略。

Result: 仿真结果表明，推导的最优飞行高度相比基线方法最多可减少35%的飞行距离；提出的奖励机制将收敛步数缩短约50%，在无人机辅助可见光通信数据收集场景中显示出显著的效率提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一个高效的三维轨迹规划框架，通过结合最优高度推导和创新奖励机制，显著提升了无人机辅助可见光通信系统的数据收集效率，为解决混合整数非凸优化问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recently, the integration of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and visible light communication (VLC) technologies has emerged as a promising solution to offer flexible communication and efficient lighting. This letter investigates the three-dimensional trajectory planning in a UAV-assisted VLC system, where a UAV is dispatched to collect data from ground users (GUs). The core objective is to develop a trajectory planning framework that minimizes UAV flight distance, which is equivalent to maximizing the data collection efficiency. This issue is formulated as a challenging mixed-integer non-convex optimization problem. To tackle it, we first derive a closed-form optimal flight altitude under specific VLC channel gain threshold. Subsequently, we optimize the UAV horizontal trajectory by integrating a novel pheromone-driven reward mechanism with the twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm, which enables adaptive UAV motion strategy in complex environments. Simulation results validate that the derived optimal altitude effectively reduces the flight distance by up to 35% compared to baseline methods. Additionally, the proposed reward mechanism significantly shortens the convergence steps by approximately 50%, demonstrating notable efficiency gains in the context of UAV-assisted VLC data collection.

</details>


### [77] [SCOPE-PD: Explainable AI on Subjective and Clinical Objective Measurements of Parkinson's Disease for Precision Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22516)
*Md Mezbahul Islam,John Michael Templeton,Masrur Sobhan,Christian Poellabauer,Ananda Mohan Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于可解释AI的SCOPE-PD框架整合主观与客观评估进行帕金森病预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病（PD）的诊断通常依赖主观的传统方法导致诊断延迟，现有机器学习方法多依赖主观报告且缺乏个体化风险估计的可解释性，需要整合主客观数据的可解释预测框架

Method: 提出SCOPE-PD可解释AI预测框架，使用PPMI研究的主客观临床评估数据构建多模态预测模型，应用多种ML技术并选择最佳模型，通过SHAP分析进行模型解释

Result: 随机森林算法在使用主客观测试数据组合特征时达到98.66%的最高准确率，从MDS-UPDRS测试中识别出震颤、运动迟缓和面部表情是预测PD的三大最重要特征

Conclusion: SCOPE-PD框架通过整合主客观数据实现了高精度和可解释的PD预测，为个性化医疗决策提供了实用工具

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is a chronic and complex neurodegenerative disorder influenced by genetic, clinical, and lifestyle factors. Predicting this disease early is challenging because it depends on traditional diagnostic methods that face issues of subjectivity, which commonly delay diagnosis. Several objective analyses are currently in practice to help overcome the challenges of subjectivity; however, a proper explanation of these analyses is still lacking. While machine learning (ML) has demonstrated potential in supporting PD diagnosis, existing approaches often rely on subjective reports only and lack interpretability for individualized risk estimation. This study proposes SCOPE-PD, an explainable AI-based prediction framework, by integrating subjective and objective assessments to provide personalized health decisions. Subjective and objective clinical assessment data are collected from the Parkinson's Progression Markers Initiative (PPMI) study to construct a multimodal prediction framework. Several ML techniques are applied to these data, and the best ML model is selected to interpret the results. Model interpretability is examined using SHAP-based analysis. The Random Forest algorithm achieves the highest accuracy of 98.66 percent using combined features from both subjective and objective test data. Tremor, bradykinesia, and facial expression are identified as the top three contributing features from the MDS-UPDRS test in the prediction of PD.

</details>


### [78] [Variational Bayesian Flow Network for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22524)
*Yida Xiong,Jiameng Chen,Xiuwen Gong,Jia Wu,Shirui Pan,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graph generation aims to sample discrete node and edge attributes while satisfying coupled structural constraints. Diffusion models for graphs often adopt largely factorized forward-noising, and many flow-matching methods start from factorized reference noise and coordinate-wise interpolation, so node-edge coupling is not encoded by the generative geometry and must be recovered implicitly by the core network, which can be brittle after discrete decoding. Bayesian Flow Networks (BFNs) evolve distribution parameters and naturally support discrete generation. But classical BFNs typically rely on factorized beliefs and independent channels, which limit geometric evidence fusion. We propose Variational Bayesian Flow Network (VBFN), which performs a variational lifting to a tractable joint Gaussian variational belief family governed by structured precisions. Each Bayesian update reduces to solving a symmetric positive definite linear system, enabling coupled node and edge updates within a single fusion step. We construct sample-agnostic sparse precisions from a representation-induced dependency graph, thereby avoiding label leakage while enforcing node-edge consistency. On synthetic and molecular graph datasets, VBFN improves fidelity and diversity, and surpasses baseline methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Diffusion models for graphs often adopt largely factorized forward-noising, and many flow-matching methods start from factorized reference noise and coordinate-wise interpolation, so node-edge coupling is not encoded by the generative geometry and must be recovered implicitly by the core network, which can be brittle after discrete decoding. Bayesian Flow Networks (BFNs) evolve distribution parameters and naturally support discrete generation. But classical BFNs typically rely on factorized beliefs and independent channels, which limit geometric evidence fusion. We propose Variational Bayesian Flow Network (VBFN), which performs a variational lifting to a tractable joint Gaussian variational belief family governed by structured precisions. ### [79] [Learn from A Rationalist: Distilling Intermediate Interpretable Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22531)
*Jiayi Dai,Randy Goebel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Because of the pervasive use of deep neural networks (DNNs), especially in high-stakes domains, the interpretability of DNNs has received increased attention. The general idea of rationale extraction (RE) is to provide an interpretable-by-design framework for DNNs via a select-predict architecture where two neural networks learn jointly to perform feature selection and prediction, respectively. Given only the remote supervision from the final task prediction, the process of learning to select subsets of features (or \emph{rationales}) requires searching in the space of all possible feature combinations, which is computationally challenging and even harder when the base neural networks are not sufficiently capable. To improve the predictive performance of RE models that are based on less capable or smaller neural networks (i.e., the students), we propose \textbf{REKD} (\textbf{R}ationale \textbf{E}xtraction with \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{D}istillation) where a student RE model learns from the rationales and predictions of a teacher (i.e., a \emph{rationalist}) in addition to the student's own RE optimization. This structural adjustment to RE aligns well with how humans could learn effectively from interpretable and verifiable knowledge. Because of the neural-model agnostic nature of the method, any black-box neural network could be integrated as a backbone model. To demonstrate the viability of REKD, we conduct experiments with multiple variants of BERT and vision transformer (ViT) models. Our experiments across language and vision classification datasets (i.e., IMDB movie reviews, CIFAR 10 and CIFAR 100) show that REKD significantly improves the predictive performance of the student RE models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.
