### [161] [Causal Characterization of Measurement and Mechanistic Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23026)
*Hendrik Suhr,David Kaltenpoth,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Root cause analysis of anomalies aims to identify those features that cause the deviation from the normal process. Existing methods ignore, however, that anomalies can arise through two fundamentally different processes: measurement errors, where data was generated normally but one or more values were recorded incorrectly, and mechanism shifts, where the causal process generating the data changed. While measurement errors can often be safely corrected, mechanistic anomalies require careful consideration. We define a causal model that explicitly captures both types by treating outliers as latent interventions on latent ("true") and observed ("measured") variables. We show that they are identifiable, and propose a maximum likelihood estimation approach to put this to practice. Experiments show that our method matches state-of-the-art performance in root cause localization, while it additionally enables accurate classification of anomaly types, and remains robust even when the causal DAG is unknown.

### [162] [Divide-and-Conquer CoT: RL for Reducing Latency via Parallel Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23027)
*Arvind Mahankali,Kaiyue Wen,Tengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DC-CoT方法，通过让模型识别并行子任务并分配worker执行，来降低长思维链推理的延迟，在保持精度的同时将最长路径长度减少35-40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长思维链推理导致LLM生成延迟高，现有方法具有高序列化特性，需要降低推理延迟。

Method: 使用DeepScaleR-1.5B-Preview作为基础模型，先进行有监督微调（SFT）学习生成worker的格式，再设计多阶段强化学习算法配合数据过滤策略恢复精度并降低最长路径长度。

Result: 在AIME 2024和HMMT 2025等基准测试中，DC-CoT保持了与基础模型相似的精度，同时将最长路径长度减少了35-40%。

Conclusion: DC-CoT能有效降低长思维链推理的延迟，在多个数学推理基准上实现高精度和低延迟的平衡。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning (Long CoT) is now fundamental to state-of-the-art LLMs, especially in mathematical reasoning. However, LLM generation is highly sequential, and long CoTs lead to a high latency. We propose to train Divide-and-Conquer CoT (DC-CoT) to reduce the latency. With DC-CoT, the model can act as a director that identifies distinct subtasks that can be performed in parallel in its reasoning process, and then spawns workers to execute the subtasks. Our goal is to achieve high accuracy, with a low longest path length, which is a theoretical measure of the latency needed for the response. We start with a long CoT base model (DeepScaleR-1.5B-Preview), and first use SFT with a small curated demonstration set to initialize its ability to spawn workers in a certain format. Because SFT degrades the accuracy significantly, we design a multi-stage RL algorithm, with various data filtering strategies, to recover the accuracy while decreasing the longest path length. Across several benchmarks including AIME 2024 and HMMT 2025, DC-CoT achieves similar accuracy as DeepScaleR-1.5B-Preview while decreasing longest path length by 35-40%. Our code, SFT dataset and models are publicly available at https://github.com/amahankali10/DC_CoT_RL_for_Low_Latency_CoT_with_Parallel_Reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [From Absolute to Relative: Rethinking Reward Shaping in Group-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23058)
*Wenzhe Niu,Wei He,Zongxia Xie,Jinpeng Ou,Huichuan Fan,Yuchen Ge,Yanru Sun,Ziyin Wang,Yizhao Sun,Chengshun Shi,Jiuchong Gao,Jinghua Hao,Renqing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models, where group-based approaches such as GRPO have emerged as efficient paradigms that optimize policies by leveraging intra-group performance differences. However, these methods typically rely on absolute numerical rewards, introducing intrinsic limitations. In verifiable tasks, identical group evaluations often result in sparse supervision, while in open-ended scenarios, the score range instability of reward models undermines advantage estimation based on group means. To address these limitations, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Relative Rewards (RLRR), a framework that shifts reward shaping from absolute scoring to relative ranking. Complementing this framework, we introduce the Ranking Reward Model, a listwise preference model tailored for group-based optimization to directly generate relative rankings. By transforming raw evaluations into robust relative signals, RLRR effectively mitigates signal sparsity and reward instability. Experimental results demonstrate that RLRR yields consistent performance improvements over standard group-based baselines across reasoning benchmarks and open-ended generation tasks.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [164] [SplineFlow: Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems with B-Spline Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23072)
*Santanu Subhash Rathod,Pietro Liò,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SplineFlow使用B样条插值的流匹配算法改进动态系统建模，在各类确定性和随机系统中表现出色


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流匹配方法不适用于动态系统建模，线性插值不能捕获复杂状态演化，尤其是从非规则采样数据中学习高阶动态

Method: 提出SplineFlow算法，利用B样条插值构建满足多边界条件的联合条件路径，平衡平滑性和稳定性

Result: 在多种复杂动态系统和细胞轨迹推断任务上实验，SplineFlow显著优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: SplineFlow为动态系统提供了一种理论完备、性能优越的生成建模框架，可捕获复杂动态过程

Abstract: Flow matching is a scalable generative framework for characterizing continuous normalizing flows with wide-range applications. However, current state-of-the-art methods are not well-suited for modeling dynamical systems, as they construct conditional paths using linear interpolants that may not capture the underlying state evolution, especially when learning higher-order dynamics from irregular sampled observations. Constructing unified paths that satisfy multi-marginal constraints across observations is challenging, since naïve higher-order polynomials tend to be unstable and oscillatory. We introduce SplineFlow, a theoretically grounded flow matching algorithm that jointly models conditional paths across observations via B-spline interpolation. Specifically, SplineFlow exploits the smoothness and stability of B-spline bases to learn the complex underlying dynamics in a structured manner while ensuring the multi-marginal requirements are met. Comprehensive experiments across various deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems of varying complexity, as well as on cellular trajectory inference tasks, demonstrate the strong improvement of SplineFlow over existing baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/santanurathod/SplineFlow.

</details>


### [165] [RN-D: Discretized Categorical Actors with Regularized Networks for On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23075)
*Yuexin Bian,Jie Feng,Tao Wang,Yijiang Li,Sicun Gao,Yuanyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: On-policy deep reinforcement learning remains a dominant paradigm for continuous control, yet standard implementations rely on Gaussian actors and relatively shallow MLP policies, often leading to brittle optimization when gradients are noisy and policy updates must be conservative. In this paper, we revisit policy representation as a first-class design choice for on-policy optimization. We study discretized categorical actors that represent each action dimension with a distribution over bins, yielding a policy objective that resembles a cross-entropy loss. Building on architectural advances from supervised learning, we further propose regularized actor networks, while keeping critic design fixed. Our results show that simply replacing the standard actor network with our discretized regularized actor yields consistent gains and achieve the state-of-the-art performance across diverse continuous-control benchmarks.

### [166] [CATTO: Balancing Preferences and Confidence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23096)
*Nisarg Parikh,Kunjal Panchal,Ananya Sai,Pannaga Shivaswamy,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Large language models (LLMs) often make accurate next token predictions but their confidence in these predictions can be poorly calibrated: high-confidence predictions are frequently wrong, and low-confidence predictions may be correct. This miscalibration is exacerbated by preference-based alignment methods breaking the link between predictive probability and correctness. We introduce a Calibration Aware Token-level Training Objective (CATTO), a calibration-aware objective that aligns predicted confidence with empirical prediction correctness, which can be combined with the original preference optimization objectives. Empirically, CATTO reduces Expected Calibration Error (ECE) by 2.22%-7.61% in-distribution and 1.46%-10.44% out-of-distribution compared to direct preference optimization (DPO), and by 0.22%-1.24% in-distribution and 1.23%-5.07% out-of-distribution compared to the strongest DPO baseline. This improvement in confidence does not come at a cost of losing task accuracy, where CATTO maintains or slightly improves multiple-choice question-answering accuracy on five datasets. We also introduce Confidence@k, a test-time scaling mechanism leveraging calibrated token probabilities for Bayes-optimal selection of output tokens.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

This improvement in confidence does not come at a cost of losing task accuracy, where CATTO maintains or slightly improves multiple-choice question-answering accuracy on five datasets. We also introduce Confidence@k, a test-time scaling mechanism leveraging calibrated token probabilities for Bayes-optimal selection of output tokens.

</details>


### [167] [Distribution-informed Efficient Conformal Prediction for Full Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23128)
*Wenbo Liao,Huipeng Huang,Chen Jia,Huajun Xi,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Quantifying uncertainty is critical for the safe deployment of ranking models in real-world applications. Recent work offers a rigorous solution using conformal prediction in a full ranking scenario, which aims to construct prediction sets for the absolute ranks of test items based on the relative ranks of calibration items. However, relying on upper bounds of non-conformity scores renders the method overly conservative, resulting in substantially large prediction sets. To address this, we propose Distribution-informed Conformal Ranking (DCR), which produces efficient prediction sets by deriving the exact distribution of non-conformity scores. In particular, we find that the absolute ranks of calibration items follow Negative Hypergeometric distributions, conditional on their relative ranks. DCR thus uses the rank distribution to derive non-conformity score distribution and determine conformal thresholds. We provide theoretical guarantees that DCR achieves improved efficiency over the baseline while ensuring valid coverage under mild assumptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of DCR, reducing average prediction set size by up to 36%, while maintaining valid coverage.

### [168] [Why GRPO Needs Normalization: A Local-Curvature Perspective on Adaptive Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23135)
*Cheng Ge,Caitlyn Heqi Yin,Hao Liang,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key driver of language model reasoning. Among RL algorithms, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the de facto standard, avoiding the need for a critic by using per-prompt baselines and variance normalization. Yet why and when this normalization helps remains unclear. In this work, we provide an explanation through the lens of local curvature of the sequence-level policy gradient: standard deviation normalization implements an adaptive gradient. Theoretically, under mild conditions, GRPO enjoys a strictly improved convergence rate over unnormalized REINFORCE, with gains characterized by the average within-prompt reward standard deviation across prompts and iterations. Empirically, our analysis on GSM8K and MATH benchmarks reveals three distinct training phases governed by the interplay between feature orthogonality and reward variance: (I) an early acceleration phase where high variance and orthogonality favor adaptive scaling; (II) a relatively stable transition phase; and (III) a late-stage regime where the loss of orthogonality limits further gains. Together, these results provide a principled account of when std normalization helps in GRPO, and offer broader insights into the design of critic-free RL algorithms.

### [169] [Manifold-Aware Perturbations for Constrained Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23151)
*Katherine Keegan,Lars Ruthotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Generative models have enjoyed widespread success in a variety of applications. However, they encounter inherent mathematical limitations in modeling distributions where samples are constrained by equalities, as is frequently the setting in scientific domains. In this work, we develop a computationally cheap, mathematically justified, and highly flexible distributional modification for combating known pitfalls in equality-constrained generative models. We propose perturbing the data distribution in a constraint-aware way such that the new distribution has support matching the ambient space dimension while still implicitly incorporating underlying manifold geometry. Through theoretical analyses and empirical evidence on several representative tasks, we illustrate that our approach consistently enables data distribution recovery and stable sampling with both diffusion models and normalizing flows.

### [170] [Behemoth: Benchmarking Unlearning in LLMs Using Fully Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23153)
*Eugenia Iofinova,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: As artificial neural networks, and specifically large language models, have improved rapidly in capabilities and quality, they have increasingly been deployed in real-world applications, from customer service to Google search, despite the fact that they frequently make factually incorrect or undesirable statements. This trend has inspired practical and academic interest in model editing, that is, in adjusting the weights of the model to modify its likely outputs for queries relating to a specific fact or set of facts. This may be done either to amend a fact or set of facts, for instance, to fix a frequent error in the training data, or to suppress a fact or set of facts entirely, for instance, in case of dangerous knowledge. Multiple methods have been proposed to do such edits. However, at the same time, it has been shown that such model editing can be brittle and incomplete. Moreover the effectiveness of any model editing method necessarily depends on the data on which the model is trained, and, therefore, a good understanding of the interaction of the training data distribution and the way it is stored in the network is necessary and helpful to reliably perform model editing. However, working with large language models trained on real-world data does not allow us to understand this relationship or fully measure the effects of model editing. We therefore propose Behemoth, a fully synthetic data generation framework. To demonstrate the practical insights from the framework, we explore model editing in the context of simple tabular data, demonstrating surprising findings that, in some cases, echo real-world results, for instance, that in some cases restricting the update rank results in a more effective update. The code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/behemoth.git.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: As artificial neural networks, and specifically large language models, have improved rapidly in capabilities and quality, they have increasingly been deployed in real-world applications, from customer service to Google search, despite the fact that they frequently make factually incorrect or undesirable statements. This trend has inspired practical and academic interest in model editing, that is, in adjusting the weights of the model to modify its likely outputs for queries relating to a specific fact or set of facts. This may be done either to amend a fact or set of facts, for instance, to fix a frequent error in the training data, or to suppress a fact or set of facts entirely, for instance, in case of dangerous knowledge. Multiple methods have been proposed to do such edits. However, at the same time, it has been shown that such model editing can be brittle and incomplete. Moreover the effectiveness of any model editing method necessarily depends on the data on which the model is trained, and, therefore, a good understanding of the interaction of the training data distribution and the way it is stored in the network is necessary and helpful to reliably perform model editing. However, working with large language models trained on real-world data does not allow us to understand this relationship or fully measure the effects of model editing. We therefore propose Behemoth, a fully synthetic data generation framework. To demonstrate the practical insights from the framework, we explore model editing in the context of simple tabular data, demonstrating surprising findings that, in some cases, echo real-world results, for instance, that in some cases restricting the update rank results in a more effective update. The code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/behemoth.git.

</details>


### [171] [Unsupervised Hierarchical Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23156)
*Damion Harvey,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Branden Ingram,Benjamin Rosman,Steven James*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于语法的方法，用于无监督技能分割与分层结构发现，能够从无标注轨迹中提取可重用技能并构建分层结构，在像素级环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能分割方法大多依赖动作标签、奖励信号或人工标注，限制了其通用性。本文旨在开发一种无需这些先验信息的无监督方法，直接从原始轨迹中学习技能分层结构。

Method: 采用基于语法的方法，对未标记轨迹进行自动分割，识别可重用技能单元，并在此基础上构建分层结构。该方法能够捕获低级行为及其组合成高级技能的层次关系。

Result: 在Craftax和完整版Minecraft等高维像素环境中评估表明，该方法在技能分割质量、重用性和分层结构合理性方面优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督方法能够发现结构化且语义明确的技能分层结构，这些结构在下游强化学习任务中能有效加速和稳定学习过程。

Abstract: We consider the problem of unsupervised skill segmentation and hierarchical structure discovery in reinforcement learning. While recent approaches have sought to segment trajectories into reusable skills or options, most rely on action labels, rewards, or handcrafted annotations, limiting their applicability. We propose a method that segments unlabelled trajectories into skills and induces a hierarchical structure over them using a grammar-based approach. The resulting hierarchy captures both low-level behaviours and their composition into higher-level skills. We evaluate our approach in high-dimensional, pixel-based environments, including Craftax and the full, unmodified version of Minecraft. Using metrics for skill segmentation, reuse, and hierarchy quality, we find that our method consistently produces more structured and semantically meaningful hierarchies than existing baselines. Furthermore, as a proof of concept for utility, we demonstrate that these discovered hierarchies accelerate and stabilise learning on downstream reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [172] [Stochastic Linear Bandits with Parameter Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23164)
*Daniel Ezer,Alon Peled-Cohen,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We study the stochastic linear bandits with parameter noise model, in which the reward of action $a$ is $a^\top θ$ where $θ$ is sampled i.i.d. We show a regret upper bound of $\widetilde{O} (\sqrt{d T \log (K/δ) σ^2_{\max})}$ for a horizon $T$, general action set of size $K$ of dimension $d$, and where $σ^2_{\max}$ is the maximal variance of the reward for any action. We further provide a lower bound of $\widetildeΩ (d \sqrt{T σ^2_{\max}})$ which is tight (up to logarithmic factors) whenever $\log (K) \approx d$. For more specific action sets, $\ell_p$ unit balls with $p \leq 2$ and dual norm $q$, we show that the minimax regret is $\widetildeΘ (\sqrt{dT σ^2_q)}$, where $σ^2_q$ is a variance-dependent quantity that is always at most $4$. This is in contrast to the minimax regret attainable for such sets in the classic additive noise model, where the regret is of order $d \sqrt{T}$. Surprisingly, we show that this optimal (up to logarithmic factors) regret bound is attainable using a very simple explore-exploit algorithm.

We further provide a lower bound of $\widetildeΩ (d \sqrt{T σ^2_{\max}})$ which is tight (up to logarithmic factors) whenever $\log (K) \approx d$. For more specific action sets, $\ell_p$ unit balls with $p \leq 2$ and dual norm $q$, we show that the minimax regret is $\widetildeΘ (\sqrt{dT σ^2_q)}$, where $σ^2_q$ is a variance-dependent quantity that is always at most $4$. This is in contrast to the minimax regret attainable for such sets in the classic additive noise model, where the regret is of order $d \sqrt{T}$. Surprisingly, we show that this optimal (up to logarithmic factors) regret bound is attainable using a very simple explore-exploit algorithm.

### [173] [Names Don't Matter: Symbol-Invariant Transformer for Open-Vocabulary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23169)
*İlker Işık,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Current neural architectures lack a principled way to handle interchangeable tokens, i.e., symbols that are semantically equivalent yet distinguishable, such as bound variables. As a result, models trained on fixed vocabularies often struggle to generalize to unseen symbols, even when the underlying semantics remain unchanged. We propose a novel Transformer-based mechanism that is provably invariant to the renaming of interchangeable tokens. Our approach employs parallel embedding streams to isolate the contribution of each interchangeable token in the input, combined with an aggregated attention mechanism that enables structured information sharing across streams. Experimental results confirm the theoretical guarantees of our method and demonstrate substantial performance gains on open-vocabulary tasks that require generalization to novel symbols.

### [174] [MeshGraphNet-Transformer: Scalable Mesh-based Learned Simulation for Solid Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23177)
*Mikel M. Iparraguirre,Iciar Alfaro,David Gonzalez,Elias Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MeshGraphNet-Transformer (MGN-T) 结合了 Transformer 的全局建模能力与 MeshGraphNets 的几何归纳偏置，通过物理注意力机制有效解决了传统 MGN 在大型高分辨率网格上长距离信息传播效率低下的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MeshGraphNets (MGN) 在大型高分辨率网格上存在局限性：基于迭代消息传递的方法导致长距离信息传播效率低下，需要深度消息传递堆栈或分层粗化网格，这在实际工业规模应用中不够高效。

Method: MGN-T 采用物理注意力 Transformer 作为全局处理器，同时更新所有节点状态并显式保留节点和边属性。它直接捕获长距离物理相互作用，无需深度消息传递堆栈或分层粗化网格，支持不同几何形状、拓扑和边界条件的高分辨率网格。

Result: MGN-T 成功处理了工业规模的冲击动力学网格（传统 MGN 因消息传递不足而失败），准确模拟了自接触、塑性和多变量输出（包括内部现象学塑性变量）。在经典基准测试中超越现有方法，精度更高且保持实用效率，参数数量仅为竞争基线的一小部分。

Conclusion: MGN-T 通过将 Transformer 的全局建模与 MeshGraphNets 的几何归纳偏置相结合，有效解决了大规模高分辨率网格物理模拟中的长距离交互问题，为工业级应用提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MeshGraphNet-Transformer (MGN-T), a novel architecture that combines the global modeling capabilities of Transformers with the geometric inductive bias of MeshGraphNets, while preserving a mesh-based graph representation. MGN-T overcomes a key limitation of standard MGN, the inefficient long-range information propagation caused by iterative message passing on large, high-resolution meshes. A physics-attention Transformer serves as a global processor, updating all nodal states simultaneously while explicitly retaining node and edge attributes. By directly capturing long-range physical interactions, MGN-T eliminates the need for deep message-passing stacks or hierarchical, coarsened meshes, enabling efficient learning on high-resolution meshes with varying geometries, topologies, and boundary conditions at an industrial scale.
  We demonstrate that MGN-T successfully handles industrial-scale meshes for impact dynamics, a setting in which standard MGN fails due message-passing under-reaching. The method accurately models self-contact, plasticity, and multivariate outputs, including internal, phenomenological plastic variables. Moreover, MGN-T outperforms state-of-the-art approaches on classical benchmarks, achieving higher accuracy while maintaining practical efficiency, using only a fraction of the parameters required by competing baselines.

</details>


### [175] [TriSpec: Ternary Speculative Decoding via Lightweight Proxy Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23180)
*Haoyun Jiang,Junqi He,Feng Hong,Xinlong Yang,Jianwei Zhang,Zheng Li,Zhengyang Zhuge,Zhiyong Chen,Bo Han,Junyang Lin,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Inference efficiency in Large Language Models (LLMs) is fundamentally limited by their serial, autoregressive generation, especially as reasoning becomes a key capability and response sequences grow longer. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a powerful solution, providing significant speed-ups through its lightweight drafting and parallel verification mechanism. While existing work has nearly saturated improvements in draft effectiveness and efficiency, this paper advances SD from a new yet critical perspective: the verification cost. We propose TriSpec, a novel ternary SD framework that, at its core, introduces a lightweight proxy to significantly reduce computational cost by approving easily verifiable draft sequences and engaging the full target model only when encountering uncertain tokens. TriSpec can be integrated with state-of-the-art SD methods like EAGLE-3 to further reduce verification costs, achieving greater acceleration. Extensive experiments on the Qwen3 and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen/LLaMA families show that TriSpec achieves up to 35\% speedup over standard SD, with up to 50\% fewer target model invocations while maintaining comparable accuracy.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [176] [Ensuring Semantics in Weights of Implicit Neural Representations through the Implicit Function Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23181)
*Tianming Qiu,Christos Sonis,Hao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weight Space Learning (WSL), which frames neural network weights as a data modality, is an emerging field with potential for tasks like meta-learning or transfer learning. Particularly, Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) provide a convenient testbed, where each set of weights determines the corresponding individual data sample as a mapping from coordinates to contextual values. So far, a precise theoretical explanation for the mechanism of encoding semantics of data into network weights is still missing. In this work, we deploy the Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) to establish a rigorous mapping between the data space and its latent weight representation space. We analyze a framework that maps instance-specific embeddings to INR weights via a shared hypernetwork, achieving performance competitive with existing baselines on downstream classification tasks across 2D and 3D datasets. These findings offer a theoretical lens for future investigations into network weights.

Particularly, Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) provide a convenient testbed, where each set of weights determines the corresponding individual data sample as a mapping from coordinates to contextual values. So far, a precise theoretical explanation for the mechanism of encoding semantics of data into network weights is still missing. We analyze a framework that maps instance-specific embeddings to INR weights via a shared hypernetwork, achieving performance competitive with existing baselines on downstream classification tasks across 2D and 3D datasets. ### [177] [Tackling air quality with SAPIENS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23215)
*Marcella Bona,Nathan Heatley,Jia-Chen Hua,Adriana Lara,Valeria Legaria-Santiago,Alberto Luviano Juarez,Fernando Moreno-Gomez,Jocelyn Richardson,Natan Vilchis,Xiwen Shirley Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 墨西哥城空气污染与交通强度的关系研究：通过将彩色交通地图转换为环状交通强度描述，结合PLS回归预测污染物浓度，提供超本地化动态空气质量预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球大城市空气污染问题日益严重，交通是空气质量恶化主要因素。现有空气质量测量预报时空分辨率不足，而交通数据普遍精细开放。本研究旨在结合污染传感器与交通数据，分析二者关系以实现超本地化动态空气质量预报。

Method: 提出创新方法将彩色编码交通地图转换为基于同心环的交通强度描述，优化交通条件表征。采用偏最小二乘回归基于新定义交通强度预测污染物水平，通过多训练样本优化模型性能。

Result: 建立了交通强度与空气质量的预测模型，获得最佳预测性能，深入揭示了污染物与交通之间的关系。开发的工作流程简单易行，可适用于其他城市情境。

Conclusion: 研究证明结合精细交通数据可有效预测污染物浓度，提供超本地化动态空气质量预报。创新交通表征方法和可扩展工作流程为其他城市空气质量管理提供实用框架。

Abstract: Air pollution is a chronic problem in large cities worldwide and awareness is rising as the long-term health implications become clearer. Vehicular traffic has been identified as a major contributor to poor air quality. In a lot of cities the publicly available air quality measurements and forecasts are coarse-grained both in space and time. However, in general, real-time traffic intensity data is openly available in various forms and is fine-grained. In this paper, we present an in-depth study of pollution sensor measurements combined with traffic data from Mexico City. We analyse and model the relationship between traffic intensity and air quality with the aim to provide hyper-local, dynamic air quality forecasts. We developed an innovative method to represent traffic intensities by transforming simple colour-coded traffic maps into concentric ring-based descriptions, enabling improved characterisation of traffic conditions. Using Partial Least Squares Regression, we predict pollution levels based on these newly defined traffic intensities. The model was optimised with various training samples to achieve the best predictive performance and gain insights into the relationship between pollutants and traffic. The workflow we have designed is straightforward and adaptable to other contexts, like other cities beyond the specifics of our dataset.

</details>


### [178] [Optimal Fair Aggregation of Crowdsourced Noisy Labels using Demographic Parity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23221)
*Gabriel Singer,Samuel Gruffaz,Olivier Vo Van,Nicolas Vayatis,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统分析了众包标注聚合方法在ε公平性框架下的表现，为多数投票和最优贝叶斯聚合提供了公平性收敛理论保证，并将多类别公平后处理算法从连续域扩展到离散域以强制执行人口统计均等约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取可靠的ground-truth标签通常成本高昂或不可行，导致众包标注成为常用替代方案。但聚合主观标注可能会放大个体偏见，特别是在敏感特征方面引发公平性担忧。当前众包聚合领域的公平性研究尚未深入，缺乏收敛性理论保证，且仅有有限的后处理方法用于在人口统计均等下实施ε公平性。

Method: 1. 在ε公平性框架下分析多数投票和最优贝叶斯聚合的公平性；2. 在小群体规模下推导多数投票公平性差距的上界；3. 在可解释条件下证明聚合共识的公平性差距以指数速度收敛到ground-truth的公平性差距；4. 将最先进的连续域多类别公平后处理算法推广到离散域，强制任何聚合规则满足严格人口统计均等约束。

Result: 1. 理论推导显示聚合共识的公平性差距在可解释条件下以指数速度收敛到ground-truth的公平性差距；2. 实验验证了所提出方法的有效性，在合成和真实数据集上的结果支持理论见解；3. 扩展的离散域公平后处理算法能够对任何聚合规则强制执行严格的人口统计均等约束。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了众包聚合公平性领域的理论空白，为ε公平性框架下的聚合方法提供了收敛性保证，并通过算法扩展为解决ground-truth本身可能不公平的问题提供了实用解决方案。实验验证了理论分析和所提方法的有效性，为构建公平的众包标注系统提供了理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: As acquiring reliable ground-truth labels is usually costly, or infeasible, crowdsourcing and aggregation of noisy human annotations is the typical resort. Aggregating subjective labels, though, may amplify individual biases, particularly regarding sensitive features, raising fairness concerns. Nonetheless, fairness in crowdsourced aggregation remains largely unexplored, with no existing convergence guarantees and only limited post-processing approaches for enforcing $\varepsilon$-fairness under demographic parity. We address this gap by analyzing the fairness s of crowdsourced aggregation methods within the $\varepsilon$-fairness framework, for Majority Vote and Optimal Bayesian aggregation. In the small-crowd regime, we derive an upper bound on the fairness gap of Majority Vote in terms of the fairness gaps of the individual annotators. We further show that the fairness gap of the aggregated consensus converges exponentially fast to that of the ground-truth under interpretable conditions. Since ground-truth itself may still be unfair, we generalize a state-of-the-art multiclass fairness post-processing algorithm from the continuous to the discrete setting, which enforces strict demographic parity constraints to any aggregation rule. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and corroborate the theoretical insights.

</details>


### [179] [Sequence Diffusion Model for Temporal Link Prediction in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23233)
*Nguyen Minh Duc,Viet Cuong Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Temporal link prediction in dynamic graphs is a fundamental problem in many real-world systems. Existing temporal graph neural networks mainly focus on learning representations of historical interactions. Despite their strong performance, these models are still purely discriminative, producing point estimates for future links and lacking an explicit mechanism to capture the uncertainty and sequential structure of future temporal interactions. In this paper, we propose SDG, a novel sequence-level diffusion framework that unifies dynamic graph learning with generative denoising. Specifically, SDG injects noise into the entire historical interaction sequence and jointly reconstructs all interaction embeddings through a conditional denoising process, thereby enabling the model to capture more comprehensive interaction distributions. To align the generative process with temporal link prediction, we employ a cross-attention denoising decoder to guide the reconstruction of the destination sequence and optimize the model in an end-to-end manner. Extensive experiments on various temporal graph benchmarks show that SDG consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in the temporal link prediction task.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [180] [How well do generative models solve inverse problems? A benchmark study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23238)
*Patrick Krüger,Patrick Materne,Werner Krebs,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Generative learning generates high dimensional data based on low dimensional conditions, also called prompts. Therefore, generative learning algorithms are eligible for solving (Bayesian) inverse problems. In this article we compare a traditional Bayesian inverse approach based on a forward regression model and a prior sampled with the Markov Chain Monte Carlo method with three state of the art generative learning models, namely conditional Generative Adversarial Networks, Invertible Neural Networks and Conditional Flow Matching. We apply them to a problem of gas turbine combustor design where we map six independent design parameters to three performance labels. We propose several metrics for the evaluation of this inverse design approaches and measure the accuracy of the labels of the generated designs along with the diversity. We also study the performance as a function of the training dataset size. Our benchmark has a clear winner, as Conditional Flow Matching consistently outperforms all competing approaches.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

We apply them to a problem of gas turbine combustor design where we map six independent design parameters to three performance labels. We propose several metrics for the evaluation of this inverse design approaches and measure the accuracy of the labels of the generated designs along with the diversity. We also study the performance as a function of the training dataset size. Our benchmark has a clear winner, as Conditional Flow Matching consistently outperforms all competing approaches.

### [181] [Particle-Guided Diffusion Models for Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23262)
*Andrew Millard,Fredrik Lindsten,Zheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We introduce a guided stochastic sampling method that augments sampling from diffusion models with physics-based guidance derived from partial differential equation (PDE) residuals and observational constraints, ensuring generated samples remain physically admissible. We embed this sampling procedure within a new Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) framework, yielding a scalable generative PDE solver. Across multiple benchmark PDE systems as well as multiphysics and interacting PDE systems, our method produces solution fields with lower numerical error than existing state-of-the-art generative methods.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: We introduce a guided stochastic sampling method that augments sampling from diffusion models with physics-based guidance derived from partial differential equation (PDE) residuals and observational constraints, ensuring generated samples remain physically admissible. We embed this sampling procedure within a new Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) framework, yielding a scalable generative PDE solver. Across multiple benchmark PDE systems as well as multiphysics and interacting PDE systems, our method produces solution fields with lower numerical error than existing state-of-the-art generative methods.

</details>


### [182] [FOCUS: DLLMs Know How to Tame Their Compute Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23278)
*Kaihua Liang,Xin Tan,An Zhong,Hong Xu,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCUS：一种专为扩散大语言模型设计的推理系统，通过动态聚焦计算于可解码token并实时剔除不可解码token，显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型解码成本高昂，主要瓶颈在于并行计算时仅少量token可解码，大量计算浪费于不可解码token。

Method: 利用注意力权重与token解码概率的强相关性，设计动态计算聚焦机制，增加有效批处理大小，缓解计算限制。

Result: 在实证评估中，FOCUS相比生产级推理引擎LMDeploy实现最高3.52倍吞吐量提升，且在多个基准测试中保持或改进生成质量。

Conclusion: FOCUS系统通过消除扩散LLM解码中的计算浪费，实现了显著的速度提升并开源可用，为扩散模型的部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to Auto-Regressive models, but their deployment is constrained by high decoding cost. In this work, we identify a key inefficiency in DLLM decoding: while computation is parallelized over token blocks, only a small subset of tokens is decodable at each diffusion step, causing most compute to be wasted on non-decodable tokens. We further observe a strong correlation between attention-derived token importance and token-wise decoding probability. Based on this insight, we propose FOCUS -- an inference system designed for DLLMs. By dynamically focusing computation on decodable tokens and evicting non-decodable ones on-the-fly, FOCUS increases the effective batch size, alleviating compute limitations and enabling scalable throughput. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FOCUS achieves up to 3.52$\times$ throughput improvement over the production-grade engine LMDeploy, while preserving or improving generation quality across multiple benchmarks. The FOCUS system is publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/sands-lab/FOCUS.

</details>


### [183] [Decoupled Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems on Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23280)
*Thomas Y. L. Lin,Jiachen Yao,Lufang Chiang,Julius Berner,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We propose a data-efficient, physics-aware generative framework in function space for inverse PDE problems. Existing plug-and-play diffusion posterior samplers represent physics implicitly through joint coefficient-solution modeling, requiring substantial paired supervision. In contrast, our Decoupled Diffusion Inverse Solver (DDIS) employs a decoupled design: an unconditional diffusion learns the coefficient prior, while a neural operator explicitly models the forward PDE for guidance. This decoupling enables superior data efficiency and effective physics-informed learning, while naturally supporting Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) to avoid over-smoothing in Diffusion Posterior Sampling (DPS). Theoretically, we prove that DDIS avoids the guidance attenuation failure of joint models when training data is scarce. Empirically, DDIS achieves state-of-the-art performance under sparse observation, improving $l_2$ error by 11% and spectral error by 54% on average; when data is limited to 1%, DDIS maintains accuracy with 40% advantage in $l_2$ error compared to joint models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: We propose a data-efficient, physics-aware generative framework in function space for inverse PDE problems. Existing plug-and-play diffusion posterior samplers represent physics implicitly through joint coefficient-solution modeling, requiring substantial paired supervision. In contrast, our Decoupled Diffusion Inverse Solver (DDIS) employs a decoupled design: an unconditional diffusion learns the coefficient prior, while a neural operator explicitly models the forward PDE for guidance. This decoupling enables superior data efficiency and effective physics-informed learning, while naturally supporting Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) to avoid over-smoothing in Diffusion Posterior Sampling (DPS). Theoretically, we prove that DDIS avoids the guidance attenuation failure of joint models when training data is scarce. Empirically, DDIS achieves state-of-the-art performance under sparse observation, improving $l_2$ error by 11% and spectral error by 54% on average; when data is limited to 1%, DDIS maintains accuracy with 40% advantage in $l_2$ error compared to joint models.

</details>

### [184] [Towards Resiliency in Large Language Model Serving with KevlarFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22438)
*Shangshu Qian,Kipling Liu,P. C. Sruthi,Lin Tan,Yongle Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KevlarFlow大幅降低大语言模型服务系统的故障恢复时间，提升服务可用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统对硬件故障极度脆弱，恢复机制缓慢（需约10分钟），导致服务中断时间长

Method: 采用解耦的模型并行初始化、动态流量重路由和后台KV缓存复制三种关键技术

Result: 平均恢复时间减少20倍，故障条件下延迟和首token时间显著改善，运行时开销可忽略

Conclusion: KevlarFlow能有效弥补硬件不可靠性与服务可用性之间的差距，提升LLM服务系统的容错性

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving systems remain fundamentally fragile, where frequent hardware faults in hyperscale clusters trigger disproportionate service outages in the software stack. Current recovery mechanisms are prohibitively slow, often requiring up to 10 minutes to reinitialize resources and reload massive model weights. We introduce KevlarFlow, a fault tolerant serving architecture designed to bridge the gap between hardware unreliability and service availability. KevlarFlow leverages 1) decoupled model parallelism initialization, 2) dynamic traffic rerouting, and 3) background KV cache replication to maintain high throughput during partial failures. Our evaluation demonstrates that KevlarFlow reduces mean-time-to-recovery (MTTR) by 20x and, under failure conditions, improves average latency by 3.1x, 99th percentile (p99) latency by 2.8x, average time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 378.9x, and p99 TTFT by 574.6x with negligible runtime overhead in comparison to state-of-the-art LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [185] [Coordinating Power Grid Frequency Regulation Service with Data Center Load Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22487)
*Ali Jahanshahi,Sara Rashidi Golrouye,Osten Anderson,Nanpeng Yu,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"外生碳"（Exogenous Carbon）指标，首次量化GPU数据中心参与电网频率调节服务所带来的电网侧碳减排，并设计EcoCenter框架，通过协调GPU功率上限、计算单元（CU）分配与多GPU协同，在2秒粒度下最大化可提供的频率调节容量。实验表明：EcoCenter平均性能得分91.20%（最低不低于75%），调节能力比PowerMorph-GPU高30%–50%、比CPU方案高4倍（高利用率下达7倍）；其带来的电网侧外生碳减排在所有负载下均超过运行碳增量，最差情况（80%负载）仍净减排约12 kt CO2eq，50%负载时外生碳减排达52.9–433.2 kt CO2eq，可完全覆盖甚至超过数据中心自身碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML数据中心的快速扩张使电力需求激增（美国2024年约占总用电4%，2030年预计达9.1%，部分州如弗吉尼亚达25%并将继续增长），而可再生能源（风光）渗透率提升又加剧电网频率波动；由于可再生能源无法快速调节出力，电网仍依赖化石燃料电厂提供频率调节备用容量。这些"隐藏"的调节备用碳排放未被电网公布的发电碳强度指标（CI_gen）计入：H1（执行调节动作时的实际排放）、H2（为提供备用而使电厂在低效率点运行）、H3（为提供备用容量而需多开机组）。既有数据中心碳感知研究（CarbonAware调度、CarbonExplorer等）仅能降低自身能耗或迁移到低碳时段/地区，无法影响电网侧调节备用的排放；同时先前工作（如PowerMorph）只调制CPU、只关注经济收益，而GPU已占AI数据中心功耗的绝对主导（约5倍于CPU）。因此需要一种新指标来刻画数据中心参与调节服务的电网侧碳收益，并给出面向GPU的可行功率调制机制。

Method: 1）碳建模：定义 C_DC = C_op + C_em，扩展为 C_op(w/RS) = E_DC×CI_gen − (R_DC×MCE_resv)，其中新项 C_exogenous = R_DC×MCE_resv，MCE_resv为调节备用的边际碳排放。2）MCE_resv获取：采用Anderson等人(2025)的电网侧机组组合(unit commitment)模型，模拟CAISO/西部互联14GW电网，假设全时段需保持770MW(5.5%容量)调节备用，基于2022年历史价格与需求轨迹按小时求解；分别在"传统电厂提供备用"与"数据中心提供备用"两种情形下求解并取总排放之差，反推MCE_resv，捕捉H1/H2/H3三类隐藏排放。3）EcoCenter框架：服务器侧Controller按ISO广播的2秒频率调节信号r(t)与Optimizer给出的Server R重塑服务器功耗；Power Monitor用rocm-smi/TurboStat每秒采样；Resource Manager提供Power capping（SMI接口改ROCm内核实现）与Core allocation（基于CU Masking，修改ROCm运行时以共享内存+IOCTL注入位掩码实现核级空间划分，可推广到NVIDIA Green Context/libsmctrl）两类API；通过在线画像构建BE负载的功率模型（改变Power Cap与GPU核数采集功耗/吞吐），确定可高精度（≥90%）调制区间；单GPU调制策略为先用power capping（对吞吐影响约≤40%）再用CU缩放精调（影响可达约80%）；多GPU协调：LC工作负载按GPU索引从低到高分配，BE工作负载从高索引向低索引分配以避免干扰，通过pause/resume内核下发实现每GPU约60W的额外功率调节范围；Optimizer基于Z3 SAT求解器按小时最大化"平均功率成本−调频净成本"，支持CAISO非对称上/下调报价（R_up/R_down），并可约束为对称（PJM）。4）评测：8×AMD MI50 + 16核EPYC 7302服务器，LC用Resnet152推理（95分位尾延迟判定QoS），BE用GPT-2训练；使用Facebook SWIM负载轨迹9种均值/方差组合、PJM三类调节信号（Extreme/Noisy/High-Transition）、CAISO 2022年聚类得到的8周成本与奖励价格；单服务器测量后按100MW数据中心规模外推（含TCO与碳模型，UPS仅用20%容量参与调节）。对比基线为LC-only、CPU-only(PowerMorph)、PowerMorph-GPU、UPS-only。

Result: 1）性能得分：EcoCenter平均91.20%，最低不低于75%（ISO认证门槛），各负载档位得分从low档96%+降至high档83%–86%，说明2秒粒度高精度调制可行。2）调节能力：GPU方案普遍比CPU方案高3倍以上；EcoCenter较PowerMorph-GPU高30%–50%，较PowerMorph-CPU高4倍（高利用率下最高7倍）；利用率从10%→50%→80%时调节量下降幅度：PowerMorph-CPU为22%/56%，PowerMorph-GPU为16%/35%，EcoCenter仅13%/26%，即多GPU协调在高负载下仍能维持调节供给。3）碳收益：50%负载下Baseline运行碳91.1 kt CO2eq，引入BE调制后升至124.1 kt（+33.0 kt），而电网侧外生碳减排达52.9–433.2 kt CO2eq，其中高保真电网模型结果约为简化模型的3倍（433.2 vs 132 kt），说明简化模型会显著低估；80%负载最差情况下外生碳减排34.2 kt，仍超过运行碳增量21.3 kt，净减排约12 kt CO2eq；10%负载时外生碳减排甚至超过数据中心运行碳与隐含碳总和；CPU-only方案外生碳收益极小且随负载快速衰减，UPS-only优于CPU-only但仍逊于EcoCenter。4）工作负载影响：LC负载吞吐与延迟基本不受影响（LC与BE使用不同GPU集）；BE吞吐因调制而下降（围绕信号中值约50%）。5）TCO：CapEx不变，OpEx中电力成本因调节奖励下降，但引入BE吞吐损失的机会成本；电价越高（0.05→0.30美元/kWh）TCO越低，而GPU小时单价越高机会成本越可能反超OpEx收益，故建议用低成本GPU资源承担调节、高成本GPU资源专职延迟敏感推理。

Conclusion: 数据中心可通过参与频率调节服务显著降低对化石燃料调节备用的依赖。论文的核心贡献是提出"外生碳"指标，把电网侧调节备用的隐藏排放（执行调节排放、低效率运行、多机组备用）纳入碳核算，证明数据中心的碳影响不应止于降低自身能耗或迁移到低碳时段，而应通过削减电网侧调节备用需求来获得收益；并提出EcoCenter框架，通过功率上限、CU级核分配与多GPU暂停/恢复协调，在不损害延迟敏感负载QoS的前提下于2秒粒度提供高质量（平均91.2%）调节服务，其电网侧外生碳收益在所有负载条件下均超过数据中心自身的运行碳增量，在低负载下甚至可完全覆盖其碳足迹，同时在低电价/低GPU小时成本条件下可降低TCO。局限与未来方向包括：结果基于单台8-GPU服务器外推至100MW数据中心、依赖CAISO机组组合模型与2022年市场数据、改造ROCm运行时的实现侵入性、以及BE吞吐损失带来的机会成本需通过资源分层策略权衡。

Abstract: AI/ML data center growth have led to higher energy consumption and carbon emissions. The shift to renewable energy and growing data center energy demands can destabilize the power grid. Power grids rely on frequency regulation reserves, typically fossil-fueled power plants, to stabilize and balance the supply and demand of electricity. This paper sheds light on the hidden carbon emissions of frequency regulation service. Our work explores how modern GPU data centers can coordinate with power grids to reduce the need for fossil-fueled frequency regulation reserves. We first introduce a novel metric, Exogenous Carbon, to quantify grid-side carbon emission reductions resulting from data center participation in regulation service. We additionally introduce EcoCenter, a framework to maximize the amount of frequency regulation provision that GPU data centers can provide, and thus, reduce the amount of frequency regulation reserves necessary. We demonstrate that data center participation in frequency regulation can result in Exogenous carbon savings that oftentimes outweigh Operational carbon emissions.

</details>


### [186] [HetCCL: Accelerating LLM Training with Heterogeneous GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22585)
*Heehoon Kim,Jaehwan Lee,Taejeoung Kim,Jongwon Park,Jinpyo Kim,Pyongwon Suh,Ryan H. Choi,Sangwoo Lee,Jaejin Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HetCCL是一个用于异构GPU集群的通信库，支持跨厂商（如NVIDIA和AMD）GPU之间的高效集体通信，无需修改驱动或深度学习应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型快速发展，组织需要在多厂商GPU集群中进行训练，但现有深度学习框架缺乏对异构GPU间集体通信的支持，导致效率低下和成本增加。

Method: HetCCL引入两种新机制：1) 统一不同厂商的后端通信库(NCCL和RCCL)；2) 基于RDMA实现跨GPU通信，无需修改驱动。系统利用优化的厂商库同时在异构环境中保持高性能。

Result: 在多厂商GPU集群上的评估表明，HetCCL在纯NVIDIA或纯AMD环境中达到NCCL/RCCL相当的性能，在异构环境中能有效扩展，可实际用于NVIDIA和AMD GPU混合训练。

Conclusion: HetCCL解决了异构GPU集群集体通信的关键问题，实现了跨厂商GPU的高性能通信，为多厂商GPU训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models is driving organizations to expand their GPU clusters, often with GPUs from multiple vendors. However, current deep learning frameworks lack support for collective communication across heterogeneous GPUs, leading to inefficiency and higher costs. We present HetCCL, a collective communication library that unifies vendor-specific backends and enables RDMA-based communication across GPUs without requiring driver modifications. HetCCL introduces two novel mechanisms that enable cross-vendor communication while leveraging optimized vendor libraries, NVIDIA NCCL and AMD RCCL. Evaluations on a multi-vendor GPU cluster show that HetCCL matches NCCL and RCCL performance in homogeneous setups while uniquely scaling in heterogeneous environments, enabling practical, high-performance training with both NVIDIA and AMD GPUs without changes to existing deep learning applications.

</details>


### [187] [CONCUR: High-Throughput Agentic Batch Inference of LLM via Congestion-Based Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22705)
*Qiaoling Chen,Zhisheng Ye,Tian Tang,Peng Sun,Boyu Tian,Guoteng Wang,Shenggui Li,Yonggang Wen,Zhenhua Han,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文识别并命名了智能体批量推理中的“中段颠簸”（middle-phase thrashing）病态：因智能体异步推进、上下文单调增长，KV cache 在显存远未耗尽时就进入「淘汰—重算」恶性循环，吞吐量断崖式下降。作者提出 CONCUR——位于智能体执行层与 LLM 推理引擎（如 SGLang）之间的轻量级中间件，把准入控制粒度从“请求级”上移到“智能体级”，并借鉴 TCP 拥塞控制的 AIMD 算法，依据运行时 KV cache 占用率（U）与命中率（H）信号动态调节活跃智能体数量。实验在 H100 上针对 Qwen3-32B 与 DeepSeek-V3 的真实智能体工作负载，Qwen3-32B 最高加速 4.09×、DeepSeek-V3 最高 1.90×，且在 batch=40 时把命中率从 35.41%（SGLang）/32.21%（请求级准入）提升到 73.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型正被广泛部署为与外部环境长期交互的智能体（ReAct 范式），带来三类高吞吐批量推理需求：智能体 RL rollout（占端到端训练时间主体）、数据蒸馏（reasoning 模型蒸馏至学生模型）、智能体评测（并发模拟与判分上千场景）。这类负载中每个智能体的上下文随步数单调增长，其 KV cache 占用持续膨胀，使 GPU 上的 KV cache 从静态加速结构变成高度争用、动态演化的共享资源。现代服务引擎依赖前缀缓存（树形结构）+ LRU 淘汰 + CPU offload，这些手段在 chat 类高前缀复用负载上有效，但在智能体负载下失效：智能体异步推进——部分在生成、部分因等待外部工具而挂起，其 KV cache 暂时不活跃但在语义上关键，LRU 会将其优先淘汰；恢复时必须通过重算或主机-设备传输重建整个前缀，且该开销在整个执行中被反复支付，即使智能体总数不变。作者通过真实部署的 trace 分析发现智能体批量推理呈现三阶段模式：预热阶段（命中率约 90%）、中段（显存占用 80–100% 饱和但命中率骤降，被淘汰-重算循环困住，占总执行时间 90% 以上，重算额外占端到端延迟的 49.1%）、冷却阶段；反直觉地，此阶段增加并发会降低总吞吐。根源在于现有服务栈存在抽象错配：请求级无状态调度无法承载智能体的长期状态连续性，且被动式淘汰无法提前阻止抖动。因此需要从“反应式请求级缓存管理”转向“主动式智能体级准入控制”。

Method: CONCUR 是插入智能体执行框架与 LLM 服务引擎之间的轻量级 Agent-Level Controller，中间件式、非侵入，不替换现有组件（实验基于 SGLang）。执行流程为四阶段闭环：(1) Agent Admission——智能体将生成请求提交给控制器，由其决定准入或暂停（保留执行状态）；(2) Batched Generation——被准入者参与批量生成，按上下文长度消耗 KV cache；(3) Tool Execution and Suspension——调用外部工具的智能体暂时失活但保留逻辑 KV cache 关联，控制器在高压力期限制新准入以保护这些空闲状态不被淘汰；(4) Feedback and Control Update——控制器监测 KV cache 占用率与命中率并更新准入策略。核心是缓存感知的 AIMD 准入控制律：以并发智能体数 W_t 类比 TCP 拥塞窗口 W，以“缓存淘汰”对应“丢包”、“KV cache 重算”对应“重传”；控制律为：若 U_t < U_low 则 W_{t+1}=W_t+α（线性探索，避免指数过冲）；若 U_t > U_high 且 H_t < H_thresh 则 W_{t+1}=W_t×β（因重算代价为 O(L²) 的凸惩罚，需乘法级快速退出，O(log N) 步）；其余保持不变。U_low 与 U_high 之间的缓冲带用于吸收长上下文智能体带来的离散显存尖峰；只要命中率健康，即使在饱和区也维持高并发以优先吞吐。控制原语仅三个：admit、pause（仅在生成步与工具调用之间的明确边界生效，不打断在途计算）、resume（与准入协同避免恢复即再度拥塞）。超参固定跨模型与负载：α=2、β=0.5、U_low=0.2、U_high=0.5、H_thresh=0.2。附录给出阈值敏感性分析：U_high 在 0.5–0.6 区间稳健（0.8 会使延迟上升 4–5×，0.4 因过早限流导致延迟高 46–64%），U_low 需精细校准（0.1 使延迟高 7–8×，0.3/0.5 导致 2–3× 上升），最优为 (0.2, 0.5)。对比基线三类：原生 SGLang（请求级批处理 + LRU）、SGLang + 请求级固定并发上限准入、SGLang + HiCache（以缓存为中心，优先保留 + CPU offload）。

Result: 硬件为 NVIDIA H100 80GB（900GB/s NVLink，跨节点 8×400Gbps RoCE），软件栈 PyTorch 2.7.1 / Python 3.12 / CUDA 12.6。端到端批量延迟（Qwen3-32B）：Batch256/TP8/8GPU 由 1480s 降至 362s（4.09×），优于请求级准入的 2049s（0.72×，即更差）与 HiCache 的 976s（1.52×）；Batch256/TP4 由 2213s→757s（2.92×）；Batch256/TP2 由 2527s→846s（2.99×）。DeepSeek-V3：Batch16/16/16GPU 由 873s→521s（1.68×，HiCache 反而劣化至 2559s/0.34×）；Batch32 由 1226s→1018s（1.20×）；Batch40 由 3877s→2043s（1.90×，优于请求级 2903s 与 HiCache 2320s）。KV cache 命中率（DeepSeek-V3, TP8, 8GPU）：batch16/32/40 下 CONCUR 分别为 96.38/93.65/73.36%，而 SGLang 为 80.38/77.72/35.41%，请求级准入为 69.00/68.39/32.21%；HiCache 命中率虽高（97.48/97.13/96.08%）但因 PCIe 传输与排队开销延迟反而升高，说明高命中率不等于低延迟。时间序列上，基线在 batch256 时占用逼近 80% 容量而命中率骤降，CONCUR 通过准入调节维持高命中率。固定上限准入（30/32/64/128）均不如自适应策略：小上限保守且阶段性浪费显存，大上限（128）过载触发 thrashing。另需注意文中存在数值不一致：§5.3 称 CONCUR 最低延迟为 846ms，而 Table 1 中 TP8 配置实为 362ms。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是三点：一是识别并刻画了此前未被充分认识的“middle-phase thrashing”——离线智能体批量推理中由异步推进与长生命周期上下文造成的、发生在显存容量耗尽之前且持续时间占 90% 以上的效率塌陷；二是从网络拥塞控制获得灵感，把准入控制粒度提升到智能体级，提出非侵入式轻量中间件与缓存感知的 AIMD 准入控制算法（admit/pause/resume 三原语），在保持执行连续性的同时约束聚合 KV cache 压力；三是实证表明该机制在 Qwen3-32B（最高 4.09×）与 DeepSeek-V3（最高 1.90×）上稳定提升批量吞吐，且与现有 SGLang 等服务引擎及各类智能体框架兼容，静态固定上限无法替代自适应策略。作者认为，流控思想在可扩展 LLM 推理系统中具有更广泛的适用空间。与已有工作的差异在于：解耦内存/前缀池类方案（TokenLake）只解空间冗余不解决时间维度的并发过载；Parrot、Teola、Autellix、Kairos 等应用感知调度默认内存压力可由淘汰或重排处理，而大批量离线场景无论调度顺序如何都需要显式准入；TokenCake、Continuum 等依赖 offload 与 TTL 固定的被动机制，本文则避免昂贵的换入换出操作。

Abstract: Batch inference for agentic workloads stresses the GPU key-value (KV) cache in a sustained and cumulative manner, often causing severe throughput degradation well before memory capacity is exhausted. We identify this phenomenon as middle-phase thrashing, a previously under-characterized pathology in which cache efficiency collapses as long-lived agents accumulate state over time.
  We argue that mitigating this pathology requires moving beyond reactive, request-level cache management to proactive, agent-level admission control. Drawing inspiration from congestion control in distributed systems, we view the KV cache as a shared resource whose efficient utilization depends on feedback-driven regulation. Based on this insight, we present CONCUR, a lightweight control layer that regulates agent admission to bound aggregate cache pressure while preserving execution continuity. CONCUR adapts a cache-aware control algorithm to dynamically adjust the number of active agents using runtime cache signals.
  Across large models and real-world agent workloads, CONCUR prevents middle-phase thrashing and improves batch inference throughput by up to 4.09x on Qwen3-32B and 1.9x on DeepSeek-V3, while remaining compatible with existing LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [188] [AscendCraft: Automatic Ascend NPU Kernel Generation via DSL-Guided Transcompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22760)
*Zhongzhen Wen,Shudi Shao,Zhong Li,Yu Ge,Tongtong Xu,Yuanyi Lin,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AscendCraft 提出了一个面向昇腾 NPU 的轻量级 DSL，将 LLM 生成 AscendC 内核的任务拆为「先生成 DSL，再多趟结构化降级转译」两步，在 MultiKernelBench 52 个算子上取得 98.1% 编译成功率与 90.4% 功能正确率，46.2% 内核性能达到或超过 PyTorch eager，并成功迁移到新型 mHC 算子上（6.6×/3.0× 加速，经人工+LLM 优化后达 15.9×/7.2×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 内核生成工作几乎全部集中在 GPU（CUDA/Triton），受益于公开代码、文档与成熟生态；昇腾 NPU 内核生成几乎空白，因为 AscendC 编程模型强约束（32 字节对齐、UB/L1/L0 片上缓冲显式分配、CopyIn-Compute-CopyOut 流水线、跨核同步、队列机制）、公开示例稀缺、文档稀疏，且 Cube 高层接口与标准 AscendC 模型差异大。直接让 LLM 生成 AscendC 时甚至难以产出可编译代码（MultiKernelBench 上 Claude Sonnet-4 端到端正确率仅 13.0%）。因此需要一种能收敛生成空间、又保留硬件语义抽象的中间表示，使 LLM 专注于分块（tiling）、数据流与片上计算结构等算法级决策。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段（DSL 生成）：设计轻量级 Ascend DSL，包含 host 函数（显式核心划分与 tiling 参数、需给出内存约束理由、启动内核）与 kernel 函数（显式 UB/L1 缓冲分配、禁止隐式别名、copyin/compute/copyout 分块结构、参数化贴近 AscendC API 的向量与归约原语）。提示词由 DSL 规范说明 + 类别/形状特定的专家示例组成，使模型可从同类别算子中学习分块与数据流模式并泛化到未见配置。第二阶段（转译）：四趟 LLM 降级——Pass1 host 侧（tiling 结构体、逐核负载计算、启动 block 数）；Pass2 内核初始化（tiling 参数写入成员变量、GetBlockIdx() 取核号计算 GM 偏移、DSL 缓冲标注映射为 TQue/TBuf 并设定容量以支持双缓冲）；Pass3 计算翻译（每个 DSL 块映射为独立 __aicore__ inline 函数 CopyInX/ComputeX/CopyOutX，Process() 中按序调用，DataCopy 入队、dequeue 出队、SyncAll 仅在需要跨核依赖时插入）；Pass4 可选的对齐与 padding 精化（以 DataCopyPad 替换 DataCopy，配置 padding/stride/layout）。每趟提示包含映射规则、AscendC API 信息、示例与上一趟产出；每趟后编译并将错误信息回喂 LLM 修正，保证逐趟可编译并约束结构、防止非法交错。

Result: 在 Ascend 910B2 + CANN 8.1 + PyTorch 2.6 环境、KernelBench Level-1 更新形状（kernel 执行时间 >15ms）上：总体 Comp@1 98.1%、Pass@1 90.4%（Activation/Loss/Math/Normalization/Optimizer/Reduce/Pooling 七类共 52 kernel；Activation、Optimizer、Reduce 达 100% 正确率，Math 编译率因 mask_cumsum 布尔类型未被提示知识覆盖而降至 83.3%，Pooling 正确率 66.7% 最低）。性能：Fast0.2 82.7%、Fast0.8 57.7%、Fast1.0 46.2%；Loss 85.7% Fast1.0、Optimizer 100%、Activation 40%，而 Reduce/Pooling 的 Fast0.8 与 Fast1.0 均为 0%（归因于低层指令与归约原语难以在 DSL 层表达；Normalization 亦偏低）。mHC 案例：mHC_post 与 mHC_post_grad 一次生成即正确，加速 6.6× 与 3.0×；由资深开发者借助 LLM 在一天内优化后达 15.9× 与 7.2×。MatMul/Convolution 尚未纳入评估，作者计划探索以 CATLASS 作为替代目标语言。

Conclusion: 恰当的抽象与结构化的生成流程可使 LLM 内核合成在 NPU 上切实可行：DSL 抽象屏蔽低层细节但显式保留硬件相关语义，多趟结构化转译加编译反馈把生成空间约束在可合法规格内，从而把端到端正确率从 <5%/13% 提升到 90.4%。局限在于性能敏感类算子（归约、池化、归一化、矩阵类）仍显著落后于专家实现，说明部分优化无法仅从高层抽象推断。后续工作方向：在 DSL 层与生成的 AscendC 层做联合优化、引入自动优化/进化式搜索、补充更细粒度的专家示例与指令级调优，并将矩阵/卷积类算子纳入框架。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models critically depends on efficient kernel implementations, yet developing high-performance kernels for specialized accelerators remains time-consuming and expertise-intensive. While recent work demonstrates that large language models (LLMs) can generate correct and performant GPU kernels, kernel generation for neural processing units (NPUs) remains largely underexplored due to domain-specific programming models, limited public examples, and sparse documentation. Consequently, directly generating AscendC kernels with LLMs yields extremely low correctness, highlighting a substantial gap between GPU and NPU kernel generation.
  We present AscendCraft, a DSL-guided approach for automatic AscendC kernel generation. AscendCraft introduces a lightweight DSL that abstracts non-essential complexity while explicitly modeling Ascend-specific execution semantics. Kernels are first generated in the DSL using category-specific expert examples and then transcompiled into AscendC through structured, constraint-driven LLM lowering passes. Evaluated on MultiKernelBench across seven operator categories, AscendCraft achieves 98.1% compilation success and 90.4% functional correctness. Moreover, 46.2% of generated kernels match or exceed PyTorch eager execution performance, demonstrating that DSL-guided transcompilation can enable LLMs to generate both correct and competitive NPU kernels. Beyond benchmarks, AscendCraft further demonstrates its generality by successfully generating two correct kernels for newly proposed mHC architecture, achieving performance that substantially surpasses PyTorch eager execution.

</details>


### [189] [ERA: Epoch-Resolved Arbitration for Duelling Admins in Group Management CRDTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22963)
*Kegan Dougal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 ERA（Epoch-Resolved Arbitration）：用一个受信任的“终局仲裁者”（finality arbiter）作为 CRDT 对等体，通过周期性地广播“epoch 事件”（公布其本地 DAG 的 sources/frontiers）来为并发事件建立一个不可变的 happens-before 全序，从而解决群组权限管理 CRDT 中的“对决管理员”（Duelling Admins）问题。作者论证：单靠 DAG 本身无法区分“真实并发互撤”与“被撤职者回溯日期（backdating）的报复性撤销”——两者产生的 DAG 结构完全相同——因此必须引入外部仲裁信息。ERA 以异步批量方式仲裁，保持可用性，并带来 CRDT 所缺失的“终局性”（finality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRDT 通过无协调复制与强最终一致性换取可用性，但当物化视图被用于管理群组权限（Matrix、Keyhive 等 BFT-CRDT）时，降级/撤权是非单调操作，可能导致物化视图“回滚”先前已生效的成员事件。典型场景为 Duelling Admins：权限相同的两名管理员并发互相撤权，形成撤权环，合并函数必须自动打破该环。论文的核心动机是揭示其中的安全性张力：现有方案（按用户排序的 seniority ranking、按事件 ID/时间戳排序的 Matrix 状态解析、共识仲裁、PoW/PoS、检测 equivocation 后排除节点、禁止同级互撤）都难以同时满足三条性质——P1 可用性（分区下仍可写入本地 DAG）、P2 授权安全（无权限者不得成功执行需权限操作）、P3 并发安全（任何用户——包括自己制造冲突的用户——都不能影响冲突消解结果）。作者尤其强调回溯日期攻击（backdating）：被撤职的 Admin 可通过回填时间戳把顺序性撤权“伪装”为并发冲突，从而重新使用已放弃的权限（Figure 5），破坏 P2/P3；并且由于真实并发与恶意回填产生相同的 DAG，任何纯本地规则都无法区分二者，必须引入外部信息，即一个中立的“终局仲裁者”。

Method: 1）问题形式化：定义角色层级 Reader/Writer/Admin 与操作 join、write、promote、demote（含自降级），给出 P1/P2/P3；定义因果稳定性（Almeida 2024b）与终局性（Final(m)：不存在并发事件能回滚 m）、回滚判据 Rollback(m′,m) ≡ MV({m,m′}) ≡ MV({m′})；区分可检测回填（与自身并发）与不可检测回填（与最新接收事件并发，与弱网络下的正常行为不可区分），并给出 Figure 1 的 DAG 说明。2）逐一评估候选仲裁机制：user-based（seniority ranking，Figure 4）、event-based（Matrix 状态解析，按事件 ID/时间戳，可被攻击者微调哈希操纵）、consensus-based（Sybil 攻击）、proof-of-X（近似 user-based 破坏 P3）、排除 equivocating 节点（Blocklace；各节点无法一致认定哪个事件被回填）、禁止同级互撤（Figure 3 显示回填仍可破坏）。3）ERA 机制：仲裁者定期发布 epoch 事件列出其 DAG 当前 sources，事件呈“洋葱层”式闭包（closed past），按 epoch 优先排序得到部分执行序；未纳入任何 epoch 的事件落入 pending epoch，一律排在所有已定序 epoch 之后；epoch 可按 pending 事件数量触发，也可针对 demote 等非单调操作按需触发；epoch 内事件再按撤权规则、时间戳、事件哈希等次级键排序，形成“有界全序”。4）信任与可靠性机制：epoch 事件签名以便构造欺诈证明（可结合 transparency log）、仲裁者列表回退（首仲裁者出问题时切换下一个）、对仲裁者隐藏 CRDT 明文（加密）、公开信誉与可用性列表；另提出以群组创建者充当仲裁者，并引入不可变的“Creator”角色使 Duelling Creators 不可能。

Result: 结论是：Duelling Admins 必须由外部仲裁者以全局全序打破撤权环，纯 DAG 本地规则无法做到公平（真实并发与回填报复的 DAG 同构）。ERA 通过 epoch 事件在 epoch 内给出有界全序，实现终局性，抑制回滚与回填攻击；代价是引入对仲裁者的信任与可达性依赖：仲裁者不可达时已终局事件仍安全但新事件无法终局，仅 pending epoch 内事件仍可被回填。论文给出 Matrix 生态的实证数据：matrix.org 与 40,779 个服务器联邦，随机抽取 9,048,520 个房间，剔除特权用户少于 3 的房间后得 213,490 个房间，特权用户所在唯一域数呈近似几何衰减（1 域 62.4%、2 域 23%、3 域 8.9%、4 域 2.6%、5 域 1.2%、6 域 0.6%、7+ 1.3%），因此需要至少 3 个特权域的法定人数式方案在 Matrix 生态中难以实质提升可靠性，作者建议在创建者服务器永久离线时将房间所有权移交给新用户。整体效果被定位为“混合一致性”：安全敏感操作可等待终局（牺牲 P1），一般操作立即执行（可能事后被作废，保留 P1）。作者还提出将终局/未终局事件分离，为后续的压缩、垃圾回收、渐进替换仲裁者列表等研究留出空间。

Conclusion: 1）Duelling Admins 是 CRDT 群组权限管理中的固有难题，任何仅依赖 DAG 与本地确定性排序的方案都无法同时满足可用性、授权安全与并发安全（P1/P2/P3），尤其是回溯日期攻击可以自洽地把顺序撤权改写为并发冲突。2）解决方案需要外部的、受信任的终局仲裁者（finality arbiter），其通过 epoch 事件以异步批量方式发布“已见 sources”，为并发事件建立不可变的有界全序，从而把 CRDT 从“无终局性”提升到“具备终局性”，改善了一致性等级；这是把共识的协调从“提交前”移到“提交后”的设计（类似区块链中矿工不预先协调挖块却逐步收敛到单一分叉）。3）信任成本被明确化：仲裁者需高可用、可达、可签名以提供可问责性（欺诈证明、transparency log）、可多仲裁者回退、可通过加密与信誉机制抑制其恶意重排；或以不可变 Creator 角色让群组创建者兼任仲裁者。4）未来工作包括利用终局/未终局事件分离设计新算法、压缩或垃圾回收已终局事件、以及基于终局性逐步替换仲裁者列表以剔除拜占庭仲裁者。

Abstract: Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) are used in a range of fields for their coordination-free replication with strong eventual consistency. By prioritising availability over consistency under partition, nodes accumulate events in different orders, and rely on an associative, commutative and idempotent merge function to present a materialised view of the CRDT. Under some circumstances, the state of the materialised view over time can appear to ''roll back'' previously applied events. When the materialised view is used to manage group permissions such as ones found in instant messaging applications, this can lead to surprising behaviour. This can occur when there are multiple concurrent events, such as in the Duelling Admins problem where two equally permissioned admins concurrently revoke each other's permissions. Who wins? This article argues that a Byzantine admin can exploit concurrency to win the duel. As a result, an external arbiter is required to arbitrate an immutable happens-before relation between concurrent events. Arbitration occurs asynchronously in batches via optional ''epoch events'', preserving availability. This introduces a bounded total order within epochs, and the resulting ''finality'' improves on the level of consistency CRDTs can provide.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Linux Kernel Recency Matters, CVE Severity Doesn't, and History Fades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22196)
*Piotr Przymus,Witold Weiner,Krzysztof Rykaczewski,Gunnar Kudrjavets*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 Linux 内核自 2024 年 2 月 13 日成为自有 CNA 以来发布的 6,464 个 CVE 做实证分析（覆盖 5.4/5.10/5.15/6.1/6.6/6.12 六个 LTS 分支、约 45,900 个 CVE-版本对、32,857 条可计算的 LTS-CVE 实例）。核心结论有三：(1) CVSS 评分、严重等级与各基础度量对补丁延迟的关联几乎为零（Somers' D ∈ [-0.04, 0.01]，自助法 95% CI 收敛到 [0,0]），且 CVSS 向量高度模板化（AV=L, AC=L, PR=L, UI=N, S=U 覆盖 >95% CVE），因而缺乏区分度；(2) 内核版本“年龄”是一个稳定但幅度有限的预测因子（D_xy = 0.13），中位修复时长随分支变旧单调上升（6.12 约 1,112 天 → 5.4 约 2,677 天，整体中位 1,976 天）；(3) 引入漏洞的提交明显大于修复提交（引入中位新增 56 行/触及 2 文件，修复 75% 不超过 9 行、绝大多数仅 1 文件），且大体量提交多为被 squash 的新特性/重构，属于复杂工程活动的副产品而非疏忽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2024 年 2 月 13 日 Linux 内核项目成为自己的 CVE 编号机构（CNA），开始自行分配和管理 CVE，数量比过去高出一个数量级（社区平均每天约 14 个新 CVE，而 2006–2018 年间每年仅约 84 个影响内核的 CVE）。这一制度性转变把内核漏洞管理从"社区对 CVE 的抵触"转变为制度化的漏洞管理流程，也直接冲击了所有维护/分发定制内核的厂商：需要判定输入 CVE 如何分诊、补丁回移（backport）的先后顺序、是否应依据 NVD 的 CVSS 评级与严重性来排优先级，以及哪些提交更容易引入漏洞。既有实证研究（Android、Eclipse、桌面客户端等）未针对"成为 CNA 之后"的 Linux 内核做过生存分析式的生命周期刻画。论文据此提出三个研究问题：RQ1 引入型提交与修复型提交的"解剖学"差异及其对修复成本（此处严格定义为 LOC 变更规模，不估算人力/时间/资金成本）的启示；RQ2 CVSS 分数在决定修复速度上的作用；RQ3 内核版本年龄对修复速度的影响。

Method: 数据采集：四阶段流水线，使用内核专用工具 strak（CVE-修复提交分析，git log 模式匹配）、dyad（漏洞-修复提交对推断，基于行级 blame）、verhaal（提交到发布周期映射）以及 NVD API（仅用于 CVSS v3.1 等元数据富化），覆盖 6 个 LTS 分支、约 1,000 个内核版本；CVE-引入提交与修复提交的关联优先取自内核 CVE 仓库而非 CVE 元数据日期（因回溯性分配与发布日期滞后不可靠）。数据集规模：6,464 个唯一 CVE（58% 有 CVSS v3.1）、约 45,900 个 (CVE, 版本) 对、32,857 个引入/修复时间戳俱全的 LTS-CVE 实例（73%）、19,693 个时间与 CVSS 元数据兼具者（43%）；CVSS 分析在唯一 CVE 层级，生存与修复延迟分析在 LTS-CVE 层级，提交解剖在（去重后的）提交对层级。

分析方法：① 生存分析——Kaplan–Meier 估计右删失生存函数，事件定义为"CVE 被修复"，起点为 dyad 识别的引入提交时间戳，终点为该 LTS 分支上最早的（回移）修复提交时间戳，Y_i = T_e − T_s（天），无修复者按研究窗口右删失；② 关联强度用 Somers' D_xy（仅在可比较对上计算，兼容删失），95% CI 用 CVE 级 bootstrap 重采样；③ 全局 log-rank 检验作为稳健性检查；④ 提交结构分析使用 PatchScope 对 diff 做行级自动标注（code / documentation / configuration / tests 等类别），并统计修改文件数、增删行数及分类别增删行；⑤ 对 CVSS 向量做 apriori 关联规则挖掘（最小支持度 0.95）；⑥ 人工抽查 23 个大型 CVE 相关提交（20 个随机 + 3 个边界案例），由一名操作系统工程师与一名安全工程师独立复核。论文另发布复现包（数据抽取脚本与人工整理的 CVE-提交映射）。

Result: （1）提交结构不对称：引入型提交均值 4 文件、+57/−42 行，中位 2 文件、+56/−9 行，最大 920 文件/204,645 行；修复型提交均值 1 文件、+9/−6 行，75% 分位不超过 9 行，绝大多数只触及单个文件，超过 11,000 个修复仅改动少于 5 行，且极少涉及文档、配置或测试。引入型提交中文档与 project/构建脚本改动显著更多。人工抽查显示大提交多为"全功能新增"（新驱动、新协议、新文件系统，如 CVE-2025-22079 的 OCFS2 引入超过 24,000 行、55 个文件；CVE-2025-37970 的 LSM6DSM 传感器驱动）或"重构/重写"（如 CVE-2025-21941 的 DRM 变量重命名引入缺陷），并常因维护者 squash 而成为开发史的粗略快照；风险多来自用户-内核边界处理（sysfs/ioctl/网络）、并发与 DMA 管理以及重构带来的行为漂移，而非草率实现。

（2）CVSS 几乎无解释力：所有基础度量与修复延迟的 Somers' D 分别为 AV 0.0、AC −0.01、PR 0.0、UI 0.0、S 0.0、C −0.04、A 0.0、严重等级 −0.01，95% CI 全部塌缩到 [0,0]；即便存在 log-rank 统计显著性，也源于极大样本量与严重失衡的分组，而非有意义或单调的关系。聚合到严重等级后，Critical 的中位修复时长 2,115 天反而高于 Low 的 1,562 天。CVSS 赋值高度模板化：apriori 显示 AV=L、AC=L、PR=L、UI=N、S=U 覆盖 >95% 的 CVE，且 AV=L ⇒ UI=N 的置信度约 0.999；这些向量由 NVD/MITRE 等外部机构打分，而非内核维护者，反映的是外部标准化惯例而非内核特定的风险评估。

（3）内核年龄是有效但有限的预测因子：32,857 个观测的整体中位修复时长 1,976 天（约 5.4 年），修复时间分布高度右偏——超过 89% 的漏洞在引入一年后才被修复，仅 1.4% 在一个月内、0.14% 在一周内修复。Kaplan–Meier 曲线显示越新的分支修复风险更高，分支中位数随年龄单调递增：6.12 = 1,112 天、6.6 = 1,427 天、6.1 = 1,844 天、5.15 = 1,960 天、5.10 = 2,189 天、5.4 = 2,677 天；按新旧排序的一致性指数 D_xy = 0.13（CI 很窄），说明版本年龄的判别力高于随机但效应量适中。

（4）效度与局限：作者明确"成本"仅指 LOC 变更规模；引入时间戳是由行级 blame 推断的近似值（受 commit squash、重构、延迟分配 CVE 影响，前 Git 历史缺失），且部分 CVE 关联到 2005 年 Torvalds 从 BitKeeper 迁往 Git 的合并提交，使起点天然偏早；结论关注相对生命周期而非绝对值；分析限于 LTS 分支，不必然外推至主线或其他开源项目；CVSS 类别严重失衡会削弱基于秩的度量灵敏度。

Conclusion: 论文以数据支持了几项长期存在但少被量化的社区直觉："保持内核新鲜度有用，CVSS 并不能刻画内核真实的打补丁动态"。面向行业的建议包括：升级到较新的 stable/LTS 内核不仅是功能诉求，而是可测量的安全收益与维护成本下降（留在旧 LTS 意味着更长的暴露窗口）；不应把 NVD 的 CVSS 评级当作补丁紧迫性信号，各组织需结合自身内核配置、威胁模型与部署场景自行分诊；CVE 洪流与 EU《网络韧性法案》（CRA）要求的积极利用漏洞 24 小时上报等合规压力叠加，使当前"3–4 名资深内核工程师逐条审阅 stable 提交"的流程难以长期持续，自动化回移（如 AUTOSEL 等 AI/LLM 辅助分类器）是必然方向，且既有自动化回移研究都早于内核成为 CNA 这一节点。作者同时提醒开源安全与志愿维护者的可持续性风险，建议依赖及时修复的企业以工程投入或资金补偿回馈上游。

未来工作：扩展提交标注维度、跨项目对比、以及漏洞修复的预测建模。需要说明的是，本研究规模宏大、方法组合（生存分析 + 关联度量 + 关联规则挖掘 + 行级 diff 标注 + 人工复核）较为扎实，但也存在上述构造效度与外推性限制，尤其引入时间的近似性使"超过 89% 漏洞需一年以上修复"这一数字的绝对解读需谨慎；此外 CVSS 与修复延迟的"零关联"部分源于向量高度模板化，样本失衡可能进一步压低秩相关的灵敏度。

Abstract: In 2024, the Linux kernel became its own Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) Numbering Authority (CNA), formalizing how kernel vulnerabilities are identified and tracked. We analyze the anatomy and dynamics of kernel CVEs using metadata, associated commits, and patch latency to understand what drives patching. Results show that severity and Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) metrics have a negligible association with patch latency, whereas kernel recency is a reasonable predictor in survival models. Kernel developers fix newer kernels sooner, while older ones retain unresolved CVEs. Commits introducing vulnerabilities are typically broader and more complex than their fixes, though often only approximate reconstructions of development history. The Linux kernel remains a unique open-source project -- its CVE process is no exception.

</details>


### [191] [Stalled, Biased, and Confused: Uncovering Reasoning Failures in LLMs for Cloud-Based Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22208)
*Evelien Riddell,James Riddell,Gengyi Sun,Michał Antkiewicz,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"去框架化"的受控实验框架，专门隔离并评估开源LLM在云原生多跳根因分析（RCA）任务中的推理能力。作者在两个真实微服务数据集（GAIA/MicroSS 与 OpenRCA/Online Boutique）上评测6个开源LLM × 3种工作流（Straight-Shot、ReAct、Plan-and-Execute），共执行48,000次模拟故障场景（累计228天计算时长），同时度量根因定位/故障类型/传播路径的top-k准确率与中间推理轨迹质量；并通过开放编码+7轮迭代修订，构建了含16类推理失败（RF）的标注体系，用GPT-5作为LLM-as-a-Judge进行标注（人工复核Cohen's κ=0.92）。结论：整体准确率低且大量低于随机猜测；智能体化工作流对LA/TA/HA常呈边际递减甚至负收益（对3B/4B小模型尤为明显，Plan-and-Execute下执行错误率高达77%~87%），但能提升传播路径有效性（PA）；模态上metrics对定位最关键、logs对类型判定最关键，而traces常常造成干扰（移除后精度反而上升）；RF中锚定偏差、重复停滞、任意证据选择、未更新信念是与正确性负相关最强的失败类型。作者强调需在假设排序前加入证据充分性校验、时序校验与KG一致性过滤，并以tree-of-thought、自洽/批判机制及显式领域指引改进推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生微服务系统高度分布式、组件互依，单一故障可跨组件级联传播，导致多跳根因分析（RCA）困难；生产环境遥测数据规模与异构性常令人工与自动化工具失效。LLM因其通用推理与工具使用能力被视为自动化RCA的新范式，但现有研究存在两大方法论缺口：(1) 多智能体/复杂流水线将RCA与异常检测、代码执行等子任务纠缠，使失败归因无法区分"推理本身"与"外围设计"；(2) 缺乏对推理质量的系统、可复现评测，现有指标多为最终输出的词面/语义相似度或粗粒度人工判断，在多跳场景下无法评估传播路径与中间推理。因此需要一种能把LLM推理能力单独隔离出来、并系统刻画其推理失败类型的受控实证评测。

Method: 1）任务形式化：将RCA定义为三目标联合推理任务——根因定位（LA）、故障类型分类（TA）、传播路径识别（PA），输出top-k=3个假设元组（根因实体、故障类型、KG中的传播路径、自然语言论证），路径须为KG中以告警实体/关系为终点的有效游走。2）数据准备：多模态告警抽取（feature fusion）——日志用Drain解析模板+两级采样（保留ERROR级与低频模板，折叠高频模板为首条代表）、追踪用Isolation Forest对服务/实例间调用的响应时间与状态码检测（高响应时间→性能退化PD，异常状态码→错误ERROR）、指标用3-sigma规则（超μ+3σ为up、低于μ−3σ为down并记录方向）；告警统一化采用时间序与元素聚合两种策略。系统被建模为显式带类型知识图谱KG（实体：Service/Service-Instance/Database/Cache/Coordination-Manager/Host；关系：control-flow、data-flow、instance-of、has-instance、hosted-on、hosts），手工解析架构文档与部署清单构建，提供列表与JSON两种表示。3）推理：基于LangGraph（递归上限50）实现三种工作流——Straight-Shot（一次性直答，可选CoT）、ReAct（thought-action-observation循环）、Plan-and-Execute（先规划后逐步执行并动态重规划）；工具为确定性且语义极简的两类——Data Characteristics（节点存在性、节点属性与告警、类型实例枚举、边属性）与 Graph Traversal（r-hop邻域、两实体间所有简单路径），每次调用强制提供"reasoning"理由字段；刻意禁止直接访问KG与写代码。4）评测：LA/TA/PA/HA四类指标的A@1、A@3与Avg@3，并给出按故障位置数/类型数计算的随机猜测基线；推理质量采用rationale-based方案，先用开放编码从30条输出归纳失败码，经7轮迭代（每轮新抽20条，LLM裁判与人工并行标注以对齐解释）形成16类RF分类法（Per-hypothesis/Full-trace/Cross-cutting × General/RCA-specific/Procedural），以GPT-5作为独立LLM-as-a-Judge对分层抽样的3,073条（19,200条的16%）推理轨迹标注；可靠性由两位作者独立复核30条，Cohen's κ=0.92。5）实验：6个开源指令模型（Llama 3.2 3B、Qwen 3 4B/32B、Llama 3.3 70B、Command R+ 104B、DeepSeek-R1 70B蒸馏版，后者仅测Straight-Shot），128K上下文、需自托管以满足隐私；10×A6000+8×V100本地执行48,000场景，228天累计时长。6）敏感性实验：留出单一模态（logs/metrics/traces）做Wilcoxon符号秩检验；对比告警统一化与KG表示策略；用风险差RD与相对风险RR量化"存在某RF"与正确性的关联。

Result: RQ1（能力）：Qwen 3 (32B) 综合最优（𝒜上HA@3=0.36，ℬ上ReAct HA@3=0.09），Qwen 3 (4B) 与32B在Straight-Shot/ReAct上差距仅约1.2±3.5个百分点；Llama 3.3 在PA上显著领先（如ℬ上PA@3 达0.73、𝒜上Plan-and-Execute达0.78）。无单一模型全面占优，需按RCA子目标选型。智能体化工作流对LA/TA/HA常呈零或负收益：Llama 3.2 LA@3由0.31(Straight-Shot)降至0.23(ReAct)、0.05(Plan-and-Execute)；小模型在Plan-and-Execute下执行错误率Qwen3-4B 87.3%、Llama3.2 77.3%，工具覆盖率仅23.4%/49.5%（其他模型约94.5%），TA等多指标跌破随机基线。但智能体化探索可显著提升PA（Llama 3.3 由0.61→0.73→0.78）。推理耗时：ReAct较Straight-Shot +40%，Plan-and-Execute +354%（Command R+ 达27.3分钟/场景）。RQ2（模态敏感性）：移除metrics对定位冲击最大（ΔLA通常−0.07~−0.15，如Qwen3-4B −0.155）；移除logs主要损害类型判定（ΔTA最低−0.13）；令人意外地移除traces常提升精度（ΔPA最高+0.28，ΔHA最高+0.07/0.06），因模型过度聚焦trace调用关系、忽略部署关系且trace噪声与体量淹没低频信号。RQ3（推理失败）：16类RF普遍存在，程序性失败（RF-09未更新信念、RF-10模拟/角色混淆、RF-11过度臆测、RF-12重复停滞）在模型与工作流间离散度最大，Llama 3.2 与 Qwen 系列在智能体控制下显著更高；RCA专属失败（RF-02指标语义误读、RF-03溯源混淆、RF-06实例特异性无据、RF-07任意证据选择）跨模型较一致，反映领域知识不足；一般性逐假设失败（RF-01捏造证据、RF-08证据不足、RF-04时序颠倒、RF-05虚假因果）平均流行度最高；跨切面失败（RF-13锚定、RF-15内部矛盾、RF-14无效推理）在 Llama 3.2 与 Command R+ 更高，Qwen 在智能体工作流下更低。RQ4（失败与正确性）：最清晰的负向预测因子是RF-13锚定偏差、RF-12重复停滞、RF-07任意证据选择、RF-09未更新信念（RD<−0.15，RR<0.55）；对路径存在性则是RF-10/RF-11/RF-12最强（RD<−0.29，RR<0.32）。智能体工作流的绝对RD更接近零（迭代推理稀释单次失败影响），但每条轨迹平均RF数反而更高（ReAct 5.1、Plan-and-Execute 4.8、Straight-Shot 4.6），存在共现与早期错误级联。RF-10/RF-11 在ReAct下与正确性正相关（RR>1.49）、在Plan-and-Execute下强负相关（RR<0.22），因其与执行错误共现率高达68.6%/80.6% vs 7.4%/0.5%。RF-03与RF-08出现表观正相关属聚合伪影（逐样本聚合、任一属性正确即判正、任一假设含RF即标记）。

Conclusion: 当前开源LLM在受控的多跳云RCA上仍表现不佳：准确率低且随工作流复杂度上升常低于随机猜测，推理失败普遍存在；智能体化结构本身不是解药——它带来更高成本（Plan-and-Execute推理时间+354%）、更多程序性失败与执行崩溃，仅对传播路径有效性有条件收益，且收益取决于模型能否可靠规划与执行工具探索。作者主张RCA智能体的设计重点应从"堆叠框架"转向"修补具体推理能力"：包括早期假设多样化（tree-of-thought）、自洽/批判机制、证据充分性校验、时序校验、KG一致性过滤，以及显式注入领域指引（分诊启发式、常见传播机制）与针对这些行为的微调。RF共现与级联（尤其多步/多智能体场景中的早期误判放大）是重要的开放问题。同时强调交互式探索在生产规模下仍然必要（一次性提供全量系统上下文不可行），故需要模型—工作流的针对性调优，而非假定规划普遍有益。贡献包括：可复现的评测框架、经人工校准的16类RF分类法与基于理由的LLM-as-a-Judge评测方案，以及完整复现包。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is essential for diagnosing failures within complex software systems to ensure system reliability. The highly distributed and interdependent nature of modern cloud-based systems often complicates RCA efforts, particularly for multi-hop fault propagation, where symptoms appear far from their true causes. Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) present new opportunities to enhance automated RCA. However, their practical value for RCA depends on the fidelity of reasoning and decision-making. Existing work relies on historical incident corpora, operates directly on high-volume telemetry beyond current LLM capacity, or embeds reasoning inside complex multi-agent pipelines -- conditions that obscure whether failures arise from reasoning itself or from peripheral design choices.
  We present a focused empirical evaluation that isolates an LLM's reasoning behavior. We design a controlled experimental framework that foregrounds the LLM by using a simplified experimental setting. We evaluate six LLMs under two agentic workflows (ReAct and Plan-and-Execute) and a non-agentic baseline on two real-world case studies (GAIA and OpenRCA). In total, we executed 48,000 simulated failure scenarios, totaling 228 days of execution time. We measure both root-cause accuracy and the quality of intermediate reasoning traces. We produce a labeled taxonomy of 16 common RCA reasoning failures and use an LLM-as-a-Judge for annotation. Our results clarify where current open-source LLMs succeed and fail in multi-hop RCA, quantify sensitivity to input data modalities, and identify reasoning failures that predict final correctness. Together, these contributions provide transparent and reproducible empirical results and a failure taxonomy to guide future work on reasoning-driven system diagnosis.

</details>


### [192] [Predicting Intermittent Job Failure Categories for Diagnosis Using Few-Shot Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22264)
*Henri Aïdasso,Francis Bordeleau,Ali Tizghadam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 FlaXifyer 与 LogSift 两个工具：前者用少样本微调的通用文本编码器 BGE 仅凭 CI 作业执行日志对间歇性（flaky）作业失败进行多类根因分类，在 TELUS 2,458 个失败样本上以每类仅 12 个标注样本达到 84.3% Macro F1 与 92.0% Top-2 准确率；后者以二分递归方式定位影响分类决策的日志片段，平均削减 74.4% 的人工审阅量、亚秒级返回，并在 87% 的情形下给出相关失败信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD 流水线中的间歇性作业失败（flaky jobs）多由网络中断、基础设施故障、资源耗尽、凭据/DNS 问题等非确定性因素引起，在 TELUS 占全部作业失败的 25%，造成重复重跑浪费算力，且诊断需跨网络、基础设施、应用多团队协作，解析周期可达数天。既有研究主要解决"检测是否为间歇性失败"以减少重跑（Lampel 等基于遥测数据的分类器、Olewicki 等基于 TF-IDF+重跑指标、Moriconi 等知识图谱），以及仅覆盖约 4% 间歇性失败的 flaky test 研究，却未解决随后的"诊断/根因归类"挑战；作者前期提出的基于正则的 46 类标注工具虽在未见样本上达到 91% 精确率，但缺乏语义理解、无法泛化到未见日志模式、需人工易错维护。因此需要一种仅依赖作业日志、低标注成本、可泛化的自动分类方法，支撑自动分诊（路由到正确团队）与后续按类别的自动修复。

Method: 方法分三阶段。1）日志预处理：7 条规则将 URL/路径/时长/版本替换为常量标记、标识符替换为 <ID>、移除非字母数字字符与数字（保留 HTTP 状态码与退出码）、去空行与重复行，平均压缩 61% 日志体积，解决 512 token 输入限制与维度灾难。2）少样本微调：使用 SetFit 框架对预训练编码器做对比学习（正三元组为同类日志对、负三元组为异类日志对，余弦相似度损失），比较 BGE（bge-base-en-v1.5，109M，通用文本）与 CodeBERT（125M，代码感知）两种编码器。3）分类头：在微调后的 768 维嵌入上训练多项逻辑回归，推理时 c = argmax_k[C(E(P(log)))]。解释性技术 LogSift 受二分查找启发，递归对半切分日志并以"该片段是否复现原预测类别"为判据定位最小影响片段（阈值 τ=2），返回 (start,end) 索引区间。实验设计：数据集来自 80 个 GitLab 工业项目、2018-02 至 2024-07 共 4,511 个已标注失败，按 RFM 优先级选取 13 类（覆盖 2,458 个样本，排除样本量不足的 cloud_token_limit_exceeded）；采用 Monte Carlo 交叉验证（学习集 25%/验证集 25%/测试集 50%，30 次迭代，平均 1,800 次模型训练），Optuna 每配置 5 次调参（A100 GPU）；RQ1 考察 2/4/8/10/12/16 shots × 两种编码器，RQ2 考察 K=8→10→13 的类别扩展与各类 F1，RQ3 在 614 条测试日志上评估 LogSift 的削减率、n-一致性、执行时间，并对 100 个样本按 C1/C2/C3 三项二元标准做人工相关性评分。

Result: RQ1：BGE 在所有 shot 设置下全面优于 CodeBERT；12 shots 时 BGE 达 84.3% Macro F1（MCC 83.8%、precision 85.4%、recall 86.3%、Top-2 92.0%、Top-3 95.3%），比 CodeBERT 的 76.2% 高 8.1 个百分点；2 shots 为 66.1%，8 shots 为 82.2%，12→16 shots 仅提升 1.1 个百分点且 Wilcoxon 检验 p>0.05 不显著，同时标准差从 6.2% 降至 1.9%，故 10–12 shots 为性能与标注成本的最佳折中。RQ2：类别从 8 扩展到 13 时 Macro F1 从 92.5% 降至 84.3%（8→10 降 7.0 点，10→13 仅降 1.3 点），说明主要复杂度增量出现在首批中等优先级类别引入时；核心类别多数稳定（runner pod waiting timeout 98.6→98.8、flaky ui test 98.6→97.3、dependency installation failure 92.6→92.4 等），但 api gateway deployment error（−14.2）与 git transient error（−8.2）因与新加入的网络类语义重叠而显著退化；K=13 时各类 F1 跨度 68.4%–98.8%，host resolution failure 最难（68.4%），helm resource error 方差最大（19.8%）。RQ3：LogSift 平均削减率 74.4%±27.6%（405 行日志压至约 104 行），30 行一致性 63.1%、10 行一致性 40.9%、2 行一致性 12.0%，平均执行 0.98 秒；定性评估中 69% 输出为"High"相关、18% 为"Relevant"、仅 6% 不相关，且在误分类案例中仍能暴露真实根因（如图中 zip 读取失败 curl:(26)）。

Conclusion: FlaXifyer 证明通用文本编码器（BGE）配合少样本对比微调比代码专用模型更适合异构作业日志，能以每类 12 条标注（13 类共 156 条）实现实用级别的自动分诊，并在类别扩展时优雅降级；LogSift 以近线性/对数级开销提供可解释性，显著降低人工日志审阅负担。二者组合支持"失败自动归类→路由到责任团队→附带可操作诊断片段"的流水线集成，可作为后续类别特定自动修复与 AI 智能体自动解决间歇性失败的基础。威胁与未来工作：标签来自正则工具（约 9% 噪声、人工复核发现漏标约 15%）、单一企业外部效度有限、Optuna 调参次数有限；未来方向包括多标签分类以处理语义重叠的失败模式、细化类别定义以消歧、引入基础设施日志/遥测等互补数据源，以及利用预测结果与高亮片段驱动 AI 智能体实施类别特定的自动修复。

Abstract: In principle, Continuous Integration (CI) pipeline failures provide valuable feedback to developers on code-related errors. In practice, however, pipeline jobs often fail intermittently due to non-deterministic tests, network outages, infrastructure failures, resource exhaustion, and other reliability issues. These intermittent (flaky) job failures lead to substantial inefficiencies: wasted computational resources from repeated reruns and significant diagnosis time that distracts developers from core activities and often requires intervention from specialized teams. Prior work has proposed machine learning techniques to detect intermittent failures, but does not address the subsequent diagnosis challenge. To fill this gap, we introduce FlaXifyer, a few-shot learning approach for predicting intermittent job failure categories using pre-trained language models. FlaXifyer requires only job execution logs and achieves 84.3% Macro F1 and 92.0% Top-2 accuracy with just 12 labeled examples per category. We also propose LogSift, an interpretability technique that identifies influential log statements in under one second, reducing review effort by 74.4% while surfacing relevant failure information in 87% of cases. Evaluation on 2,458 job failures from TELUS demonstrates that FlaXifyer and LogSift enable effective automated triage, accelerate failure diagnosis, and pave the way towards the automated resolution of intermittent job failures.

</details>


### [193] [PriviSense: A Frida-Based Framework for Multi-Sensor Spoofing on Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22414)
*Ibrahim Khalilov,Chaoran Chen,Ziang Xiao,Tianshi Li,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Yaxing Yao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PriviSense 是一个基于 Frida 的 Android 端运行时伪造工具：在已 root 的真机上通过注入 JavaScript 钩子，实时改写加速度计、陀螺仪、计步器等传感器流以及电量、系统时间、设备元数据等系统值，无需模拟器、无需重打包应用，即可对未修改的真实应用做可复现的上下文行为测试与"行为审计"（behavioral auditing）。已在 5 个传感器可视化应用上验证实时伪造有效，代码不公开，仅向经过验证的研究者按需提供。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
