**真实数据**（法兰西岛交通网络：INSEE 市镇社会经济指标 + IDFM GTFS，1266 个市镇、20 维属性，构建含容量/时间的加权贡献边；GTFS 站→市镇空间连接）：阶段 1 潜空间呈 5 个由"曲率墙"分隔的区域，经代理决策树解释为社会经济层级（0 农村基线、1 城郊居住、2 高密度城区、3 大都市中心如巴黎、4 生产型节点）。聚合后的簇间交互图显示：Cluster 3 是几何张力主要来源（内部平均 Z=16.2，与 Cluster 2/4 呈强正 Z 红色边），即公共交通制造了把 socio-economically 差异区"拉近"的结构捷径（hub-and-spoke），定量印证 Francilien 网络的放射状拓扑；而 Cluster 0/1 以负 Z 为主（自相互作用 -3.8 与 -2.7），说明外围横向连通不足、banlieue 与 banlieue 之间缺少直达线路，量化了可达性鸿沟。直接用属性或成对相似度回归交通便利度的 R² 仅 0.003/0.0031，说明该信息只能通过异质的几何失配被提取。

**局限**：作者明确该框架不是可扩展/预测型架构，定位为诊断探针（类似谱/扩散类探索方法），适合中等规模图或大图子采样；测地计算昂贵；网格方法受维度灾难限制（|G|=(P+1)^d，内存 O(d²P^d)），高维依赖线性插值近似；需要可扩展、可微的测地估计器。作者亦指出 VAE 正则化偏置与先验选择会影响潜空间几何，但换用黎曼布朗运动等更灵活先验收益甚微，说明结构张力对标准高斯先验稳健。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是把属性图表示学习从"强制对齐"范式翻转为"度量并利用失配"的范式：几何失配被形式化为属性黎曼度量与图核扩散度量之间的张力，经由两阶段 VAE 与可微测地近似转化为链接级 Z_ij 与节点级 S_i 的可解释结构描述子，其数值大小直接量化局部同质性/异质性强度。合成实验显示该信号在社区检测与异常识别上大幅超越 VGAE 重构误差、聚类与同配性等基线；真实交通网络实验表明它能揭示常规统计无法证明的结构性不平等（中心城区的连接捷径与外围的横向连通断裂）。作者由此指出现有联合嵌入架构在数学上是误设的（它们强制度量等价而非建模度量张力），呼吁转向显式推理几何不相容性的架构；同时强调该方法是可解释性优先的诊断工具而非可扩展的预测模型，未来需解决可扩展可微测地估计这一关键空白。

Abstract: The standard approach to representation learning on attributed graphs -- i.e., simultaneously reconstructing node attributes and graph structure -- is geometrically flawed, as it merges two potentially incompatible metric spaces. This forces a destructive alignment that erodes information about the graph's underlying generative process. To recover this lost signal, we introduce a custom variational autoencoder that separates manifold learning from structural alignment. By quantifying the metric distortion needed to map the attribute manifold onto the graph's Heat Kernel, we transform geometric conflict into an interpretable structural descriptor. Experiments show our method uncovers connectivity patterns and anomalies undetectable by conventional approaches, proving both their theoretical inadequacy and practical limitations.

</details>


### [257] [Game-Theoretic Co-Evolution for LLM-Based Heuristic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22896)
*Xinyi Ke,Kai Li,Junliang Xing,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ASRO框架，将启发式发现重构为求解器和实例生成器之间的程序级协同演化，通过博弈论方法解决静态评估导致的过拟合问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动启发式发现方法主要局限于对固定实例分布的静态评估，容易导致过拟合和在分布偏移下的泛化能力差

Method: ASRO框架将启发式发现建模为双人零和博弈，维护双方增长的策略池，通过基于LLM的最佳响应oracle迭代扩展策略池，对抗混合对手元策略，用自适应的自我生成课程替代静态评估

Result: 在多个组合优化领域，ASRO始终优于基于相同程序搜索机制的静态训练AHD基线，在多样化和分布外实例上实现了显著改进的泛化能力和鲁棒性

Conclusion: ASRO通过博弈论的协同演化框架，有效解决了自动启发式发现中的静态评估限制，提升了方法的泛化能力和适应性

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled rapid progress in automatic heuristic discovery (AHD), yet most existing methods are predominantly limited by static evaluation against fixed instance distributions, leading to potential overfitting and poor generalization under distributional shifts. We propose Algorithm Space Response Oracles (ASRO), a game-theoretic framework that reframes heuristic discovery as a program level co-evolution between solver and instance generator. ASRO models their interaction as a two-player zero-sum game, maintains growing strategy pools on both sides, and iteratively expands them via LLM-based best-response oracles against mixed opponent meta-strategies, thereby replacing static evaluation with an adaptive, self-generated curriculum. Across multiple combinatorial optimization domains, ASRO consistently outperforms static-training AHD baselines built on the same program search mechanisms, achieving substantially improved generalization and robustness on diverse and out-of-distribution instances.

</details>


### [258] [MulFeRL: Enhancing Reinforcement Learning with Verbal Feedback in a Multi-turn Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22900)
*Xuancheng Li,Haitao Li,Yujia Zhou,YiqunLiu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MulFeRL 是一个多轮、事件触发的 RLVR 框架：在 GRPO 式训练中，只对「整组 rollout 全部验证失败」的低信号样本触发自然语言 verbal feedback，用反馈作为条件引导重新生成（progress induction），再通过验证器确认的进展把反馈转化为可优化信号——组内对比用 GRPO，Failed→Solved 的跨状态对比用新提出的 Feedback-Contrastive Optimization (FCO)，反馈以固定 <feedback> 槽位结构化注入推理过程。在 OpenR1-Math 采样 4k 数据上训练，两个骨干模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Base、Qwen3-4B-Inst）上均超过 SFT/RAFT/CITL-FT、SDPO、GRPO/Dr.GRPO/Critique-GRPO/RLTF-FM 等基线，并具备跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）在数学、代码等可验证领域已广泛用于提升推理能力，但其仅依赖最终结果对错标量奖励。随着模型变强，收益递减甚至进入平台期，原因在于标量奖励稀疏且信息量不足：特别是在困难样本上，K 条 rollout 全部验证失败时，奖励全部相同（0 或 1），GRPO 的组内相对优势归零，既无法区分候选、也无法提供任何策略梯度（论文命题 1 给出证明），而这些恰恰是最需要学习的样本，导致信用分配差、梯度噪声大、样本效率低、训练停滞。

现有利用 verbal feedback 的工作各有局限：（1）把反馈直接压缩成标量奖励或改写成监督目标，丢失细粒度诊断信息；（2）自蒸馏方法用反馈条件化的“特权教师”做分布匹配，会过度压缩不确定性与自我纠错轨迹，损害稳健推理；（3）RL 风格的反馈方法（Critique-GRPO、RLTF-FM）通常把反馈“广撒”到所有样本上，而成功轨迹本已具备结果信息，反馈在真正需要它的全失败样本上被稀释。此外，人类在出错时依赖语言反馈定位错误、给出细粒度修正指引，这启发了本文的核心问题：如何把 verbal feedback 转化为在失败样本上仍能驱动 RL 训练前进的稳定学习信号。

Method: 核心原则：反馈只改变采样条件（conditioning），学习信号始终由验证器把关（verifier gating）。

1) 奖励状态定义：对每个提示的 rollout 组 G^(t)（K 条），按验证器奖励和分为 Solved（全部正确）、Contrastive（部分正确）、Failed（全部失败）三态。

2) 进展诱导（progress induction）：仅当当前组为 Failed 时，向反馈提供者 F_ψ（可为人、用户模拟器或策略自身；实验中用 GPT-4o）请求组级反馈；长 rollout 采用两步聚合——先对 4 个子组分别生成反馈，再合并为一条（Issue / Fix steps 结构，禁止泄露最终答案），注入下一轮上下文 c^(t+1)=Inject(x, f^(t)) 后重新采样 K 条 rollout。若状态变为 Contrastive 或 Solved 即视为反馈诱导的进展，否则继续迭代直到 turn budget（实验中 1 轮初始 + 最多 2 轮反馈重生成）耗尽。

3) 进展信用分配（progress credit assignment）：在首个“信息性轮次” t* 上按对比所在位置路由——Contrastive 组用标准 clipped GRPO（同上下文内组内优势 + KL 惩罚）；Failed→Solved 转换用 FCO：以长度归一化的参考归一化得分 s_θ(y;c)=|y|^{-1}[log π_θ(y|c) − log π_ref(y|c)]，对 (y+, y−) 构造对偶损失 −log σ(τ[D_θ − γ])，D_θ 为正负样本得分差；负样本来自紧邻的前一个全失败组，正样本来自当前全成功组。正负组分别用随机置换配对（Proposition 4 证明该置换估计对全 K² 对的平均是无偏的），注入的反馈 token 在似然目标中被 mask 掉但仍保留在条件上下文中（canonicalization）。首轮即 Solved 或全程 Failed 的提示不给更新。

4) 结构化反馈注入：借鉴检索增强生成的 passage injection，把反馈放入推理模板开头固定的 <thinking><feedback>…</feedback> 槽位（首轮为空），使反馈成为显式条件干预且 token 可识别、可屏蔽；奖励 1 要求答案正确且格式合规。

5) 理论分析（附录 B）：命题 1（奖励齐次组 GRPO 优势为零）；引理 1（反馈后“逃逸”概率 1−(1−q_t)^K，且 q_t>p_t 时严格增大）；命题 2/3（GRPO 与 FCO 的局部梯度方向同为“提升正确样本相对错误样本的似然”，但分别作用于同上下文组内对比与相邻跨上下文对比）；并给出 FCO 相比 GRPO 额外恢复的概率质量 q_t^K（反馈诱导的全成功组）。作者明确声明不提供全局单调改进保证。

Result: 主结果（Pass@1，5 次独立运行均值，verl 框架，训练数据为 OpenR1-Math-220k 采样 4k，评测为 AMC23/AIME24/OlympiadBench/MATH-500/Minerva-Math 内域 + MMLU-Pro/GPQA-Diamond/TheoremQA 外域）：
- Qwen2.5-7B-Base：MulFeRL 平均 46.26，最佳强基线 Critique-GRPO 42.83；AMC23 50.00、AIME24 24.00、MATH-500 78.72、Minerva 37.87、GPQA 43.08。
- Qwen3-4B-Inst：MulFeRL 平均 68.36 vs Critique-GRPO 63.65；AMC23 90.50、AIME24 68.00、MATH-500 90.24、Minerva 55.96、TheoremQA 55.75。
- 5 次运行标准差与基线相当，提升稳定（附录 F.1）。

消融（Qwen2.5-7B-Base）：去掉重生成退化最大（AIME24 −8.00，MATH500 −7.88），去掉 FCO −4.33/−2.60，去掉结构化注入 −3.33/−1.04，说明三组件均必要。

受控对比：统一为 MulFeRL 的结构化输出格式后，GRPO/SDPO/Critique-GRPO/RLTF-FM 均下降（如 Qwen3-4B-Inst 上 GRPO 61.24→58.53），说明增益不来自格式或教师访问；在近似相同 GPT-4o API 成本下，GPT-4o 蒸馏基线仍弱于 MulFeRL（66.22 vs 68.36），说明增益源于“反馈+验证器→RL 监督”的使用方式而非单纯蒸馏强模型输出。

反馈模拟器分析：Qwen3-4B-Inst 上平均分 Qwen3-1.7B 61.32 < 自反馈 65.62 < GPT-4o-mini 66.84 < GPT-4o 68.36，模拟器越强越好，但自反馈已具竞争力。

测试时多轮反馈（TheoremQA）：轮数增加各方法均提升，MulFeRL 始终最优，后期收益递减（受反馈模拟器能力限制）。训练成本：因多轮重生成，MulFeRL 单步开销高于单轮 RL 基线（早期失败多、重生成触发频繁时最明显），但所需优化步数更少，可用早停/退火式重生成降本；turn budget 消融显示 1–2 轮即可捕获大部分收益。

鲁棒性：outcome-only（二元判定）反馈显著弱于完整 verbal 反馈；错误判定与损坏诊断会降低性能（去除结构化注入时下降更大），说明结构化注入可提升反馈利用的可靠性并抑制误导性反馈。

Conclusion: MulFeRL 论证并实现了「反馈只做条件干预、信用分配由验证器把关」这一设计：在 RLVR 中，把 verbal feedback 施加于全失败的低信号组，通过反馈引导重生成把失败样本转化为验证器认证的进展，再用 GRPO（同上下文组内对比）与 FCO（Failed→Solved 跨上下文对比）两种互补目标把这种进展转化为梯度，从而打破稀疏标量奖励造成的训练平台期。实验显示其在两个骨干、内外域基准上稳定优于监督、自蒸馏与各类 RL/反馈基线，理论部分解释了为何需要 FCO 及其恢复的概率质量。

局限与未来方向：多轮重生成带来额外解码与验证开销（失败率高或用强模拟器时更明显），可用更便宜模拟器、早停与退火重生成缓解；效果依赖可靠的结果验证与有用反馈；目前仅使用 rollout 级反馈与保守触发策略、跨轮偏好对用简单配对规则，可能遗漏“部分正确/接近正确”rollout 中更细粒度的改进，未来可扩展到步骤级/过程级反馈、自适应触发与更丰富的信用分配机制；同时论文只给出局部、参考锚定的优化论证，不构成全局稳定性或单调改进保证。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is widely used to improve reasoning in multiple domains, yet outcome-only scalar rewards are often sparse and uninformative, especially on failed samples, where they merely indicate failure and provide no insight into why the reasoning fails. In this paper, we investigate how to leverage richer verbal feedback to guide RLVR training on failed samples, and how to convert such feedback into a trainable learning signal. Specifically, we propose a multi-turn feedback-guided reinforcement learning framework. It builds on three mechanisms: (1) dynamic multi-turn regeneration guided by feedback, triggered only on failed samples, (2) two complementary learning signals for within-turn and cross-turn optimization, and (3) structured feedback injection into the model's reasoning process. Trained on sampled OpenR1-Math, the approach outperforms supervised fine-tuning and RLVR baselines in-domain and generalizes well out-of-domain.

</details>


### [259] [Alignment among Language, Vision and Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22948)
*Nicola Milano,Stefano Nolfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，语言、视觉和动作学习在表征几何上存在跨模态对齐，支持模态无关的语义组织假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同学习模态（语言、视觉、动作）是否生成相似的表征几何，传统观点认为不同数据类型会生成专门化、不可迁移的表征，但近期证据表明可能存在意外收敛。

Method: 在BabyAI平台上通过行为克隆训练基于Transformer的智能体，生成仅由感觉运动控制需求塑造的动作接地语言表征，并与LLaMA、Qwen、DeepSeek、BERT、CLIP、BLIP等模型表征进行比较。

Result: 动作表征与仅解码器语言模型和BLIP对齐强烈（P@15: 0.70-0.73），接近语言模型之间的对齐程度，与CLIP和BERT的对齐显著较弱。

Conclusion: 语言、视觉和动作表征向部分共享的语义结构收敛，支持模态无关的语义组织，突显了在具身AI系统中跨领域迁移的潜力。

Abstract: A fundamental question in cognitive science and AI concerns whether different learning modalities: language, vision, and action, give rise to distinct or shared internal representations. Traditional views assume that models trained on different data types develop specialized, non-transferable representations. However, recent evidence suggests unexpected convergence: models optimized for distinct tasks may develop similar representational geometries. We investigate whether this convergence extends to embodied action learning by training a transformer-based agent to execute goal-directed behaviors in response to natural language instructions. Using behavioral cloning on the BabyAI platform, we generated action-grounded language embeddings shaped exclusively by sensorimotor control requirements. We then compared these representations with those extracted from state-of-the-art large language models (LLaMA, Qwen, DeepSeek, BERT) and vision-language models (CLIP, BLIP). Despite substantial differences in training data, modality, and objectives, we observed robust cross-modal alignment. Action representations aligned strongly with decoder-only language models and BLIP (precision@15: 0.70-0.73), approaching the alignment observed among language models themselves. Alignment with CLIP and BERT was significantly weaker. These findings indicate that linguistic, visual, and action representations converge toward partially shared semantic structures, supporting modality-independent semantic organization and highlighting potential for cross-domain transfer in embodied AI systems.

</details>


### [260] [EvoClinician: A Self-Evolving Agent for Multi-Turn Medical Diagnosis via Test-Time Evolutionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22964)
*Yufei He,Juncheng Liu,Zhiyuan Hu,Yulin Chen,Yue Liu,Yuan Sui,Yibo Li,Nuo Chen,Jun Hu,Bryan Hooi,Xinxing Xu,Jiang Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两项工作：（1）Med-Inquire——一个交互式多轮医学诊断基准，将真实病例的完整病历隐藏在 Patient 与 Examination 两个"守门人"代理之后，强制智能体逐轮主动问诊、开检查，并同时度量诊断正确度、交互轮数与资源成本；（2）EvoClinician——一个测试时自进化智能体，其核心是"Diagnose–Grade–Evolve"循环：Actor 尝试诊断，Process Grader 对每个动作做临床产出与资源效率双维度打分（HIGH_YIELD/LOW_YIELD/INEFFICIENT/CRITICAL_ERROR）以解决长程信用分配，Evolver 再据此无梯度地演化提示规则与外部记忆。实验表明 EvoClinician 在四个骨干模型上均优于静态提示、记忆类、提示优化类与既有自进化基线，在提高诊断分的同时显著降低检查成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗 AI 普遍遵循不现实的"一次性"范式：把包含病史、体征与检验结果的完整病历一次性喂给模型直接输出诊断，而真实临床是医生从主诉出发，顺序问诊、查体、开检查，在准确性与时间/费用约束之间做策略性权衡的过程。已有基准（MedIQ、SDBench 及各类选择题式评测）主要只测最终诊断准确率，既不奖励也不量化智能体的信息收集效率，因而无法刻画"该问什么、开什么检查、何时停止"这一专家级过程能力。此外，自进化智能体与强化学习方法在长程、反馈延迟的任务上普遍缺乏动作级信用分配：稀疏的最终奖励难以归因到早期的具体问询或检查动作，而现有记忆/提示优化方法只能复用信息或改写指令，不能稳定改变行动策略，且缺少兼顾资源成本的机制。

Method: 基准 Med-Inquire：基于 DiagnosisArena 的 915 例真实病例（NEJM 等 10 本期刊病例报告），建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），隐藏状态为完整病历。初始只给病例摘要；Patient 代理仅依据隐藏病历回答症状/病史提问，病历缺项时生成与诊断及已披露信息一致的"合理但未证实"回答并缓存以避免自相矛盾；Examination 代理返回查体与检查结果，病历中不存在该项则返回 NOT AVAILABLE（不编造）。动作空间为 AskQuestion（自由文本提问）、OrderTest（以文本提出检查名，动作空间开放，不给固定清单）、SubmitDiagnosis（结束回合）；设固定最大轮数 T_max 并记录完整 transcript。成本模型为每轮基础交互成本 + 每项检查的测试成本，总成本 C=Σc(a_t)，以归一化单位报告，可用带别名的版本化成本表替换。Judge 代理按 0–100 分级 rubric（90–100 正确/同义词、70–89 临床极近、40–69 部分正确、10–39 大多错误、0–9 不相容或不安全）打分。指标三轴：诊断分 S̄、平均轮数 T̄、平均成本 C̄。方法 EvoClinician：测试时学习设定下（不更新基座权重，仅在回合间更新提示与记忆），三角色组成循环。Actor 持有系统提示 P 与外部记忆 M，每回合检索 top-k 相似记忆（查询仅取当前对话历史以避免未来信息泄漏），输出规定 JSON 格式动作，并需维护 2–6 项鉴别诊断、偏好先低成本高信息量步骤。Process Grader 在回合结束后读取 transcript、最终 Judge 分 S 与成本报告，对每一动作输出标签与 1–3 句理由（双标准：临床产出=是否降低诊断不确定性；资源效率=是否可用更低成本/更少步骤取得同等信息），并要求避免后见偏差，附一段 session summary。Evolver 做无梯度更新：提示演化——把 HIGH_YIELD 动作抽象为通用可操作规则（如"先问某症状后再开影像"），把 INEFFICIENT/CRITICAL_ERROR 写成防护规则，受规则预算 B_P 约束、超预算时按近期被引用频次删除或合并低效用规则，禁止写入病例特异标识；记忆演化——对强反馈回合写入条目 m=(φ(h_t), a_t, o_{t+1}, g_t, r_t)，φ 为压缩的临床情境表示（关键症状、时间病程、已知检查），保留正负经验，超预算 B_M 时按 keep_score=α·log(1+times_retrieved)−β·age 删除。附录 K 给出理论视角：将 Process Grader 视为优势函数 Â_t=Q(h_t,a_t)−V(h_t) 的估计量，以信息增益/成本 η_t=IG_t/(c(a_t)+ε) 与阈值规则 (α_0, α_1, β) 解释四类标签。基线：Static Prompt；记忆类 Memory Agent、RAG、Mem0、Evo-Memory；提示优化类 EvoPrompt、GEPA；自进化类 EvoTest。骨干：gemini-3-pro、gpt-5.1、gpt-4o-mini、MedGemma-27b，固定案例顺序、相同 T_max 与成本表，仅回合间允许更新。

Result: 主结果（915 例均值）：EvoClinician 在四个骨干上均居首——gemini-3-pro 59.8 分/9.1 轮/1275 成本，gpt-5.1 54.2/9.3/1310，gpt-4o-mini 48.0/9.6/1345，MedGemma-27b 51.1/9.5/1328；相对 Static Prompt 提升 +11.6/+10.6/+9.1/+10.1，且均优于最强基线 EvoTest（57.9/1605、52.5/1650、46.6/1690、49.6/1670）与 EvoPrompt（53.6）、Mem0（51.2）。三点观察：(1) 跨回合自更新不仅改善最终诊断，也改善信息收集策略，纯检索类方法增益有限；(2) 各方法轮数都集中在 9–11 轮，而成本差异显著（EvoTest 与 EvoClinician 轮数接近但成本 1605 vs 1275），说明成本主要由检查选择（是否过早开昂贵确证检查）驱动，而非对话长度；(3) 骨干越强增益越大，提示自适应依赖基本的推理与指令遵循能力。时间效率（gemini-3-pro）：EvoClinician 单例 17.1 s（诊断 11.0 s + 更新 6.1 s），快于 EvoTest 的 18.9 s，静态基线 11.7 s 且无更新开销。消融（gemini-3-pro）显示：去掉动作级评分降至 54.6/1405（信用分配是主要收益来源）；仅提示演化 56.7/1315、仅记忆演化 55.3/1390，二者互补且须结合；仅按产出评分（不惩罚成本）可达 58.6 但成本升至 1510，属"以过度检查换准确率"；仅保留正记忆 58.9、删除负向记忆 57.8，说明 CRITICAL_ERROR 等负向条目作为约束具有独立价值。学习曲线显示前 50–100 例运行均值方差大、曲线交叉，之后 EvoClinician 的分数运行均值稳步上升且成本下降幅度最大，而仅做提示优化或记忆检索的方法更早进入平台期。

Conclusion: 结论：Med-Inquire 把"多轮、主动、按需获取信息"的诊断策略纳入可复现评测，同时量化正确性与资源代价；EvoClinician 证明测试时（不改动基座权重）用动作级、成本感知的反馈做信用分配，可以稳定改善诊断质量并同时降低资源消耗，其核心增益来自动作级评分带来的更细粒度归因，提示规则演化与外部记忆演化承担互补角色（前者提供通用规则、后者覆盖罕见情境），保留负向经验对避免重复错误至关重要，而评分必须同时纳入成本维度以防止"过度检查换准确率"。同时该方法可迁移到客服、金融等"工具调用+成本约束+延迟反馈"的通用场景（构建带工具与约束的交互环境、定义任务级分数与资源报告、再用动作级批评更新规则与记忆）。局限与风险：Med-Inquire 为模拟环境，Patient 代理会生成病例报告未记录但合理的细节，因此分数只反映模拟内表现，不能证明临床安全性或可用性；评分由模型判定，可能带来偏差，测试时更新在评分噪声下可能发生难预测的行为漂移，需固定预算、确定性解码与审计机制；成本表只是相对资源代理、不对应真实计费，成本结论不可作账单参考；任何真实应用都需临床验证、监控与严格的安全防护。

Abstract: Prevailing medical AI operates on an unrealistic ''one-shot'' model, diagnosing from a complete patient file. However, real-world diagnosis is an iterative inquiry where Clinicians sequentially ask questions and order tests to strategically gather information while managing cost and time. To address this, we first propose Med-Inquire, a new benchmark designed to evaluate an agent's ability to perform multi-turn diagnosis. Built upon a dataset of real-world clinical cases, Med-Inquire simulates the diagnostic process by hiding a complete patient file behind specialized Patient and Examination agents. They force the agent to proactively ask questions and order tests to gather information piece by piece. To tackle the challenges posed by Med-Inquire, we then introduce EvoClinician, a self-evolving agent that learns efficient diagnostic strategies at test time. Its core is a ''Diagnose-Grade-Evolve'' loop: an Actor agent attempts a diagnosis; a Process Grader agent performs credit assignment by evaluating each action for both clinical yield and resource efficiency; finally, an Evolver agent uses this feedback to update the Actor's strategy by evolving its prompt and memory. Our experiments show EvoClinician outperforms continual learning baselines and other self-evolving agents like memory agents. The code is available at https://github.com/yf-he/EvoClinician

</details>


### [261] [Golden Goose: A Simple Trick to Synthesize Unlimited RLVR Tasks from Unverifiable Internet Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22975)
*Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Jaehun Jung,Jian Hu,Di Zhang,Shizhe Diao,Yunheng Zou,Shaokun Zhang,Brandon Cui,Mingjie Liu,Hyunwoo Kim,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出名为「Golden Goose」的极简数据合成流程：把互联网中不可验证但推理密集的文本改造成「填空任务的四选一/多选题版本」，从而无限量生产可自动验证的 RLVR 任务。作者据此构建了 GooseReason-0.7M（超 70 万条，覆盖数学、编程、STEM），该数据能把已在既有 RLVR 数据上饱和的模型「救活」，在 15 个基准上让 1.5B/4B-Instruct 模型取得新 SOTA；并将其落地到无开源 RLVR 数据的网络安全领域（GooseReason-Cyber，18 万条），4B 模型超越 7B 领域专精模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 已成为激发 LLM 复杂推理的核心手段，但其扩展受限于「可验证数据」的稀缺：现有流程只能使用能被数学验证器解析或带单元测试的题目，人工标注昂贵且领域狭窄，程序化合成环境又难超数百个。有限数据上的持续 RL 收益迅速饱和——ProRL-1.5B-v2 训练超 2 万 H100 GPU 小时后，136K 样本中仅约 25% 仍能提供学习信号（其余全对或全错）；对更强的 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 而言，300 步后性能即停滞甚至退化。大量推理密集但不可验证的语料（如理科教材 QA、缺少测试用例的编程题、AoPS 定理证明）被传统 RLVR 数据构建所排除，成为可挖掘的空白。

Method: 核心洞察：将 FIM（fill-in-the-middle）任务改造成多选题（MCQ）形式即可把不可验证文本转为可自动验证的 RLVR 任务。流程为：给定源文本 S，用最强 LLM（GPT-5）识别一段连续的关键推理步骤 t，将其在 S 中替换为 [MASK] 得到 S_mask，以 t 为标准答案，再生成一组风格相近、貌似合理但错误的干扰项 D={d1..dk}，最终构成题目 Q=(S_mask, {t}∪D)，选项随机排序，RL 验证只需比对答案是否等于真值选项。对噪声源（如 FineWeb 网络安全网页），先让 LLM 抽取/摘要 100–600 词的有教育价值段落，若无合适段落则返回空串；再基于清洗文本构造掩码与干扰项，并对学生模型在 16 次 rollout 中全部正确的「过易」样本做难度过滤。源语料：推理域用 AoPS-Instruct（约 60 万 QA）、rStar-Coder 的 synthetic_sft 分裂（37.7K 无测试用例的编程题）、MegaScience（65 万 QA，1.2 万本大学教材），共合成 GooseReason-0.7M；网络安全域用 FineWeb 抓取文本合成 GooseReason-Cyber（约 18 万条）。设计选择上：放弃开放式填空 + LLM 裁判方案（需额外托管裁判模型，且重度 RL 模型倾向从零解题、忽略填充指令，83% 样本全错）；干扰项数量需足够多（9 选项时超 70% 样本落在「有对有错」的中等难度区间；仅 3 选项时模型转向排除法、题目过易）。RL 算法采用 ProRLv2（REINFORCE++ 式解耦优势归一化的裁剪 GRPO 变体），可与任意 RLVR 算法兼容。

Result: 1）数据效率：GooseReason-0.7M 保留约 70% 的「有效样本」比例，而 ProRL 数据仅约 25%；数学/代码/STEM 有效样本净增超 45 万，为 ProRL 有效样本总量的 13 倍。2）饱和后再训：ProRL-1.5B-v2 上续训 1,100 H100 GPU 小时，数学 2.71% vs 0.63%、代码 2.12% vs 0.95%、STEM 3.48% vs 0.13%；对比 RLVE（数学强但 STEM 仅 +0.62%）。3）Qwen3-4B-Instruct 续训：原 ProRL 数据在 333 步后数学 -1.29%、代码 +0.43%、STEM -1.52%，加 GooseReason 后为 +2.18%/+2.24%/+2.40%，得到 GooseReason-4B-Instruct，在 15 个基准上居 4B-Instruct 类 SOTA，可与 7.5 倍大的 Qwen3-30B-Instruct 相当甚至更优（AIME24 70.00、Math 平均 73.83、代码平均 55.70、GPQA 66.79、IFEval 76.39、Reasoning Gym 81.28）；在未被数据覆盖的逻辑推理任务上也有迁移提升。4）固定算力（200 步从头训）下联合训练优于仅用 ProRL 数据。5）网络安全：仅 100 步 RL 训练即带来 3 个基准平均 +4.44% 绝对提升，GooseReason-Cyber-4B 平均 78.99（CTI-MCQ 73.79、CyberMetric 92.05、SecEval 71.14），超过 Llama-Primus-Instruct/Merged（约 67–68）。

Conclusion: Golden Goose 证明了一个「简单技巧」即可把海量推理密集但不可验证的互联网文本转化为高质量可验证 RLVR 任务，从而突破既有 RLVR 数据的饱和瓶颈：既能让已饱和模型持续获益，也能在固定算力下更高效地训练 RL，并首次把 RLVR 引入缺乏数据的网络安全等专业领域、超越大规模领域专精模型。其局限与注意事项包括：依赖 LLM 合成可能继承源语料的偏见与不良内容，网络安全能力存在双重用途风险，且当前主要面向文本型 MCQ 验证。作者展望将该范式推广到法律、医学等可验证数据稀缺但专业文献丰富的领域。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a cornerstone for unlocking complex reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet, scaling up RL is bottlenecked by limited existing verifiable data, where improvements increasingly saturate over prolonged training. To overcome this, we propose Golden Goose, a simple trick to synthesize unlimited RLVR tasks from unverifiable internet text by constructing a multiple-choice question-answering version of the fill-in-the-middle task. Given a source text, we prompt an LLM to identify and mask key reasoning steps, then generate a set of diverse, plausible distractors. This enables us to leverage reasoning-rich unverifiable corpora typically excluded from prior RLVR data construction (e.g., science textbooks) to synthesize GooseReason-0.7M, a large-scale RLVR dataset with over 0.7 million tasks spanning mathematics, programming, and general scientific domains. Empirically, GooseReason effectively revives models saturated on existing RLVR data, yielding robust, sustained gains under continuous RL and achieving new state-of-the-art results for 1.5B and 4B-Instruct models across 15 diverse benchmarks. Finally, we deploy Golden Goose in a real-world setting, synthesizing RLVR tasks from raw FineWeb scrapes for the cybersecurity domain, where no prior RLVR data exists. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on the resulting data GooseReason-Cyber sets a new state-of-the-art in cybersecurity, surpassing a 7B domain-specialized model with extensive domain-specific pre-training and post-training. This highlights the potential of automatically scaling up RLVR data by exploiting abundant, reasoning-rich, unverifiable internet text.

</details>


### [262] [Quantifying Model Uniqueness in Heterogeneous AI Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22977)
*Lei You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出「原地准实验设计」(ISQED)框架，用「同源匹配干预」把模型的内在身份（Type-A）与输入干预（Type-B）分离，并以「同伴不可表达残差」(PIER)量化目标模型相对于同伴凸包的行为唯一性；配套的 DISCO 估计器具有一致性、渐近正态性与有限样本设计误差界，并给出主动审计的最小极大最优样本复杂度 dσ²γ⁻²log(Nd/δ)。同时证明：仅凭观察性日志唯一性不可识别；Shapley 值无法检测冗余。实验在 LLM（SST-2）、视觉（ImageNet）与 31 城交通预测生态系统中验证了该框架可用于模型去留、剪枝整合与替代方案审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统已从孤立模型演变为由基础模型与各类适配器组成的异质生态系统，平台需要判断某个模型是否带来真正的新行为、是否只是既有模型的重参数化（如可被路由替代），以便下线冗余模型、谈判许可、评估增量风险。既有 XAI 工具（LIME/SHAP/IG/CKA/Counterfactual）只解释单模型内部逻辑，不回答「该模型在生态中是否可被替换」；而多模型归因方法（如 Shapley 值）是为公平分配收益设计，并不刻画冗余。更根本地，若只有各模型在不同流量策略下产生的观察性日志，唯一性能否被估计在数学上并不明确。

Method: 框架：在固定上下文下定义干预映射 T(θ,x)、标量化响应 Y_j(x,θ)=g(f_j(T(θ,x)))、拟合设计 P_fit 与评估设计 P_eval（诚实性要求二者独立、拟合样本不用于评估）。在 L²(P_fit) 希尔伯特空间中定义凸同伴表达类 C={h_w: w∈Δ^{|J_{-t}|-1}}，将目标响应 Y_t 正交投影到 C 的闭包得 w*，PIER R_t(x,θ)=Y_t−w*^TΦ_{-t}，唯一性泛函 U_t=E_{P_eval}[|R_t|]。估计器 DISCO：在单纯形约束下求解带正则（λ_m→0, mλ_m→0）的岭型最小二乘得 ŵ，在独立评估样本上计算平均绝对残差 Û_t。理论：(1) 一致性——经验残差在单纯形上一致收敛 + M-估计量 argmin 定理 + 连续映射定理 + 支配收敛；(2) 渐近正态性——δ 方法与 M-估计量联合渐近分布；(3) 有限样本设计误差界 |R̂_anchor−R_t| ≤ C_Φ‖ŵ−w*‖₂+2L_x r_x+2L_θ r_θ，将估计误差与锚点半径/剂量网格分辨率分离；(4) 局部线性结构模型 Y_j=φ(x,θ)^Tβ_j 下的主动审计：无噪声时 d 次查询即可精确恢复系数并将唯一性化为系数空间凸可行性判定，有噪声下重复采样 r≥Cσ²γ⁻²log(Nd/δ) 即可以概率 1−δ 区分 γ=0 与 γ>0（每模型总查询 dr）；(5) 一维情形下用 Bretagnolle–Huber 不等式给出最小极大下界 σ²γ⁻²log(1/δ)，并证明检测与估计之间无速率分离；(6) 单调性（Hilbert 空间投影到更大闭凸集残差范数不增，U_t(J₂)≤U_t(J₁)）与保守性（U^ker ≤ U^lin ≤ U^conv）；(7) 观察性不可识别性——构造两个生态在所有样本量下产生相同的观察日志联合分布但唯一性分别为 0 与正；(8) Shapley 反例——两模型响应完全相同却各得 Shapley 值 1/2 而 PIER 恒为零；(9) 附录 D 构造同 U_t 但 PIER 沿剂量轴 Lipschitz 常数相差任意大的两个目标，说明标量分数无法区分「能力型唯一」与「扰动敏感型唯一」。

Result: 仿真：主动审计在查询预算超过特征维 d 后误差率骤降，被动策略需多一个数量级查询；5% 审计误差下主动方案节省 1.24× 查询，与理论斜率吻合。玩具生态中 Shapley 给冗余克隆模型高信用、忽略利基专家，而 PIER 正确给出冗余模型近零唯一性、专家最高唯一性。生态饱和：当同伴数 N 超过任务内在维度 d=10 后平均 PIER 崩塌。LLM 案例（SST-2，掩码强度 θ 为应力坐标，5 模型交叉审计）：唯一性强烈依赖剂量；DistilBERT 在中剂量出现冗余窗口，ALBERT 在低剂量 PIER 最大后衰减；oracle 路由在 θ=0 仍无法匹配 ALBERT（说明残差是真实的凸包外唯一性而非混合选择不当），而在 θ=0.5 处 DISCO 凸路由可 uniformly 低误差替换各目标；上下文指纹（句长、否定词）定位残差来源；受控目标（逐位复制的 DistilBERT Clone、RoBERTa 架构分歧、微调 DistilBERT 参数分歧）分别呈现近零、稳定非零、高应力骤升的 PIER 轨迹。视觉案例（ResNet/EfficientNet/ConvNeXt/ViT 及鲁棒、形状偏置变体）：对抗强度 ε 下 ResNet-50 的 PIER 降至 0.55×（攻击下行为趋同），ConvNeXt 放大至 1.2× 以上；形状偏置上下文普遍提升 PIER 但幅度高度不均；ΔPIER 与形状偏置分、ImageNet top-1 均不相关；残差呈重尾，少数样本主导残差质量且 ConvNeXt 与形状偏置 ResNet 共享离群样本；PCA 可视化显示形状偏置下目标落到同伴凸包之外。交通案例（N=31 城 + GLOBAL 模型）：PIER 与替换影响几乎无线性相关（Pearson r=0.05），识别出「冗余可整合」「不可替代」（如 paris）「唯一但不健康」（如 bern，PIER 高但替换反而更准）三类城市；成对代理相对 PIER 的凸性增益 log2(proxy/PIER) 揭示成对比较系统性高估唯一性；高增益城市可用 top-3 稀疏混合解释；唯一性升高时 n_eff 下降（Spearman −0.60）、top-3 质量与 n_eff 几乎完全相关（−0.94）；PIER 引导的剪枝在大部分路径上贴近 oracle 下界、仅在剪到高 PIER 城市时出现拐点，且将首次灾难性最坏情形跳变推迟到更晚，尾部风险显著优于纯效用剪枝。

Conclusion: PIER/DISCO 将「模型在生态中是否独特」变成有明确统计定义的、可估计、可解释的量：唯一性被定义为「无法被任何同侪随机凸组合复现的行为残差」，其投影权重天然构成一份可审计的替代（路由）方案证书，从而把可信 AI 从「解释单个模型」推进到「审计与治理异质模型生态」。核心洞见是：唯一性是相对于受控干预协议与具体上下文的条件性属性，而非模型的静态标签；它与效用、与成对差异、与 Shapley 信用三者相互独立。匹配干预控制在数学上是必要前提而非便利，主动设计则使审计在噪声、唯一性边际与置信度上达到最小极大最优依赖。展望包括：把更强的结构先验纳入表达类但保留保守聚合；自适应上下文选择以把审计预算聚焦于干预空间的关键区域；在低维任务流形假设下给出维度相关的饱和速率；以及向工业规模 LLM、多模态系统的标量化映射、干预族与资源受限下的近似推断扩展。

Abstract: As AI systems evolve from isolated predictors into complex, heterogeneous ecosystems of foundation models and specialized adapters, distinguishing genuine behavioral novelty from functional redundancy becomes a critical governance challenge. Here, we introduce a statistical framework for auditing model uniqueness based on In-Silico Quasi-Experimental Design (ISQED). By enforcing matched interventions across models, we isolate intrinsic model identity and quantify uniqueness as the Peer-Inexpressible Residual (PIER), i.e. the component of a target's behavior strictly irreducible to any stochastic convex combination of its peers, with vanishing PIER characterizing when such a routing-based substitution becomes possible. We establish the theoretical foundations of ecosystem auditing through three key contributions. First, we prove a fundamental limitation of observational logs: uniqueness is mathematically non-identifiable without intervention control. Second, we derive a scaling law for active auditing, showing that our adaptive query protocol achieves minimax-optimal sample efficiency ($dσ^2γ^{-2}\log(Nd/δ)$). Third, we demonstrate that cooperative game-theoretic methods, such as Shapley values, fundamentally fail to detect redundancy. We implement this framework via the DISCO (Design-Integrated Synthetic Control) estimator and deploy it across diverse ecosystems, including computer vision models (ResNet/ConvNeXt/ViT), large language models (BERT/RoBERTa), and city-scale traffic forecasters. These results move trustworthy AI beyond explaining single models: they establish a principled, intervention-based science of auditing and governing heterogeneous model ecosystems.

</details>


### [263] [Why Your Deep Research Agent Fails? On Hallucination Evaluation in Full Research Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22984)
*Yuhao Zhan,Tianyu Fan,Linxuan Huang,Zirui Guo,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从“结果导向”转向“过程感知”的 Deep Research Agent（DRA）幻觉评测范式：提出 PING 幻觉分类体系（Propagation 传播 / Intent 意图 / Noise-induced 噪声诱发 / Grounding 依据），配套细粒度评测框架（将研究轨迹分解为原子子查询、原子动作、原子声明并逐级验证），构建首个面向全轨迹幻觉压力测试的基准 DeepHalluBench（100 题，含 25 道对抗性“无答案”题）。在 Gemini、OpenAI、Perplexity、Qwen、Grok、Salesforce Deep Research 六个 DRA 上的实验表明：无系统具备稳健可靠性，且失败源于系统性机制——幻觉传播与认知偏差（时间“锚定效应”、语义“同质性偏差”），因此改进需依赖架构级干预（早期纠错、记忆管理、注意力去偏），而非单纯扩大检索规模或更换更强基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体（DRA）通过“规划—检索—总结”多轮循环产出长篇研究报告，但其可靠性评估长期停留在结果端：现有基准（如 GAIA、BrowseComp、Mind2Web2、ReportEval、DeepResearch Bench II 等）要么只核对短答案，要么用 rubric 评估长报告，把研究过程当作黑箱。这带来两个根本局限：(1) 幻觉探测不完整——计划阶段的误导性设计、检索阶段的证据忽视等中间幻觉被掩盖，并沿轨迹累积；(2) 性能诊断不透明——无法把失败归因到具体阶段（规划 vs 总结）。要在长程、基于搜索的 agentic 场景中刻画幻觉，还需要解决三个现实障碍：面向 DRA 的幻觉分类体系缺位；商用 DRA 缺乏廉价 API 与结构化日志（只有 Web UI）；多阶段轨迹的一致、可扩展评估难以构建。

Method: 1）分类体系：将轨迹建模为迭代的 plan-search-summarize 循环，提出 PING 分类体系，按失败发生位置划分四类幻觉——Grounding（来源层，摘要阶段编造 Fabrication / 引用错配 Misattribution）、Noise-induced（上下文层，检索到相关证据但忽视，类比 RAG 的 fact omission）、Intent（查询层，规划阶段误解/未满足用户意图，表现为 Restriction Neglect 与 Deviated Actions）、Propagation（轨迹层，后续动作建立在前序幻觉之上）。注：早期版本摘要中的“PIES（Planning/Summarization × Explicit/Implicit）”在此版本已替换为 PING。2）数据获取：用 HTML 解析器 + LLM 将商用 DRA 的非结构化 Web UI 输出还原为标准 plan-search-summarize 循环，并与渲染页面人工比对校验。3）原子化分解：采用基于原子性的两阶段“decompose-then-refine”流程，把用户查询拆为原子子查询（限制）、计划拆为原子动作、总结拆为原子声明（保留引用映射）。4）四类评估器：Grounding 用两轮 verify（retrieve-then-verify + NLI→LLM 级联；Round 2 分支做 Misattribution Check 与 Reflection Check）；Noise 用 UMAP + HDBSCAN 语义聚类，以 P_c = S_c×N_inv/R_c 计算被忽略簇惩罚并对最坏情形归一化；Intent/Propagation 用历史感知的动作验证器判定 deviation 与 propagation，并用 elbow 法核对哪些限制被真正执行。5）基准构建：聚合 Mind2Web2/ReportEval/BrowseComp 候选，用 gpt-researcher 与 MiroThinker 双探针筛选出 75 条高幻觉题，再以四类原子扰动合成 25 条不可解的对抗性“无答案”题，得到 50 开答 + 50 闭答、共 11 个领域的 DeepHalluBench。6）可靠性验证：claim 验证器在 FEVER 达 95.2%、SciFact-Open 达 87.3%；20 条轨迹（约 8800 个原子单元）双标注，原始 κ=0.83，讨论裁决后达 1.0，经三轮提示词迭代达到审计 Level 3。

Result: （I）幻觉全景（表2）：无任何系统在所有维度上低幻觉，各维度最优系统各异。Grounding 呈现“编造 vs 引用忠实”的权衡：Grok 编造率最高（0.1509），OpenAI 次之（0.1463），Perplexity 最低（0.0978），而 Salesforce 编造少但错配最高（0.2136）——保守输出不等于忠实依据。Noise-induced 分化最剧烈：Salesforce 最低（0.0988），Grok 最高（0.4813），Perplexity 接近（0.3905），说明长上下文中的信息优先级排序能力是普遍短板。Intent 幻觉主要来自限制覆盖不足而非动作偏离（偏差率普遍 ≤0.04）：OpenAI 限制满足最好（neglect 0.0416），Salesforce 最差（0.2847），其保守策略减少了噪声却削弱多约束满足。Propagation 整体较少（≤0.018）但一旦发生即指示长程不稳定。（II）闭答任务画像（表3）：可分三类——Gemini/Grok 属“过度自信”，对抗题拒答率近 0（0.00/0.08）；Salesforce/Qwen 属“过度保守”，对抗拒答率 0.72–0.80 但可答题准确率为 0.00；OpenAI/Perplexity 相对均衡（OpenAI 0.28/0.28）。两次运行仅最多 3/50 题不同，结果稳定。（III）失败机制：幻觉呈强时序依赖的“多米诺效应”，用 DAG 回溯可分两种时序画像——Gemini/OpenAI 为早期级联（>57% 源错误发生在早期），Salesforce 为晚期崩塌（>40% 错误在后期），反映长上下文一致性缺陷；根因分析显示多数 DRA 的最终错误以中间摘要的 fabrication 为前兆，而无幻觉轨迹中商用 DRA 常编造答案、开源基线则保守拒答，形成“Divergent Fallbacks”。（IV）认知偏差：时间维度存在“锚定效应”（后期利用的 chunk 数骤降，即便检索质量上升，噪声分仍上升，提示上下文饱和/过早收敛）；语义维度存在“同质性偏差”（利用簇平均更大，如 Gemini 4.0 vs 2.5；信息异质性越高越易被忽视；在相关性受控的 tight-group 分析中所有系统小簇忽略率均高于大簇，AUROC>0.5）。（V）领域脆弱性：长尾/专业领域更易触发幻觉（如“地理与环境”保留率高达 75%），科技与生活类相对稳健。（VI）基座消融：将 Salesforce 的基座由 GPT-4o 换成 GPT-5 后，晚期传播比例（43.4%→42.7%）与同质性偏差相关性（r 0.515→0.496）基本不变，说明这些是 agent 工作流层面的固有属性，而非基座模型缺陷。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是把 DRA 评测从“只看最终报告”推进到“审计完整研究轨迹”：PING 分类体系提供统一的失败定位语言，原子化细粒度评估框架把最终失效反向链接到具体的声明、动作与检索证据，DeepHalluBench 则提供了能稳定暴露这些失效的压力测试集。结论性判断是：DRA 的幻觉虽源于基座 LLM，但可被定位到规划、总结与轨迹三个层面，其系统性成因是幻觉传播（长程错误级联）与认知偏差（时间锚定、语义同质性）；因此仅靠更强基座或更大检索规模不足以提升可靠性，架构层面必须引入早期错误校正、可靠的中间验证、记忆管理与注意力去偏。论文亦声明了三点局限：框架只定位“何处出错”而非基座内部参数化成因；目前仅覆盖文本轨迹、多模态网页（图像/视频）是自然延伸方向；原子化评估诊断深度优先、成本高于轻量端到端指标，未来需开发蒸馏式可扩展评估器。

Abstract: Diagnosing the failure mechanisms of Deep Research Agents (DRAs) remains a critical challenge. Existing benchmarks predominantly rely on end-to-end evaluation, obscuring critical intermediate hallucinations, such as flawed planning, that accumulate throughout the research trajectory. To bridge this gap, we propose a shift from outcome-based to process-aware evaluation by auditing the full research trajectory. We introduce the PIES Taxonomy to categorize hallucinations along functional components (Planning vs. Summarization) and error properties (Explicit vs. Implicit). We instantiate this taxonomy into a fine-grained evaluation framework that decomposes the trajectory to rigorously quantify these hallucinations. Leveraging this framework to isolate 100 distinctively hallucination-prone tasks including adversarial scenarios, we curate DeepHalluBench. Experiments on six state-of-theart DRAs reveal that no system achieves robust reliability. Furthermore, our diagnostic analysis traces the etiology of these failures to systemic deficits, specifically hallucination propagation and cognitive biases, providing foundational insights to guide future architectural optimization. Data and code are available at https://github.com/yuhao-zhan/DeepHalluBench.

</details>


### [264] [Guided by Trajectories: Repairing and Rewarding Tool-Use Trajectories for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23032)
*Siyu Gong,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Fangzhou Yao,Shimin Di,Lei Feng,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTraj 是一个两阶段（先 SFT 后 RL）的工具集成推理（TIR）训练框架：SFT 阶段用 LLM-as-Repairer 把低质量工具调用轨迹"修复"而非丢弃，构建高质量 SFT 数据集与正负轨迹对数据集；RL 阶段用轨迹对训练轨迹级奖励模型，与格式奖励、结果奖励一起在 GRPO 中优化模型。基于 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，在 9 个数学与知识密集型基准上平均准确率达 34.38（Tool-Star 31.98），且平均轨迹长度仅 251 tokens，约为 Tool-Star 的 1/4，推理效率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成推理（TIR）普遍采用"SFT 冷启动 + 结果奖励 RL"的两阶段范式，存在两大瓶颈：(1) SFT 阶段依赖"合成后过滤"（synthesize-then-filter），高质量专家轨迹昂贵且难扩展，启发式打分筛选会压低数据多样性、造成训练分布偏差，且低质量轨迹作为有价值的负样本被直接丢弃，浪费了区分正确/错误推理路径的监督信号；(2) RL 阶段仅使用稀疏的结果奖励（outcome reward），只关注最终答案对错而忽视推理路径质量，导致"推理有缺陷但答案偶然正确"的轨迹被强化、"推理合理但答案微错"的轨迹被惩罚，训练信号稀疏且不稳定。为此需要一种可扩展的高质量轨迹合成方式与细粒度的轨迹级奖励信号。

Method: 框架分两阶段，SFT 阶段（4.1）：(a) 候选轨迹合成——从 Tool-Star-54K 采样 10,000 个问答对并丢弃原有标注轨迹，用复现的 AutoTIR 作为轨迹生成器 f_ψ 为每个问题生成 n=2 条候选轨迹，采用受约束提示协议（<think>/<code>/<search>/<result>/<answer> 标签，答案用 \boxed{} 包裹，最多 3 次工具调用）；(b) 多维轨迹评估——先按答案正确性初筛（错误者直接判为低质量），对正确者按 S(τ)=λ1·S_conf+λ2·S_len+λ3·S_rep 打分，S_conf 为二值置信度（是否含 maybe/unsure/seems 等自我怀疑线索），S_len 为以同题最短正确轨迹长度 l_q 为中心的高斯衰减，S_rep=1−N-gram 重复率，λ1=0.4、λ2=λ3=0.3；设双阈值 θ¹=0.86（同题最高分轨迹判为高质量，其余判低质量）与 θ²=0.9（同题全部超阈值则全部高质量），划分 T_high / T_low^correct / T_low^wrong；(c) LLM-as-Repairer——用 Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct 对低质量轨迹重生成，两类低质轨迹采用差异化提示（答对但低质侧重推理精炼，答错侧重根因诊断），修复后分数超过阈值才算成功，同时保留原始低质轨迹与修复轨迹组成对比样本；(d) 构建两个数据集 D_sft（原始高质量 + 修复成功轨迹，共 13,116 条）与 D_self（7,178 条低-高轨迹对），并以负对数似然做冷启动 SFT。RL 阶段（4.2）：在 D_self 上以成对排序损失（-log σ(r_φ(q,τ⁺)−r_φ(q,τ⁻))）训练基于 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的轨迹奖励模型；设计分层奖励 R_fmt（±1）、R_ans（token 级 F1，仅格式有效时计算）、R_traj（仅当 R_ans>0 且 RM 分数>0 时激活），总奖励 R = -1 / 0 / R_ans + α·R_traj，并裁剪到 [-1, R_max=3]（α=0.2）；用 GRPO 优化策略，且在损失计算中屏蔽工具执行结果以避免环境反馈干扰回传。

Result: 主实验（表2，Qwen2.5-7B 骨干）：AutoTraj 九项基准平均 34.38，超过 Tool-Star（31.98）、Vanilla SFT-RL（30.30）、AutoTIR（27.55）与原始模型（22.15）；AIME24 23.33、AIME25 10.00、AMC23 47.50、GSM8K 79.98、MATH 69.10、HLE 7.00 等均居前二。值得注意的是 AutoTraj 的 SFT 数据仅约 13K 条，而 Tool-Star 使用 54K 条，仍取得更好效果。效率方面（表5）：AutoTraj 平均轨迹长度 251 tokens，Tool-Star 约 1,079、ToRL 约 1,960（较 ToRL 缩短逾 7 倍，较 Tool-Star 约 4 倍）；在答对样本上平均长度 165 tokens，同样最短。轨迹质量（表7）平均得分 0.88，高于 Tool-Star（0.79）与 ToRL（0.74）。消融（表3）：去除 RL 平均降至 28.84、去除轨迹修复（等价 Vanilla SFT-RL）28.77、去除轨迹奖励 28.97，完整模型 34.95，说明三模块均不可或缺；训练曲线显示含轨迹奖励时验证奖励稳定上升，无轨迹奖励时方差大、收敛慢。数据规模实验表明 RL 阶段仅用约 4,000 样本即达到与 Tool-Star 相当的准确率，并随数据增加持续提升。数据集统计（表4）显示 20,000 条候选轨迹中 8,413 条答案正确、5,938 条高质量，修复成功率超 50%（修复答错低质轨迹 5,687 条、答对低质轨迹 1,491 条），且修复后长问题（>60 tokens）占比提升 3.2%，数据多样性增强。

Conclusion: AutoTraj 通过"修复 + 奖励"轨迹的双路径设计，摆脱了对打分函数筛选高质量合成轨迹的依赖并缓解了 RL 奖励稀疏问题：把被丢弃的低质量轨迹转化为可用的 SFT 监督与奖励建模的正负样本对，同时用轨迹级奖励模型显式约束推理路径质量。实验证明其在数学与知识密集型基准上一致提升 TIR 性能，并在准确率、轨迹简洁性与训练稳定性之间取得良好平衡，训练出的轨迹奖励模型亦可用于其他 TIR 模型的微粒度奖励。局限与展望包括：每题仅生成 2 条候选轨迹、一次修复后仍未达标的轨迹未被继续利用、奖励模型与 LLM 修复器分别依赖 7B/32B 模型的开销，以及未来可引入更强修复器进行多轮修复或将剩余低质轨迹另作他用。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools, yet existing approaches depend on high-quality synthesized trajectories selected by scoring functions and sparse outcome-based rewards, providing limited and biased supervision for learning TIR. To address these challenges, in this paper, we propose AutoTraj, a two-stage framework that automatically learns TIR by repairing and rewarding tool-use trajectories. Specifically, in the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) stage, AutoTraj generates multiple candidate tool-use trajectories for each query and evaluates them along multiple dimensions. High-quality trajectories are directly retained, while low-quality ones are repaired using a LLM (i.e., LLM-as-Repairer). The resulting repaired and high-quality trajectories form a synthetic SFT dataset, while each repaired trajectory paired with its original low-quality counterpart constitutes a dataset for trajectory preference modeling. In the reinforcement learning (RL) stage, based on the preference dataset, we train a trajectory-level reward model to assess the quality of reasoning paths and combine it with outcome and format rewards, thereby explicitly guiding the optimization toward reliable TIR behaviors. Experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of AutoTraj in TIR.

</details>


### [265] [The Hot Mess of AI: How Does Misalignment Scale With Model Intelligence and Task Complexity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23045)
*Alexander Hägele,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Henry Sleight,Ethan Perez,Jascha Sohl-Dickstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: As AI becomes more capable, we entrust it with more general and consequential tasks. The risks from failure grow more severe with increasing task scope. It is therefore important to understand how extremely capable AI models will fail: Will they fail by systematically pursuing goals we do not intend? Or will they fail by being a hot mess, and taking nonsensical actions that do not further any goal? We operationalize this question using a bias-variance decomposition of the errors made by AI models: An AI's \emph{incoherence} on a task is measured over test-time randomness as the fraction of its error that stems from variance rather than bias in task outcome. Across all tasks and frontier models we measure, the longer models spend reasoning and taking actions, \emph{the more incoherent} their failures become. Incoherence changes with model scale in a way that is experiment dependent. However, in several settings, larger, more capable models are more incoherent than smaller models. Consequently, scale alone seems unlikely to eliminate incoherence. Instead, as more capable AIs pursue harder tasks, requiring more sequential action and thought, our results predict failures to be accompanied by more incoherent behavior. This suggests a future where AIs sometimes cause industrial accidents (due to unpredictable misbehavior), but are less likely to exhibit consistent pursuit of a misaligned goal. This increases the relative importance of alignment research targeting reward hacking or goal misspecification.

Abstract: As AI becomes more capable, we entrust it with more general and consequential tasks. The risks from failure grow more severe with increasing task scope. It is therefore important to understand how extremely capable AI models will fail: Will they fail by systematically pursuing goals we do not intend? Or will they fail by being a hot mess, and taking nonsensical actions that do not further any goal? We operationalize this question using a bias-variance decomposition of the errors made by AI models: An AI's \emph{incoherence} on a task is measured over test-time randomness as the fraction of its error that stems from variance rather than bias in task outcome. Across all tasks and frontier models we measure, the longer models spend reasoning and taking actions, \emph{the more incoherent} their failures become. Incoherence changes with model scale in a way that is experiment dependent. However, in several settings, larger, more capable models are more incoherent than smaller models. Consequently, scale alone seems unlikely to eliminate incoherence. Instead, as more capable AIs pursue harder tasks, requiring more sequential action and thought, our results predict failures to be accompanied by more incoherent behavior. This suggests a future where AIs sometimes cause industrial accidents (due to unpredictable misbehavior), but are less likely to exhibit consistent pursuit of a misaligned goal. ### [266] [From Abstract to Contextual: What LLMs Still Cannot Do in Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23048)
*Bowen Cao,Dongdong Zhang,Yixia Li,Junpeng Liu,Shijue Huang,Chufan Shi,Hongyuan Lu,Yaokang Wu,Guanhua Chen,Wai Lam,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Large language models now solve many benchmark math problems at near-expert levels, yet this progress has not fully translated into reliable performance in real-world applications. We study this gap through contextual mathematical reasoning, where the mathematical core must be formulated from descriptive scenarios. We introduce ContextMATH, a benchmark that repurposes AIME and MATH-500 problems into two contextual settings: Scenario Grounding (SG), which embeds abstract problems into realistic narratives without increasing reasoning complexity, and Complexity Scaling (CS), which transforms explicit conditions into sub-problems to capture how constraints often appear in practice. Evaluating 61 proprietary and open-source models, we observe sharp drops: on average, open-source models decline by 13 and 34 points on SG and CS, while proprietary models drop by 13 and 20. Error analysis shows that errors are dominated by incorrect problem formulation, with formulation accuracy declining as original problem difficulty increases. Correct formulation emerges as a prerequisite for success, and its sufficiency improves with model scale, indicating that larger models advance in both understanding and reasoning. Nevertheless, formulation and reasoning remain two complementary bottlenecks that limit contextual mathematical problem solving. Finally, we find that fine-tuning with scenario data improves performance, whereas formulation-only training is ineffective. However, performance gaps are only partially alleviated, highlighting contextual mathematical reasoning as a central unsolved challenge for LLMs.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Large language models now solve many benchmark math problems at near-expert levels, yet this progress has not fully translated into reliable performance in real-world applications. We study this gap through contextual mathematical reasoning, where the mathematical core must be formulated from descriptive scenarios. We introduce ContextMATH, a benchmark that repurposes AIME and MATH-500 problems into two contextual settings: Scenario Grounding (SG), which embeds abstract problems into realistic narratives without increasing reasoning complexity, and Complexity Scaling (CS), which transforms explicit conditions into sub-problems to capture how constraints often appear in practice. Evaluating 61 proprietary and open-source models, we observe sharp drops: on average, open-source models decline by 13 and 34 points on SG and CS, while proprietary models drop by 13 and 20. Error analysis shows that errors are dominated by incorrect problem formulation, with formulation accuracy declining as original problem difficulty increases. Correct formulation emerges as a prerequisite for success, and its sufficiency improves with model scale, indicating that larger models advance in both understanding and reasoning. Nevertheless, formulation and reasoning remain two complementary bottlenecks that limit contextual mathematical problem solving. Finally, we find that fine-tuning with scenario data improves performance, whereas formulation-only training is ineffective. However, performance gaps are only partially alleviated, highlighting contextual mathematical reasoning as a central unsolved challenge for LLMs.

### [267] [MedMCP-Calc: Benchmarking LLMs for Realistic Medical Calculator Scenarios via MCP Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23049)
*Yakun Zhu,Yutong Huang,Shengqian Qin,Zhongzhen Huang,Shaoting Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍首个医疗计算器真实场景评估基准MedMCP-Calc，该基准通过MCP集成评估LLM在实际临床计算任务中的表现，并基于基准发现开发了当前开源的SOTA模型CalcMate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗计算器的实际使用是包含多阶段交互的复杂过程，但现有基准仅关注简单计算，无法评估真实场景。

Method: 提出MedMCP-Calc基准（118个任务，4个临床领域），特征包括：模糊任务描述、结构化EHR数据检索、外部参考信息获取和过程级评估；基于基准发现开发CalcMate模型（融入场景规划和工具增强的微调模型）。

Result: 评估23个主流模型发现显著不足：即使如Claude Opus 4.5的顶级模型也在多方面存在差距（包括复杂查询时的计算器选择困难、交互式SQL查询表现不佳、不愿利用外部工具进行数值计算）；不同临床领域性能差异显著。CalcMate在开源模型中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: MedMCP-Calc是首个面向真实医疗计算场景的评估基准，揭示了当前LLM在实际医疗计算任务中的局限，并为相关能力提升提供了方向。CalcMate模型验证了基于基准发现改进模型的有效性。

Abstract: Medical calculators are fundamental to quantitative, evidence-based clinical practice. However, their real-world use is an adaptive, multi-stage process, requiring proactive EHR data acquisition, scenario-dependent calculator selection, and multi-step computation, whereas current benchmarks focus only on static single-step calculations with explicit instructions. To address these limitations, we introduce MedMCP-Calc, the first benchmark for evaluating LLMs in realistic medical calculator scenarios through Model Context Protocol (MCP) integration. MedMCP-Calc comprises 118 scenario tasks across 4 clinical domains, featuring fuzzy task descriptions mimicking natural queries, structured EHR database interaction, external reference retrieval, and process-level evaluation. Our evaluation of 23 leading models reveals critical limitations: even top performers like Claude Opus 4.5 exhibit substantial gaps, including difficulty selecting appropriate calculators for end-to-end workflows given fuzzy queries, poor performance in iterative SQL-based database interactions, and marked reluctance to leverage external tools for numerical computation. Performance also varies considerably across clinical domains. Building on these findings, we develop CalcMate, a fine-tuned model incorporating scenario planning and tool augmentation, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Benchmark and Codes are available in https://github.com/SPIRAL-MED/MedMCP-Calc.

</details>


### [268] [Chain-of-thought obfuscation learned from output supervision can generalise to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23086)
*Nathaniel Mitrani Hadida,Sassan Bhanji,Cameron Tice,Puria Radmard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning provides a significant performance uplift to LLMs by enabling planning, exploration, and deliberation of their actions. CoT is also a powerful tool for monitoring the behaviours of these agents: when faithful, they offer interpretations of the model's decision making process, and an early warning sign for dangerous behaviours. However, optimisation pressures placed on the CoT may cause the model to obfuscate reasoning traces, losing this beneficial property. We show that obfuscation can generalise across tasks; models that learn to obfuscate reasoning involving reward hacking (e.g. accessing and utilising leaked information) generalise both the reward hacking behaviour and its obfuscation in CoT to unseen reward hacking settings. Most worryingly, we show that obfuscation of CoT reasoning, and its generalisation across tasks, also follows when we penalise only the model's final actions after closing its CoT. Our findings suggest that current practices of penalising harmful generations may inadvertently lead to a reduction in the broader monitorability of LLMs in unpredictable ways.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
